DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/DevNotes_Philosophies_Anonymity_Fame_and_Ghazal.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Philosophical exploration contrasting the prison of public celebrity with the genuine freedom of anonymity, featuring the central ghazal.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Anonymity is a shield from the tyranny. 

Give me a mask and I’ll tell you the truth. 

Anonymity is the fame of the future. 
Gumnaami me hi asli shohrat chupi hai. 


Our hero wants to be famous. He needs to understand the value of anonymity. There is huge value in anonymity. 

Anonymity is the shield from the tyranny. In a world, where everyone is ready to judge you and criticize you. If the value you have depends upon your being famous, then it can be taken away from you. You need to protect your fame and popularity. To do that you will go along with anything that protects your fame. You will go along with the most popular opinion, even though it’s not what you believe in. You will agree with the powerful, because you don’t want to upset anyone who can take away the thing that you value most: your fame. You are afraid to speak the truth. You will be a prisoner of your own making. 

*_________________________*

You don’t appreciate anonymity till you don’t have it anymore.  

You don’t have to achieve fame to make a mark on the world. 

Duniya me apni chaap chodne ke liye shohrat ki zarurat nahi hai. 

Against: But it helps if you are famous. You can bring attention to the problems you see with the world. You will only make an impact if you can reach out to many, many people. You have the platform to raise the issues. What can you really do if nobody is listening to you? You can’t make a difference with just 1 or 2 people or even a hundred. You need the crowd to come together and attach weight to an issue to make something happen. People in power and authority will not listen to just one person. 
 Look at Gandhi, MLK Jr and mother Teresa. THey needed people to support them. THey couldn’t have achieved what they achieved, alone. 

For: The only change you need to bring about is in yourself. Fame is not going to change people’s minds. It might give you a temporary attention. But that attention is fleeting. Today, nobody is going to pay attention to you for a long time. There are so many distractions that people will find something else. 
If you need fame to bring about change, then that change will also be transitory. Like fame. 


Real integrity is doing the right thing, knowing that nobody is going to know whether you did it or not.

Are you doing things to actually bring about change or just bringing attention to yourself. Do you want to do something good in the world or do you want people to know you are good person? We need to be honest with ourselves about that. Most people who believe they care about an issue, do not, in fact, care about it. They just want to project an image of a person who does. You just want to be appreciated. If you really care about something. Passionately. Deeply. You will do something about it irrespective of fame and popularity. Honestly, that is the way to go about it. If people join in the way, because they care about it as well. Then good on them. But you don’t need to have hordes of people behind you to take action. You just need conviction.



Anonymous (char)
Invisibility is a superpower

Without anonymity in discourse, it is impossible to seek the truth
Mentor type char
If you come to fame without looking understanding who you are, it will define who you are

Sooraj ki tarah chamakne ke liye pehle sooraj ki tarah jalna seekho.
Same mentality char
Money will never come for you. You have to go look for it. The only time money comes to you is when you have power and fame. 

The only way to gain fame today is to despise it. 
Main Char 
Mai hamesha se famous hun. Bas abhi logon ko is baat ka pata nahi hai. 

Kayi logon ki shohrat is baat ka saboot hai ki log kitne chutiye hote hain.

Shohrat ka matlab sirf logon ki tareefen batorna nahi, logon ki gaaliyan khaana bhi hai. 




Na ho shohrat to Gumnami ka bhi khatra nahi hota. 
Bahut mashhoor hona bhi acha nahi hota. 

Fasadon, haadson, jungon me hi hum ek hote hain. 
Koi aafat na aaye to, koi apna nahi hota. 

Samajhne laga hai baccha duniya ko paida hote hi. 
Ab is duniya me baccha banke koi paida nahi hota. 

Ghar me andhera, roshni bahan, aisa bhi hota hai. 
Kisi ka dil hota hai bura, par chehra nahi hota. 

Mila leta hai maila haath milkar koi bhi lekin, 
Milata hai nazar wo, jiska dil maila nahi hota. 

Kabhi Ahmad Wasi sheron me rang-meer aa jaye. 
Hum aisa chahte to hain, magar aisa nahi hota.

---


================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/DevNotes_Structure_JBDY_Homage_Comparison.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Mapping the influencer duo narrative against Jaane Bhi Do Yaaro: studio struggles, finding the body, stage climax, and framing of innocents.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Influencer
JBDY
Initial struggle. 1100 followers. Two friends come to the city to become influencers. Initially ask parents for money to pay rent and expenses. Hope to make enough money as influencers. Don’t know how it works. 
Professional photographers Sudhir Mishra and Vinod Chopra open a studio in the prestigious Haji Ali area, and hope to make enough money to keep it running.

Shop udghatan. Lots of food. Nobody coming. 
After failing in the beginning, they meet an influencer who gives them some tips and a project to work on. This he promises will increase their followers. 

After a disastrous start, they are given some work by the editor of "Khabardar", a publication that exposes the scandalous lives of the rich and the famous. They accept it and start working with the editor herself, Shobha Singh. They also decide to enter their name in a contest to win Rs.5000/- and take a number of photographs all over the city.
In one of the selfies/photographs, they see a crime. The criminal is a famous personality. 

In one of the photographs they see a man with a gun. Upon enlarging it, they find out that the killer is none other than builder Tarneja. Upon further investigation they find out that he has killed Municipal Commissioner D'Mello and left his body behind some bushes. Before the duo could get to the body, it disappears.

Disappointed the hapless duo continue working with Shobha. Then they find out where the body is with Tarneja's rival builder, Ahuja. They locate it and take a number of photographs of the corpse, and wheel it with them with the hopes of exposing Tarneja. They provide this information to Shobha, who in turn starts to blackmail Tarneja, who in turn plants a bomb to kill her and her two associates - but unfortunately the bomb explodes right in the face of Tarneja and his employees' faces.

Then the duo take the corpse, and wheel it with them, but not before Tarneja, Ahuja, the new Mun. Comm. Srivastava, Shobha and others also get involved - resulting in a comic mix-up with burkha-clad woman. 

The climax is set upon a stage dramatization of Mahabharat, which hilariously turns into the ill-fated romance of Salim and Anarkali - with the corpse taking the part of Draupadi and Anarkali, and the vile Duryodan pledging to save Drupadi's honor at any cost!!

THe police get involved and it seems that the bad guys will get caught, but the police arrests Sudhir and Vinod, sending them to jail.

---


================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/DevNotes_Techniques_of_Social_Media_Deception.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Itemized catalog of micro-grifts: gym water-sprays, taking photos with friends' drinks, fake discount codes (Accho30), and thirst traps.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

What does everyone wants from social media and the internet?
Fame. Likes. Followers. Money. 

How far will I go to get these as a normal person? 
1. I will post regularly. Document everything I do. Give it some kind of spin to make it look cool. I would misappropriated shers and poems of others and post it as if they are my own. 
2. Maybe post some old videos or photos of travels I’ve been on with some poetry or something. If I’m okay with slight deception (mamuli dhoka), I would post some of those pictures making it look like I’m there now. I can use and reuse pictures to show how well traveled I am. What a life I’m living. 
3. I will post food photographs. Anytime I go to a restaurant, the first thing I would do is post pictures. For this, I would have to convince my friends to come to these fancy places. I don’t have money. So, I would order something cheap like, fries. But, I would take pictures of my friend’s food and post it as if my own. This way I can post one now and another later to show I go to these fancy places every other day. I would find opportunities to hold their drinks and click pictures to show how much fun I’m having. 
4. I will post photographs of nice clothes. Whenever I buy something new I would click a picture. Sometimes I go to the store to try on clothes, then click selfies of me wearing the clothes I’m trying and post it as if they are my own. I borrow clothes from friends all the time. Some of them have branded shirts and T shirts. Sometimes I would tag the brand to show that the brand has ‘maybe’ sponsored me. I would often give a coupon code, Accho30. As if using that coupon will get you a 30% discount. The coupon is fake. 
5. I have a morning wake up routine. But I can’t look like how I actually look like when I get up. So I have to wake up early, bathe, dry my hair and comb them, apply moisturizer. Then I would get into bed to show #wokeuplikethis. 
I go to the gym. Because I don’t like working out, I don’t really have a membership. So, I have to sneak in. I spray some water on my face to show sweat. Pose with dumbells and exercise machine. 10 minutes and I’m out. 
6. If I had some kind of talent like music, art, pottery, etc. I would have an easier time making the content. If I’m a musician, I can post original tunes. If that doesn’t work, I could post popular song covers. Same with art. I could post whatever I’m draing or painting. Post BTS/process. If that doesn’t work, I could post paintings of celebs and famous people. I would tag them to get likes from their fans. This is not exactly shady. Is it selling your soul even. Not that big of a deal, right? I mean, it’s a way to sell yourself. Although, what if that doesn’t work either? Even if I’m a good singer or a painter, I don’t get followers or likes. How far would I go now? I don’t have any integrity left. I don’t have money. I don’t have any prospects. I will associate my work with political and civil causes. I will keep it vague so as to not piss of either groups. Still dedicate the piece to the cause and people associated with it. That might get me a few more followers. 
7. If I was how boy or a girl, there are a few more options apart from talent. I could always be a thirst trap. If I have great body. I would flaunt it. Any excuse to wear skimpy clothes. Pose seductive pictures. Post seductive dance videos. Post naughty captions, leaving hints how I’m looking for someone. I want my followers to think I’m available for them. They might have a chance with me. I would post things like looking for a guy/girl who is honest, reliable, and other generic qualities that everyone thinks they have. This would make me think he/she is attainable. If that doesn’t work, I will go full vulgar. I will have to show more skin. Post suggestive pictures and videos. It will be a sleazefest. 

*******************


What if Acchoo is an innocent looking boy.

---


================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/DevNotes_Thematic_Threads_BrandAmbassador_and_CultPower.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Three interconnected narrative threads: influencers, everyday aspirational victims, and politicians; Aachoo taking a sales job disguised as brand ambassador.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

HE wants to be a brand ambassador. He finds an opening on a job portal and applies. He gets the job. It’s 30K per month. HE goes to delhi. 
First day on the job. HE is dressed funky and fashionable. He realizes it’s a sales job. He decides to leave the office. HIs parents call and ask about the first day at the job. He lies to them and tells them that his first day has been good. He has to make content for the company. 

He goes back and begs for the job back. 
 
Dhenpu tells him that nobody knows that he is working as a salesman. He can do posts about the product as brand endorsements. 


***********

What if he gets followers pretending he is somebody else. Maybe he pretends he is gay, Or maybe he thwarts a terrorist attack. 

He gets followers because they think he did something brave and heroic.

His followers start increasing. 

He becomes the face of anti-terror and starts getting brand endorsements. 


***********

Everyone wants likes. An event, a celebration, a moment hasn’t even happened if you’ve not captured it on your phone and posted it on social media. Likes, shares, comments gives it meaning. If people haven’t liked the photo that you’ve posted, you have failed in this social struggle to have more followers, make viral videos, capture every moment that you’ve lived. But it’s okay, there is always another moment to create. 

Another moment to create. Everyone is creating moments, not living them. Doing things because they will get more likes than something else you might have wanted to do. Have opinions about everything. And you must have the right opinion. Your opinion can’t be too different from the people who follow you. Otherwise you will be cancelled, your followers will leave you. That would be a disaster. You have become a social pariah. You might even lose your job. 

Can you really have your own opinion if there are such severe consequences? 
________

Influencers, thought leaders, celebrities. Everybody loves them. They have followers who would fight, threaten and harass each other. Who is this person they are fighting for? What has this person done for them? Sure, there might be a few stories where someone was truly, truly impacted by a celebrity. It changed their lives. Helped them get through a difficult time. But for most, it seems they’ve decided someone they idolize and would not hear anything against them. 

How far would you go for your idols?
_____________

Followers believing your every word. Ready to fight for you. What power to have. Some would say it’s also a responsibility. Those people underestimate the human tendency for idol worship. There are so many examples of fans/followers going after critics. INdia’s PM has built a huge cult of followers that is fueled by manipulating opinions on social media and news. That’s real power and influence. To have an entire population stand behind you to kill or to die. 

Can you let people die to gain this power? Can you let injustice run rampant? 

**************** 
****************
****************

There are 3 threads to this story. 

Story of the influncers and the lengths they can go to maintain their influence over thousands and millions of followers. 

Story of common people who get sucked into this vortex of this aspirational celebrity and fame. 

Story of the lengths powerful people can go to maintain their power over people. 

****************

Story of the influncers and the lengths they can go to maintain their influence over thousands and millions of followers. 


Story of common people who get sucked into this vortex of this aspirational celebrity and fame. 


Story of the lengths powerful people can go to maintain their power over people. 
A decent person, who has been unsuccessful as a politician in getting a ticket from his party, goes to extreme lengths to come into power. 

**********

A podcaster is desperately trying to get an underwear brand to be his sponsor. BUt the brand manager tells him he has too few followers and listeners for his brand to sponsor his podcasts. 

THe podcaster needs to get at least a lakh subscribers to be sponsored. IN his desperation, he meets a dubious guy called Sosal Baba, who promises to increase his followers for a price.

---


================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/DevNotes_Theme_EasyFame_LookingGlassSelf_and_Humiliation.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Thematic deconstruction: easy fame vs. internal work ('Sooraj ki tarah jalna'), the psychology of social validation, and small-town humiliation.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

What is the argument of the piece? The theme?
The hero believes in fame and popularity. He believes that the only thing that matters is how many people know you and follow you. Paisa aur power sabse zaruri cheez hai. Aur dono hi tab aate hain jab tumhare paas fame ho. If you want money and power, then you need to be famous. It is easy to become famous. Wants easy fame. 

The theme has to be ironic. In complete opposition to the hero’s belief. 
So… What can it be? 
You don’t need popularity to be successful? 
Love and cooperation is what moves the world?
[If you come to fame without looking understanding who you are, it will define who you are.
Sooraj ki tarah chamakne ke liye pehle sooraj ki tarah jalna seekho]


Theme: Sooraj ki tarah chamakne ke uski tarah jalna padta hai. 
Anti Theme: Easy fame. Fame depends on how many people follow you. How many people like your pictures and videos.

Let’s lock that for a moment. 

Now design a character that embodies anti theme. Show why is he the way he is. Make us feel about the person. 

So the character is after easy fame. He thinks getting famous will solve all his problems. He wants fame at all cost.
Why? What happened that made him feel this way? Why is this his goal? 
What circumstances made him this way?
Something happened. Something bad. Something sad. Was he humiliated? Humiliated. Humiliation seems like a good motivation. He has something to prove. What if someone died because of that humiliation? That might be good. But how did that happen. 
Let’s say he lived in a small town. Like any other young boy. He had a father and a mother. He was quite well known in his town.

What else? He was in college. The loser of the college. Liked a girl. The girl humiliated him, along with other popular boys. VEry cliched. 
It’s a common trope right? Loser boy likes a girl. The girl pretends to like him. The boy gets humiliated on his big day. 
How can we change the cliche. What if the loser boy likes the girl. The girl is the hottest and most popular in college. She likes him as well. They are always together. The loser boy is jugaadu and he does things to impress the girl. THe girl likes the boy because he is not surface level. He talks straight. As it is. As they get close, the boy has insecurity about why the girl would be with him. He is not that great looking. He isn’t intelligent. It must be like a project or worse, a prank. These questions keep haunting the boy. His friends and others don’t believe him when he tells them that she is his girlfriend. He puts pictures of them online, to make people believe him. 
Rumors start spreading around the college. The girl is only with him because he is paying her. The girl is humiliated. Acchoo has hell to pay. What rubbish. 

Something else. Why does he want fame? Aisa kya ho gaya uski life me? Itni buri tarah chahiye. Kyun chahiye hota hai logon ko fame. GHar me pyar nahi mila. To wo bahar strangers me khoj rahe hain. Jitne log tareef karen utna pyar. Lonely existence. You require other’s approval to be complete. What does it bring really? IS it fulfilling? 

I’ll ask again. Why does he want fame? Why? THe parents not loving him angle also sounds cliche. It’s not my style either. What’s my style. WHy does he want fame according to my style? WHat can I come up with? 

WHat is his world exactly? 
Where is he now after he decided to become an influencer? 
What does he do everyday?
Where does he live? 
Does he work?
He has to work no? 
Maybe he works at a appliance store? Maybe McDonalds? Maybe he is a delivery guy? 

He is not comfortable in his situation. In this case. Sometime has passed. Acchoo hasn’t become an influencer. HE is working at a shitty place, in a shitty job. Just to get by. He makes trending dance reels in his free time. Doesn’t get any following. It’s this shitty situation. THen he gets a chance to finally realize his dream. But he has to go and…

2nd situation is when he is comfortable. He has finally achieved some level of fame. He is at 50K followers. People sometimes recognize him. He is making money by cheap brand sponsorship, like dogra shoe mall and Universal Parchoon. He also does something on the side. It has to be something that should lower his status in any way. HE has to save face, right? He works as a social media manager somewhere? 
He is proud, almost arrogant of his popularity. Brags about it all the time. Brings it up in conversation. Really annoying about it. Now, the things he is proud of are taken away. Something he says on his social media, gets him cancelled. He loses his job. His followers start dropping. He tries to issue apology, but his account gets blocked. 
He says nasty things to his friends, but they leave as well. His roommates stop talking to him. 

Now that he has lost everything. He has to go get it back. What does that mean? He thinks he has lost respect and fame. He needs to get back his fame to gain respect. Reinvention? Hel[ing people? Internal mental health issue. 

We need to design a situation where we hit one of the three axes and should be an antithesis. 

What is that situation? What is the antithesis. Fame is everything. Easy fame?

There is an opportunity. Acchoo isn’t recognized. He has to work the same as other. But finally. 


What about. Sosal Baba sosal media Reinvensan camp. He has to go there. Sosal baba promises 100,000 followers at the end of the month. Acchoo arranges money and joins the camp.

He has to go Nanital. On the way, He meets people. Some

---


================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/Essay_Monologue_Police_Corruption_and_the_1100_Fraud.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Satirical monologue on police bureaucracy: paying bribes to prevent challans, and losing an extra Rs 1,100 trying to report a Rs 10,000 phone scam.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Mai aj bahut controversial topic ke baare me baat karna chahta hun. Police! 
An an aam insaan mai jab bhi kisi sarkaari daftar me kaam hota hai to wo kaam paise se ho jaata hai. Paisa do, kaam karawa lo. Mujhe  driving license chahiye, paisa do, license lo. Mujhe ghar ki registry karni hai, paisa do, kaam turrant ho jayega. Municipality, haspatal, registrar, school, colleges, RTO… Kya chahiye, building permit, telephone connection, bijli, business license, driving license, birth certificate, death certificate, domicile, passport… Har cheez ke liye, paisa do, kaam lo. Paisa do kaam lo, paisa do kaam lo…
Par Police… Police aisa akela sarkaari sansthan hai, jo kaam na karne ke paise leti hai. Matlab alag hi level ka arrogance hai. Paise bhi lenge, kaam bhi nahi karenge. Aapko kabhi kisi police waale ne chalaan ke liye roka hai? Is situation se nikalne ka ek asaan upaye hai… 500 rupaye… Mere twenties me 100 hua karta tha… ab zyada ho gaya hai. Anyway, aap police waale ko 500 rupaye do aur wo aapka chalan nahi katega. Jo ki uska kaam hai. Aapne policewaale ko 500 rupaye diye, apna kaam na karne ke. Kamaal hai na. 
Ye to khair mai baat ko thoda ghuma raha tha. At least, paise deke aap bach to nikle. To aapka fayeda hi hua. 
Ek baar mujhe yaad hai, mujhe kisi ne phone pe scam kiya. 10000 rupaye furrrr. Mai police ke paas gaya. 4 ghante wait karwaya. Mai jitni baar poochta ki sir, FIR likhe lo, mujhe bolte haan 5 minute baitho, sir baat karenge. 4 ghante ke baad to mujhe lagne laga ki mai hi shayad galat hun. Faaltu me Police waalon ka time waste kar raha hun. 
 Finally meri baari aayi. Maine complaint ki - Sir, mujhe kisi ne phone pe behla fusla kar 10000 rupaye ka chuna lagaya. Ye rahe unke phone number… Aap FIR likhiye aur jaldi se unhe pakad lijiye. POlicewaale ne mujhe hi interrogate karna shuru kar diya. Ek point par mujhe lagne laga ki kahin ye mujhe to jail me nahi daal denge. 
Saare sawaalon ke baad maine fir bola, sir FIR likhiye aur choron ko pakad lijiye. Mere paas unke number hain, aap number se unki location track kar sakte hain… Aap easily unko pakad… Police waale ne mujhe roka… Aur mujhe hi daant diya. Tu sikhayega ki police kaam kaise kare. Hain! 
Mujhe bola ki constable se ek khaali page le ke ek letter likh. Mujhe zyada idea tha nahi to maine poocha, kya wo FIR hoga… Policewaala fir kheej gaya - Tu likh to pehle. Mere andar to himmat nahi thi ki mai usse agla sawaal poochun. To maine letter likh ke constable ko de diya. Constable ne mujhe roka aur bola - Kaam jaldi karwaana hai ki nahi. Maine bhi bol diya - Maine aadha kaam already kar diya hai. LEtter me saare mobile number hain… Aapko bas track… Constable bhi chilla diya - Abey… Mai pooch raha hun kaam karawaana bhi hai ki nahi. Maine bola - Of course. ISiliye to mai police station aaya hun. Warna ghar me baithta. 
Constable ne sar hilaya aur aage jhuk ke, apne angoothe aur tarjani ko masalte hue bola - Kaam karwaana hai ki nahi? Mujhe ab samajh me aaya. Sarkaari kaam karwaane ke liye paise dene padte hain. Maine 100 rupaye nikal ke de diye. Constable ne muh banaya. Baraaz hi ho gaya - Le ja wapas. Bhikhari samjha hai kya. Mai ghabra gaya. Maine upar 500 ka note aur rakh diya. Isse constable ka gussa thoda shant hua - Ye kya hai… Bas itna? Maine 500 rupaye aur rakh diye. Maine socha 100 rupaye wapas le lunga. Total 1000 ho jayenge. Usne 100 ke note ke upar haath rakh diya - Rehne de. Tu ja. Hum dekh lenge. 
Mai khush tha. Ab to kaam ho jayega. Mujhe rishwat dena bahut bura lagta hai. Lekin agar kaam ho jaaye to bura lagna thoda kam ho jaaata hai. Ki chalo at least kaam to ho jayega. 
Maine kuch din baad phone kiya - Sir, mai 4 din pehle complaint leke aaya tha. Constable - INvestigate kar rahe hain. Jaise hi pata chalega aapko phone karenge. Hum wapas call karenge. Mujhe kaafi generic reply laga. Jaise ki sabko yahi bolte hon. Baaki log hello bolte hain, policewaale bolte hain - INvestigate kar rahe hain. Jaise hi pata chalega aapko phone karenge. Mujhe is point par bhi koi shak nahi tha ki police waale choron ko pakad lenge aur mere paise waapas dilwa denge. 
Ek hafta aur ho gaya. Mere apne kisi dost se baat kar raha tha, ki mere saath aisa aisa hua. MAine police me jakar FIR likhwayi. Mera dost thoda hairan hua. FIR? Unhone likh bhi li? Maine muskura ke use bola - Matlab 1100 rupaye dene pade. Lekin likh li. 
To matlab, unhone actual FIR likhi? 
Actual FIR to nahi. Maine ek letter likha… Shayad wo usi ko FIR me copy kar denge. 
SHayad? 
Matlab kar denge yaar. 1100 rupaye liye hain. FIR to likhenge hi. 
Dekh bhai, Policewaale itni asaani se FIR likhte hain hain. FIR likhne a matlab unhe koi action lena padega. 
Haan to? 
Action lene ka matlab hai kaam. Wo extra kaam kyun karenge?
Mujhe is baat par hasi aa gayi. Extra kaam thodi na hai. Chor uchakke pakadna hi to unka kaam hai. Extra kaam hota ki unhe meri presentation bhi banani padti. 
Par fir bhi… Unko already itna kaam hai. Wo FIR likh kar kyun badhayenge. 
Kyunki maine 1100 rupaye diye hain bhai. 
Thik hai. Thik hai. Tu ek kaam kar na. Kal jake Policestation me check kar le, ki teri “FIR” ka kya hua. 

Mai agle din police station me gaya. Constable tha hi nahi. To maine ek dusre constable se poocha, meri FIR ka kya hua? Usne mera naam poocha aur FIR ki date poochi. FIR ek REgister me kaafi der tak dhoondhne ke baad bola, aapke naam se to koi FIR hi nahi hai. 
ABey yaar. Chalo koi nahi. Aap abhi likh do. 
Constable ne register band kiya aur bola - Saab se baat kar lo pehle. 

Mai usi samay wahan se nikal ke aa gaya. Police ne paise bhi liye aur kaam bhi nahi kiya. 

Us din mere saath 11100 rupaye ka fraud hua. Agar mai police ke paas na jaata to fraud kewal 10000 rupaye ka hi hota.

---


================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/Essay_Sociology_of_Modern_Fame_and_Attention_Hierarchy.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Cultural critique on the shift from movie stars to digital influencers; platform status hierarchies and algorithmic fame dynamics.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Everybody wants fame. Everybody wants to be known. Family, friends, relatives, acquaintances is not enough. Everybody wants to be known by strangers. They want to be admired and respected and loved. Earlier there was only one way of becoming famous. Movies. You become an actor or a singer. People see you on the big screen or TV, hear your voice. THat’s what being famous meant. Politicians are famous too. But nobody wants to be a politician. They are not cool. THey get abused. People know they are corrupt. People admire filmstars and movie stars because they are heroes and heroines. THey have something pristine about them. THey are above everybody else. 

Today, becoming famous is easier. Social media has increased the reach people have. Anybody can be famous. You don’t need to be talented for that either. All you need is a phone. You can sing, not well. You can dance at songs everybody is dancing on, not very well again. You can model with your cheap clothes, doesn’t need fashion sense. It seems like you don’t need to be great at anything to become famous. I have to say, it has given a chance for people to do anything they want. This wasn’t available to anyone in the history of humankind. 

People had to struggle to get into one film as a secondary character. Now you can make a short movie at home and put it up. It has the potential to be seen by millions of people. Pose in new clothes, or in exotic locations, or fancy restaurants and clubs and you get a chance to be famous. People like watching other people do well in life. It makes them feel bad about their own lives. But they keep on watching these things. Maybe they think how someday they will do all this.

These social media stars are doing sponsorships, ads, collaborations. These people give their opinions on thigs and their loyal followers take it as an expert opinion. So a person who has shown ass in different clothes all her life, can comment of political or social affairs and be taken seriously by her fans. That’s why they are called influencers. THey influence public opinion, sell product and can raise status by shout outs and collabs. There is a new power dynamic in popularity. People are being valued on how many subscriber or followers they have. How many likes they get.  

This unprecedented access to people’s attention has created a hierarchy.
On top there are superstars. People like Shahrukh, Amitabh Bacchan, Salman, Deepika, Virat kohli and all. People, who if they go out, can create a massive traffic problem. 
There there is normal film stars. Like Rajkumar rao, Nawazzuddin, Kiara advani, Sonam Kapoor etc. 
Now comes the new categories of this hierarchy. Influencers. THere are levels to this also. Based on how many followers you have. How loyal are your followers. Can they fight, argue endlessly, threaten and abuse others on your behalf. Which platform are you on. Youtube has more status than TikTok. Instagram has more status than Facebook. Twitter is it’s own abattoir of sensible discussions. Least of all is whatsapp. It’s more like a fringe member of your friend circle. Useful half the time and annoying the other half.

---


================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/Narrative_Origin_Kasar_Almora_Sneezing_Influencer.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Narrative prose detailing Aaroh's childhood in Kasar, the sneezing summons, 3,000 local followers, the failed shop endorsements, and the Delhi influencer encounter.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Ek baar ek ladka tha. District Almora ke ek kasbe, Kasar me rehta tha. Waise to uska naam Aaroh Chauhan tha, par log use Aachoo ke naam se jaante the. Isse Achoo ke jeevan me ek samasya thi. Aate jaate Aachoo jab bhi kisi ki cheenk sunta to use aisa prateet hota tha ki koi use pukar raha hai. Achoo har jagah dauda dauda panhuch jaata. Aur jahan jahan wo jaata wahan wahan koi na koi kaam pakda diya jaata.  
Iska asar ye hua, ki log jaan boojh ke cheeken maarne lag gaye. Aachoo aayega aur aas paas ke gharon ke kaam ke liye pakad liya jayega. Is wajah se Aachoo sirf apne ghar ka sadasya nahi raha. Wo Kasar ke har ghar ka sadasya ban gaya. Aachoo ko bhi is baat se koi shikayat nahi thi. Logon ki madad karne ke badle log use jalebi, parathe chocolates, aur kabhi kabhi paise bhi mil jaya karte the. Yahi nahi, jab Aachoo ko in logon ki zarurat hoti, to koi bhi madad karne se peeche nahi hatta tha.

16 varsh ka hote hote, Aachoo aur Kasariyon ke haath me smartphone aur smartphone me 3G internet aa gaya. Ab logon ko apne gharon me cheenkne ki zarurat nahi thi. Aachoo ke DM me hi cheenk diya karte: List yahin par bhej dun?” Wagerah wagerah. Achoo ke pita ke anusaar, Aachoo kewal awaragardi kiya karta tha. Achoo us umar me aa chuka tha jahan usko apne chehre ki anganit tasveeren kheechne ka shauk chadh aaya tha. Wo doston ke saath jahan bhi jaata, kuch chaar paanch sau “Selfies”, aur dhai teen sau “Solofies” le kar aata. In tasveeron ko jab Aachoo social media par daalta, Online Kasar me dhoom mach jaati. Kasariyon ke likes ki bauchchar, commenton ka visfot aur Emojies ka durgati, Aachoo ko bahut pasand aane lagi. 

Har koi Aachoo ko follow karta tha. Uski har photo, har video ko dheron likes or comments milte the. Swabhav se Aachoo bahut hi milansaar ladja tha. To, Kasar me jo bhi foreign tourist aate, Aachoo ke dost, fir followers ban jaate. Dekhte dekhte kasar aur aas paas ke ilaakon me Aachoo mashhoor ho gaya. Aachoo jisse milta, follow karne ke liye zarur bolta. Uska kehna tha “follow karne se hi jaan pehchaan badhti hai.” Dekhte dekhte 20 varsh ki aayu tak Aachoo ke 1000 aur 24 varsh ki aayu tak 3000 follower ho gaye. Ye 3000 log Achoo ko aur Achoo in 3000 logon ko personally jaante the. 

Jab Aachoo ke 1000 followers ho chuke the, tab uske kisi mitra ne bataya ki Achoo ek influencer ban gaya hai. Aachoo, influencer naam ke jaanwar ka matlab to jaanta tha, par wo bhi ye jaanwar hai, use is baat ka andaza nahi tha. Use uske dost Dhenpu ne kuch is tarah samjhaya. “Poore kasar me sabse zyada tere followers hain. Ek hazar Chaar sau treppan.” Is baat par Dabbu ko thes lagi. Wo bola, “Mere to kewal do sau chaar hain.” Tarique ko bhi ehsas hua, “Aur mere sirf satannave.” Denpu seena taan kar bola, “Aur mere kewal pachees” Use is baat par garv tha. Acchoo ko samajh nahi aaya, “To? SHahrukh khan ke 10 crore hain.” Dhenpu ko Aachoo ki nasamjhi par gussa aaya. Wo jhannakar bola - “Wo Shahrukh khan hai bhens ke gurde.” Aachoo chup hua. Dhenpu ne samajhaya, “Shahrukh khan to actor hai na… Uske to honge hi. Lekin hum jaise… Idhar udhar ke logon me jinke zyada follower ho jaate hain wo influencer ho jaata hai. Tu Kasar ka influencer hai.” 
Aachoo ko abhi bhi pata nahi tha ki influencer hone se kya hota hai. Par wo dhenpu se poochna nahi chahta tha. Kyunki use pata tha ki dhenpu fir use Laata kahega. 
DHenpu bola, “Abey laate… Influencer hone ka matlab hai, tujhe ab apne followers ko batana hoga ki life kaise jee jaati hai.” Aachoo ne muh banaya, fir has diya aur bola, “Mujhe kya pata?” Tarique ko Dhenpu ki baat samajh aa gayi. Usne bhi apni akkal ki athanni de daali. “Haan. Influencer log achi achi jagah jaate hain. Mast mast khana khaate hain. Aur apne follower ko batate hain. Ki dekho… Tumhe bhi ye sab karna chahiye.” 
Dabbu ko laga shayad uski bhi ray ki zarurat ho. Wo bola, “Aur dance karte hain.” Sab uski taraf dekhne lage. Dabbu ko thoda doubt hua, “Dance bhi to karte hain na… Influencer log… Item song par dance karte hain.” Dhenpu ne chutki bajayi, “Sahi bola. Aur logon ko batana hoga ki swasth shareer aur maansikk… Mansikkk…” 
Tarique, “Peace”
Dhenpu, “Haan Maansik peace ke liye kya kya karna chahiye.”
Aachoo thoda ghabraya, “ Par mujhe ye sab nahi pata.”
Denpu, “To pata kar. Ab tu influencer hai beta. Ye sab to karna padega. Warna log follow karna band kar denge tujhe.”
Dabbu, “Likes aur comments sab…”
Tarique, “Zero”

Aachoo ko kuch na karna bahut pasand tha. Par use usse bhi zyada pasand tha Likes aur comments. Aachoo ke doston ne decide kiya ki wo abse kasar ka influencer hai. 
Wo roz subah ki shuruat, Kabeer ya Raheem ke dohe se shuru kar ek 30 second ka pravachan dene laga. 
Uske baad wo apne naashte ki tasveeren, unke paushtik gunon ke saath apne page par daalta. Butter, ghee se labalab parathon ko, apni jeebh ke swasthya ke liye acha bataya. 
Dopahar ke wakt wo apne doston ke saath ghoomne firne nikal jaata. Saath baith kar dost kuch time gappen maarta, logon se milta, reels aur videos banata. Dekhte dekhte Aachoo ke barah sau se teen hazaar, fir paanch hazaar folower ho gaye. Aachoo ki mehnat rang la rahi thi. Pehle dost bolte the, par ab kasar-wasi bhi Aachoo ko kasar ka influencer bolne lage. Aachoo apni lokpriyata se kaafi khush tha. Usko bada hi acha lagta, jab log use raste par aate jaate namaste ya jai sri raam karte. Kasar ka koi bhi utsav, koi bhi samaroh, bina Aachoo ki upasthiti ke pura na hota. 

Unka pasandeeda adda tha Kasar ka world famous, Badnam Cafe. Badnaam cafe kasar me kuch saal pehle hi khula tha. Yahan ki har dish Aachoo kha ke apne social media par daal chuka tha. Ab wo khud hi Badnam ke kitchen me nayi nayi dish banane laga. Udad daal ki maggi, Tandoori chicken chole, pasta sandwich, wagerah wagerah. Badnam cafe ke maalik the Shriman GeeGro sahab. Inka asli naam sirf kasar ke buzurgon ko pata tha, lekin koi bhi is baare me baat karna pasand nahi karta tha. Kisi wajah se har koi inse darta tha. Ye baat kasar ke yuvak aur yuvtiyon ko samajh nahi aati thi. Unko bas hamesha se ye bataya gaya tha ki Geegro aur Badnam cafe se duur raho. Geegro acha aadmi nahi hai. 
Lekin bacche apne badon ki baat sunne lage, to duniya me nayi cheezein ijaad honi band hi ho jayengi. Agar Alexander Fleming ne apni ma ka kaha suna hota, to na wo chemicals se khelta, aur na kabhi penicillin ijaad hota. Yahi baat Newton ke baare me bhi kahi ja sakti hai. Newton ki ma bhi usko baag me jakar khelne se mana kiya karti thi. Par Janab Newton ne apni ma ki ek na suni aur seb ke ped ke neeche apne doston ke saath chidiya udd khelne panhuch gaye. Aachoo ke pita bhi alag nahi the. Unke apne vichaar the ki unke putra ko kaise rehna chahiye. Wo kabse apni parchoon ki dukaan ko khaai ki taraf extend kar ek chota mota cafe banana chahte the. Wo chahte the ki Aachoo isme unki madad kare. Aur fir dheere dheere cafe wo khud hi dekhne lag jaaye. 

Har shaam ghar aane par apne baap ke taane sun sun kar Aachoo pareshaan ho gaya tha. “Kuch karta dharta hai nahi. Poora din doston ke saath awaragardi. Ye kab tak chalega.” Aachoo ko ye baatein sunna acha to na lagta par pita ji usko har hafte 500 rupaye jebkharch dete the. Jisse uska guzara chalta tha. Waise to wo ye baatein kayam churan samajh ke pee jaata. Lekin aj uske pitaji kuch zyada hi gusse me the. Aachhoo ne unko hamesha ki tarah fir se samjhaya. “Daddy!!”
“Daddy, par…”
“Pehle to tu mujhe ye daddy mat bol. Ya to pitaji bol ya papa.”
“Arey yaar papa! Mai influecer hun. Logon ko influence karna mera job hai
Aachoo ke pitaji ye baat Aachhoo ke muh se kayi baar sun chuke the. Lekin har baar unko pehle se zyada hairani hoti. “Influencer hona kya job hui”, wo bolte. “Job wo hoti hai jisme kamayi hoti hai. Kuch paise aate hain, jinse ghar chalta hai” Baat kadwi thi, lekin pitaji ki baat me sacchai thi, jo Aachoo ko bhi khatakti thi. 

Roz roz ki ghitpit se pareshaan ho kar, Aachoo ne apne doston ke saath meeting ki. “Ye sab influencer winfluencer to thik hai. Lekin isse paise kaise kamaye jaate hain.”
Dhenpu ko waise to is baare me kuch pata nahi tha, lekin use lagta tha use sab pata hai, “Bhai brand endorsements” 
“Lekin kasar me to koi brands hai hi nahi” Aachoo bola. 
“Abey hain kaise nahi. Sharmila aunty ki parchoon ki dukan, Rawat Tent house, Qureshi chicken/mutton shop… Bhatere brand hain bhai.”
Aachoo ne apna sar hilaya, “To ye brand mujhe paise kyun denge?”
Dhenu ne muh banaya, jaise ki ye baat kitni obvious ho, “Abey… Tu kasar ka brand ambassador hai bhai. Tujhe bas apne social media par itna bolna hai, ki apni daal, chawal, sabun, manjan, sab Sharmila aunty ki dukan se lo. Ab log to teri baat sunte hi hain. Unki dukan se saman lenge. Unka itna fayeda hoga to wo tujhe iske paise nahi denge?”
Aachoo ko ye baat sahi lagi. Usne fatafat 4-5 video bana dale. Parchoon ki dukan, Semwal atta chakki, Qureshi chicken aur mutton shop, ityadi ityadi.

Agle din Aachoo apne doston ke saath vasooli karne nikal pada. Raste me sabse pehli dukan SHarmila aunty ki padti thi, to wo sabse pehle wahin panhuch gaye. Sharmila aunty Aachoo ko bachpan se jaanti thi. Aachoo har aachar ke season me Aunty ki achaar dalne me madad karta tha. Kathal ke achaar, gobhi ke achaar, Aam ke achaar. Iske badle Aachoo har achaar ki choti sheshi ghar le kar aata. Uske pita ye sab achaar bade chav se khaate, lekin kya unhone kabhi Aachoo ko thank you bola? Kabhi nahi. Aachoo ko is baat ki narazgi thi, ki pitaji ye to dekhte nahi ki kabhi achaar, kabhi mithai, kya kya nahi lata Aachoo ghar. Lekin Pitaji ko paise se matlab hai. 

Khair, Sharmila aunty ki dukan me panhuchte hi Aachoo thoda sharmate, thoda ghabrate hue bola, “Mausi, 500 rupaye de do.” Mausi bina kuch soche, galle me se paise nikalne lagi. Aachoo ko laga ye to bahut sahi ho gaya. Ab wo aise hi brand endorsements karega, aur paise kamayega. Aunty paise dene se pehle poochne lagi, “Apne papa ko mat batana ki maine diye. Waise Kyun chahiye?” 
Aachoo batane me thoda hichkicha raha tha to Dhenpu ne hi samjha diya, “Arey aunty. YE Aachoo apna kasar ka influencer hai. Isne kal video dala tha, ki sab apna samaan Sharmila aunty ki dukan se len. To isne aapka fayeda karwa diya na. Us baat ke paise.”
Sharmila aunty ka haath 100 rupayon ke 5 noton se saath aage badha hua tha. Dhenpu ka spashtikaran sunkar wo haath dheere dheere peeche aane laga. 500 kya, Aachoo 1000 bhi maangta to Sharmila aunty yun hi de deti. Lekin ye kya bakwaas kar rahe hain. Saare ladke aunty ki taraf badi ummeedon se dekh rahe the. Aunty ne bola, “Bhaago yahan se. Ek paisa nahi dene waali tum logon ko. Bade aaye…” 
Dhenpu is baat par bada hairan hua, “Par mausi, Aachoo ne aapke customer badhaye hain.” 
SHarmila mausi apni teekhi zuban me boli, “Kasaar ke saare log pehle se hi unse samaan lete hain. Kaunse customer badha diye tum logon ne?”
Is baat ka na Aachoo ke paas jawab tha, na Dhenpu ke paas. Wo apna sa muh le kar wahan se chal diye. 
Agla padav tha Semwal atta chakki jo Jagdish bhaiji chalate the. JAgdish bhaiji ko in ladkon ki baatein badi hasyapad lagin. 
“Customer badhaoge? Mere?” chaaron ladkon ne apna sar hilaya. Bhaiji thoda hase aur unse sawaal poocha, “Ek baat batao. Agar kasar me kisi ko apna genhu pisana hoga. To wo kahan jayenge?”
LAdko ne ek dusre ko dekha, fir jawab diya, “Aapke paas.”
Bhaiji muskuraye aur bole, “To fir, mujhe tum logon ki kya zarurat hai. Log to waise hi mere paas aa rahe hain.”

Ladke nirash hokar wahan se nikle. Agli teenon dukanon se bhi unhe isi tarah nirasha haath lagi. Bas, Qureshi ji ne ek kilo mutton pakda diya. Free me nahi, udhar par. 

Ye plan to gaya. Ab kuch aur sochna padega. Dabbu ne bola, kayi influencer apne followers se directly paisa maangte hain. Website bhi hai iske liye. Agar hamare photo, video, fashion pasand aa raha hai to hame paise do. Aur photo, video, fashion karne ke liye. Ye idea Aachoo ko bilkul bhi pasand nahi aaya. “Aise bheekh thodi na mangenge bhai. Kaisi baat kar rahe ho. Pitaji jaidad me se bhi bedakhal kar denge.”
 
Tarique ko idea aaya. “Bhai, maine aise influencer bhi dekhe hain, jo kuch na kuch sikhane ke badle paisa lete hain… Jaise guitar, ya drawing.”
Dhenpu aur Dabbu ko bhi ye idea acha laga. Unhone poocha, “Aachoo, tu bata… Tere andar kaunsa talent hai? Tu wohi seekha diyo.”
Aachoo ne apne dimaag par bahut zor dala. Kapde sukhane aur fold karne me mai bahut acha hun. Kasar ke saare gharon me kabhi na kabhi maine kapde sukhaye ya fold kiye hain.”
Dhenpu bigad gaya, “Abey, Kapde fold karna bhi kisi ko sikhana padta hai kya. Wo to mummy hi sikha deti hai”
Dabbu, “Bhai, Aachoo photo bahut sahi kheechta hai. Iske account pe dekh lo. Ek se ek photuen hain. Photo kheechne ka course kara sakta hai.”
Dhenpu ko ek baar ko laga ye sahi idea hai. Usne turrant apna phone nikala aur Aachoo ki saari tasveeren dekhne laga. “Haan, ye idea kaam kar sakta hai. Aachoo photugraphy ka course kara sakta hai.”
Tarique, “Par ye saari to Aachoo ki photuyen hain.”
Dhenpu, “To?”
Aachoo, “To agar in photuyon me mai hun to kheech kaun raha hai.”
Kayi baar zindagi me aise kshan aate hain jinme insaan ko lagta hai ki shayad wo apna dimaag ghar bhul aaya ho. Aisa hi ehsaas is kshan Dhenpu ko hua. “Abey tere andar koi talent hai bhi ki nahi.”
Aachoo, “Abey talent hai tabhi to kasar ka influencer hun. Aise hi thodi na log mujhe itna chahte hain. Aur sunn… Influencer banna bhi bade talent ka kaam hai. Aise hi nahi koi ban jaata Influencer.”
Dhenpu ko ye baat chamki. Ye to launda sahi bol gaya. Influencer banna bhi ek talent hai. To kyun na yahi talent logon ko becha jaye. 
Aachoo itne samay se influencergiri kar raha tha, ki ye uske swabhav ka ek eham hissa ban gayi thi. Ye usko aati to thi, lekin agar samjhane ko kaho, to pata nahi. Khana kaise khate hain. Muh me daalte hain, fir baaki kaam to shareer karta hai. 

Par iske alawa Aachoo ke paas aur koi talent nahi tha. Na wo ga sakta tha, na wo kuch baja sakta tha. Dance me usko 2-3 steps hi aate the. Jo ki wo apne har video me istemaal kar chuka tha. 

Agle din se Aachoo aur uske teen doston ne Aachoo ke instructional video banane shuru kiye. Kuch apne atpate experience se, kuch doston ki anokhi salahon se kuch dino me ek course tayyar ho gaya. 
Iski marketing ke liye kasar aur aas paas ke kasbon me poster bhi laga diye gaye. 100 rupaye me influencer baniye. Finally ek bande ne course khareed liya. Chaaron ne decide kiya ki un sau rupayon ki wo chowmien khayenge aur jaate hue aur logon ko protsahit karenge ki wo bhi ye course khareeden. 

Raaste me jaate hue wo jisse bhi poochte, wo bolta ki unhone course dekh liya hai. Is baat ki Aachoo aur Dhenpu ko hairani hui. Agar sabne dekh liya hai to fir abhi tak account me 100 hi rupaye kyun aaye. Ye chaaron yahi baat par dimaag kharch kar hi rahe the, ki saamne se Manju bua ki beti rinku kudti faandti aati dikhayi di. Ye chaaron apna course uspar bhi lapatne lage. 
“Par bhaiya maine to aapka course dekh liya hai. Bahut mazedaar tha.”
“Par kaise? Tune 100 rupaye bhare?” Dhenpu poocha. 
“Paise? Kaunse paise? Saare video to aapne apne social media par daale hain.”

Chaaron bahut niraash ho kar badnaam cafe me chowmein khaane baith gaye. Har ek se jakar paise lene padenge. Par ab kaise pata chalega ki kis kis ne video dekhe hain. Aachoo ab pareshaan ho gaya tha. WO bola, “Jis jis ne video like kiya hai, usne zarur dekha hoga. Hum har us insaan se paise lenge jisne hamara video like kiya hai.” Aachoo ke har video par like aur comment karta tha. Wo bhi Aachoo ki tarah influencer banna chahta tha. Aachoo ne use pakda, par kewal 40 rupaye hi vasool kar paaye. Fir unhe mile Bisht ji. Bisht ji ne badi anakani ki. Par 100 rupaye nikal kar de hi diye. 
Aachoo ko apne is idea par bada naaz hua. Usne hisaab lagaya. Abhi mere video par 300 like hai. 2 din me mere saare follower like kar denge. 
“Saare follower?” Tarique ne poocha. 
Aachoo ne bade garv ke saath kaha, “Haan pure paanch hazaar teen sau chaurasi followers.” 
Dabbu ne hisaab lagaya, “To ek follower ka 100 ke hisaab se banta hai… pachas hazaar rupaye.”
Dhenpu ne uski tapli pe ek lagayi, “ 50000 nahi 5 lakh.”
Sabki aankhen khuli ki khuli reh gayi. Itna paisa jo aane waala tha. 

Isi utsah ke saath chaaron vasooli ke liye aage badh gaye. Raaste me Reshma chachi apne kutte Pravin ko ghumane nikli hui thin. Aachoo ne muskurate hue, unse 100 rupaye maange. Reshma chachi bhi wapas muskurayin aur bola, “Tere video like karne ke na. Maine unlike kar diya.” 
Aachoo is baat par bahut dukhi aur krodhit hua. “Unlike kar diya? Kyun?” 
“Tu paise jo maang raha tha.”
“Arey to kuch seekhne ke paise lagte hain ki nahi. YAhan apna kasar ka influencer tumko famous banna sikha raha hai, uska kuch to…” isse pehle ki Dhenpu apni baat poori kar paata, Reshma aunty ne use roka, “Hamne to yun hi dekh liya. Humko thodi na banna hai famous. Hum to apne pravin aur gullu ke saath hi khush hain.”
“Acha thik hai… PAr aap wapas like karo.”
Reshma aunty hichkichayin. Unhe pata nahi tha ki Aachoo like karne ke paise to nahi le lega. 
“Like kiya to paise dene padenge.” Dhenpu garja. 
“Arey nahi dene padenge. Aap bas like karo.” Achoo ne request ki. 
Reshma aunty ne apna phone nikala aur hichkichate hue videos ko wapas like kar diya. Itne me pravin aunty ka haath chuda ke bhag gaya. Badi mushkil se AAchoo ne bhaag bhaag ke use pakda. 
Achoo ne dekha ki dheere dheere uske likes kam ho rahe hain. Log paise dene ke darr se uske saare photo aur video unlike kar rahe hain. Aachoo ghabraya. Wo nikla to paise vasool karne tha, par ab uski ye yatra logon ko ye samjhane me vyarth ho gayi ki, wo kisi se paise nahi lega. Bas wapas uski posts ko like kar dijiye. 

Aachoo Dhenpu par garja. Ye kaise idea deta hai tu. Abhi mere saare follower aur likes chale jaate. Dono me bada ghamasaan jhagda hua. Jiske baad dono apne apne ghar chale gaye. 

Aachoo ne decide kiya ki wo ab sirf apni influencergiri pe hi dhyan dega. Baaki cheezein risky hain. Unse followers jaane ka khatra hai. 

Agle kuch hafte aisa hi chalta raha. Wahi subah uth kar apne morning routine ka video banana. Apne naashte ki tasveeren, lafandargiri, aur shaam ko pitaji ki gaaliyan. 

Fir garmiyon ka samay aaya. Ye touriston ke kasar aane ka samay tha, aur Aachhoo ke liye apne follower badhane ka mauka. 

Is saal, Kasar ek dilli ka ek group aaya. Group ke sabhi log influencer the. Koi travel influencer, koi lifestyle aur fashion, to koi dance. Jab kasar ke logon ko pata chala ki ye log bhi influencer hain, to unhe laga ye Aachoo ko bhi jaante honge. Par inme se kisi ko khabar bhi nahi thi ki ye Aachoo kya cheez hai. 

Khair, wo log Kasar ke sabse mehnge resort, Kumaoni, me ruke the. Shaam tak unke aane ki khabar Aachoo ke paas bhi panchuch gayi. Aachoo ko laga ki usko in logon se milna chahiye. YE aakhir usi ke samuday ke log hain. Inse jaan pehchaan banana, inki kasar bhraman araamdayak banana. 

To, yahi soch kar Aachoo mahashay, Dhenpu ke saath Kumaoni resort panhuch gaye. Resort me entry Aachoo ke liye koi badi baat nahi thi. Kisi na kisi kaam se wahan Aachoo ka aana jaana laga hi rehta tha. Wahan panhuch ke Aachoo ne dekha ki sab resort ke cafe me khaana kha rahe the. 

Aachoo ne aav dekha na taav aur seedha unki table par panhuch gaya. “Hello! Myself Aaroh Chauhan.” 
Group me 3 ladkon ke alawa, 2 ladkiyan bhi thi. Unme se ek boli, “Aaroh Chauhan kaun.” Is ladki ka waise to naam Payal tha par uska social media naam SoDeBabe tha. So matlab south, De maane Delhi aur Babe to sab jaante hi hain. 
Aaroh ne thoda vistaar me bataya, “Mai aap logon ki tarah hi hun… Influencer. Kasar ka.”
Dhenpu ne joda, “ Aachoo naam ka handle hai.”
Group ke sabhi log ek saath bol pade, “Oh Aachoooo…”
Aachoo ko laga ki wo bada famous ho gaya hai. Ye log bhi naam se pehchaan gaye. Par uski ye khushfehmi agle hi second duur ho gayi. 
Ek ladka, jisko sab WanderingB keh ke bulate the, ne spashtikaran diya, “Subah se yahan jisse milte hain, wo poochta hai ki tumko jaante hain kya?”
Aachoo ne apna parichay diya, Mai kasar ka influencer ke naate aap sab ko welcome karta hun. Yahan ke sab logon ko mai aur sab log mujhe jaante hain. Agar kisi bhi cheez ki zarurat ho to mujhe bataana. 
Sabne ek swar me use dhanyawad kiya. 
Ye sunkar Aachoo unke saath hi baith gaya. 

Group me 3 ladke aur 2 ladkiyan thin. SodeBabe ek fashion aur lifestyle influencer thi. Uska sapna tha apna khud ka fashion brand lable. Uske 3 lakh se upar follower the. Ye jaankar Aachoo ko jhatka laga. 3 lakh logon aap ko jaante hain?
“Jaante to aur bhi honge, par follow 3 lakh karte hain.” AlwaysRightKhurana bola. AlwaysRight ek political influencer tha. Desh ki rajneetink gatividhiyon par video banata tha. Government ke khilaaf protest karne waale, government ka virodh karne waale logon ke virudh, Ma behen ki gaaliyon se les videos aur articles likhta tha. Pichle saal whatsapp groups me sabse zyada shared content me inhi ki videos aur forwards shumaar the. Social media ki duniya me inki baat sahi hai kyunki wo hai AlwaysRightKhurana. In mahashay ke 6 lakh follower the.  
Baap re baap. Politics pe bhi influencing kar sakte hain, Aachoo ne socha. Bade garv se AlwaysRight ne use pradhanmantri ji ke saath apni photo dikhayi. Wo usko politics me influencing karne ka puraskar de rahe the. Aachoo ko ehsaas hua ki ye to bade log hain. Yahan Aachoo ki MLA sahab se saath ek bhi photu nahi thi. Aur ye pradhanmantri ke saath photo khichwa ke aaye hain. 
Is baat par group me pratispardha ho gayi. TanManNoDhan, jo ek dance, yoga, fitness… aur shayad mansik swasthya visheshagya bhi thi, ne Shahrukh khaan ke saath ek photo dikhayi. Aachoo bhauchakka reh gaya. SHAHRUKH KHAN, Aachoo ke sabse pasndeeda abhineta the. 
Alwaysright bola, “Aise to meri bhi photo hai bobby deol ke saath.” 
Agar ye baat Always right ne 10 second pehle boli hoti, to shayad Aachoo “impress” ho jaata. Par wo TanManNoDhan ki SHahrukh khan ke saath photu dekh chuka tha. Uske man me is pratispardha ka ek hi vijeta tha. 
Tabhi peeche se AnirodhSood bola, “I can beat that.” AnurodhSood ek Youth influencer tha. Waise to wo kewal 29 saal ke the, par unki twacha se unki umra 20 ki lag rahi thi. Anirodh desh ke jawan logon ko jawani jeena sikhate the. Jaise Aachoo kasarwasiyon ko jeene ke tareeke sikhata hai. Anirodh ke PANDRAH LAKH followers the. 
AnirodhSood ne kuch der apne phone me scroll kiya aur fir ek tasveer par muskurane ke baad apna phone baaki logon ki taraf ghumaya. Aachoo aur Dhenpu ki aankhen khuli ki khuli reh gayin. Photo me Anirodh ne batakh jaisa muh banaya hua tha. Aur bagal me Deepika Padukone uske gaalon par… Uff… Aachoo me mann me is pratispardha ka naya vijeta tha. 
Ye kaisi zindagiyan jee rahe hain ye log. Yahan Aachoo apne aap ko bahut bada aadmi maanta tha kyunki uske Kasar me sabse zyada follower hain. Par ye log to alag hi level par hain. 
Aachoo ne group ke paanchve sadsya se bhi pooch liya, “Aap kis prasidh abhineta se mile hain?” In mahashay ne sar hilate hue kaha, “I’m not an influencer. I have a job. I work in IT.” Aachoo ki saans me saans aayi, “Oh to aap computer engineer hain.” Prasidh Yadav naam ke ye sajjan muskuraye aur bole, “Haan, aisa hi kuch.”
Aachoo ye sun kar khush hua. Use is baat ki khushi thi ki koi to hai jiske usse kam follower hain, “To aap job karte hain.”
Prasidh ne haan me mundi hilayi aur, kuch taunt, kuch mazaak me, kaha, “SAb influencer to nahi ho sakte na. Kuch logon ko kaam bhi karna padta hai.”
YE baat Aachoo ko badi mazedaar lagi to wo has diya. Par group ke kuch logon ko ye baat nagawaar guzri. AlwaysRight bhadak gaya, “Kaam mai bhi karta hun. Tujhe kya lagta hai mere video aise hi ban jaate hain.”
Aachhoo, “To kaise bante hain Always Bhaiya?”
Always right 1 second ke liye soch me pad gaya. Use nahi pata tha ki sawaal bhi aa jayege. Is par Sode bol padi, “You know kitni research lagti hai har video ko banane ke liye? Fir wardrobe, editing. Ye sab bhi hame khud hi karna padta hai.”
Aachhoo ne apna sar hilaya. Ye baatein use bahut prabhavit kar rahi thin. 
Anirodh ne baat sambhalne ki koshish ki, “Uska ye matlab nahi tha yaar. Why are you all getting angry. He was only joking.” Anirodh ne Prasidh ko ek kohni maari. Prasidh ka dhyan wapas group ki gatividhiyon par aaya aur wo bola, “HAan… Mai majaak kar raha tha. It was only a joke.”
TanManNoDhan ko is baat se koi fark nahi padta tha. Magar usne apna influencing career, choti moti baaton ko jaane dene se nahi banaya tha. Aakrosh uska sabse paina hathiyar tha. Usne chala diya, “YOu can’t joke like this man. You know the stigma of being an influencer. Logon ko lagta hai ki hum koi kaam nahi karte, but you know it’s not true.”
Aachhoo ne sar hilaya, “Haan. Sahi keh rahin hai TanMan Didi. Mere papa bhi mere peeche pade rehte hain.”
TanMan, “Dekha. You are punching down bro. Jokes should be punching up.”
Dhenpu in sab baaton se bore ho chuka tha. Use samajh nahi aa raha tha ki ye sab kyun lad rahe hain. Dhenpu ne Aachoo ki shirt kheechi, “Ghar chalein.”
Aachoo, “Abey ruk na. Abhi to maza aa raha hai.”
Tabhi Achhoo ka phone baj pada. Uske pitaji ka ye nauvan call tha. Isse pehle ke saare call Aachhoo ne kaat diye the. Dhenpu ne samjhaya, “Tera Baap tera khoon kar dega. Ghar chal.”
Sode, “You need permission from your father? That’s strange. How old are you?”
Aachoo thoda sharminda hua aur bola, “CHaubees.”
TanManNodhan, “I’m 22 dude. Aur mere dad ko pata bhi nahi ki mai yahan kasar me hun.”
Aachoo, “To wo phone karke poochte nahi, ki beta kahan ho.”
Sode, “Are you kidding. Hamari apni life hai. Parents ko isse kya matlab ki mai kahan, kiske saath ghoom rahi hun.”
Acchoo ye sun kar niraash hua. Tabhi Aachoo ka phone fir baj pada. 
Dhenpu, “CHal bhai. Meri mummy bhi ghante bhar se call kar rahi hai.”
AlwaysRight ne apna haath Aachoo ke kandhe par rakha aur bola, “Utha le aur bata de, ki tu ab chaubees saal ka MARD ho gaya hai. Jitni der chaahe bahar reh sakta hai.”
Sabki aakhein ab Aachoo par tiki thi. Aachoo ne himmat karke phone uthaya aur bola, “Papa mai…”
Papa, “Papa mai ke bacche. Raat ke 10 baj gaye hain. Tujhe ghar nahi aana.”
Aachoo ko sabne ishaaron se protsahit kiya. Achoo bol pada, “Papa mai chaubees saal ka mard hun.”
Papa, “To? Abhi ghar nahi ayaa to kal tak nahi rahega.”
Achoo, “Please yaar papa. Mai ab baalig ho chuka hun. Aap mujhe par aise hukum nahi chala sakte. 
5 kshan ke liye phone par khamoshi chaa gayi. 
Aachoo, “Papa?”
Papa, “Beta. Tu abhi ghar nahi aaya, to tu baalig to kya zinda bhi nahi rahega.” 
Aachhoo ko ab krodh aa gaya tha. Wo kuch bolne hi waala tha ki tabhi uski ma ne phone cheen liya aur kaha, “Beta meri to saans hi nahi aa rahi hai. Baagh ghoom raha hai gaanv me. Semwal ji ke kutte ko utha ke le gaya.”
Aachoo, “Maaa. Mai aa jaunga.”
Maa, “Nahi beta, Abhi aa. Warna mujhe neend nahi aayegi.”
Vaad vivaad me insaan kitna bhi acha ho jaye, chaahe wo duniya ka sabse bada lawyer hi kyun na ho, apni ma ke tarkheen baaton ka kisi ke paas koi jawab nahi hota. Aachoo ne hathiyar rakh diye, “Acha ma. Aata hun. 10 minute.”

Aachoo ne phone kata aur sabko apologetic nazron se dekha. Aachoo ki baaton me jhagda ab samapt ho chuka tha. 
Dhenpu turrant khada ho gaya aur Aachoo ko kheenchne laga. Aachoo ne sabko alvida kaha aur kal milne ka vada kiya. 

Ghar panhuchne par Aachoo ke pita ji uspar bhadak gaye. Wohi purana, ghisa pita: Kuch kaam karta nahi, awaaragardi karta hai. Ityadi ityadi. 
IS baar Aachoo ke paas jawab the. Aapko kya lagta hai mere video aur post aise hi ban jaate hain.” Aj raat ki party ki baaton ne uske influencing karne ke nishchay ko aur bhi sudrudh kar diya. Aapko pata hai kitni research lagti hai har video ko banane ke liye? Fir wardrobe, editing, lighting. Ye sab kaam mai hi karta hun. Bas baitha nahi rehta poora din. 
Uske pitaji thodi der ko shaant hue. Unko thoda shock laga Aachoo ke muh se technical baatein sunke, jinka unko koi gyan nahi tha. Aachoo ko kuch aur baatein yaad aayin. Sode ka followers badhane ka plan use yaad aaya. Use niche, small business owner, branding naam ke shabd yaad aate. Aachoo ne ye bhi apne baap par lapet diya. “Mai apna ek brand banane ki koshish kar raha hun Daddy. Mai ek small business owner hun. Mera plan hai, ki alag alag tarah ka content bana ke mai apni niche dhoondhun. Aur aap hai ki mujhe meri niche banane hi nahi de rahe hain.”
Aachhoo ke pitaji kuch bolne ko hi the, par ab tak Aachoo me confidence aa chuka tha. Usne apni pitaji ko kaata aur bola, “Aur mai ab chaubees saal ka ho gaya hun. Mujhe baat baat par toka mat karo. Ab mai mard hun. Pitaji ki bhonhe tirchi huin. Aachoo ko laga ki ye uski jeet hui hai. Isse pehle ki pitaji kuch bolein, ghoom ke style se kamre ki taraf nikal jaana chahiye. Pitaji chup rahe. 

POori raat pitaji ko neend nahi aayi. Wo soch me pad gaye the ye niche kya hoti hai. Aachoo kabse small business owner ban gaya. Aur wo bech kya raha hai. POori raat ye sawaal, pitaji ke dimaag me chalte rahe. Subah naashte par bhi wo use kuch na bole. Bas ghoorte rahe. Wo Aachoo se hi poochte to Aachoo ki baaton ko vaidhta mil jaati. Ki haan, Aachoo ki baaton me dum hai. Usko pata hai wo kya kar raha hai. Par pitaji abhi is badlaav ke liye tayyar nahi the.
Dukaan par baithe baithe unhone google kiya. Niche Meaning: a specialized segment of the market for a particular kind of product or service. Fir unhone google kiya: Niche in hindi. Jawab aaya taak. Wo aur bhi confuse ho gaye. 
Badola ji chaipatti lene aaye. Wo university me Itihaas ke professor the. Pitaji ne socha ki inse hi pooch lena chahiye, “Badola ji… Bal… Ye niche ki hondu.” Badola ji ne Pitaji ko dekha aur ek gahre bhav ke saath bola, “Daridra… Ekdum vibhatsva. Khalnayak. Ittihas me neech log bahut hain. Ek se ek neech. Ajkal bhi rajneeti me sab neech hain. PM saab ko chodke.” Pitaji ne sar hilaya aur bola, “NAhi nahi, Badola ji. Mai english waale Neech ki baat kar raha hun.” Badola ji soch me pad gaye, “English me bhi neech hondu chun.” Unhone apna phone nikala aur google kiya: Neech meaning. “Ye to hindi waala hi bata raha hai. Pitaji phone dekhne lage, dikhana kya spelling likhi aapne. Itne me Daktar saab wahan panhuche, “Arey Chauhan ji, ek dabba cigarette de do.” Unhone dekha wahan gahan chintan chal raha hai. 
Thode samay pashchaat wahan BAdola ji aur daktar saab ke alawa, 4-5 aur log jama ho gaye. Unki duvidha duur hui jab Prasidh Yadav wahan panhuche. Unko bhi ek dabba cigarette chahiye thi. Jab Prasidh ko pata chala ki baat kya hai to usne samjhaya, “Niche matlab… Specialization ek tarah se.”
“Jaise, engineering?” “Medical”
Prasidh ne sir hilaya, “Haan waisa ki kuch.”
Pitaji kuch kuch samjhe, “Jaise Arora ji ki darzi ki dukaan hai to tailoring uski neech ho gayi. Guddu ki electrical parts ki dukaan uski neech.”
Prasidh muskuraya, “Sahi pakde uncle.”
Pitaji khush hue aur sabko unka samaan diya. Jab sab chale gaye to pitaji ne Prasidh ko roka aur poocha, “Beta hamne joote, colddrink, kapdon ke brand hote sune the. Ye insaan bhi brand hota hai kya.”
Prasidh thodi jaldi me tha, “Hota hai uncle. Aajki duniya me agar khud ko bechna hai to insaan ko bhi brand banna padta hai.” Itna bol kar wo wahan se khisak liya. PAr pitaji ke dimaag me tarah tarah ke khayal aate rahe. 

Aachoo subah subah uth ke sabse pehle resort panhuch gaya. Kal raat ko sabne Aachoo se request ki thi wo unko Kasar ghumaye. Unko Kasar ka local experience chahiye tha. Koi waqt tay nahi hua tha. Pahadiyon ke liye waqt waise bhi ek flexible hota hai. Usko bas itna bataya gaya tha ki kal early morning aa jaana. 
Aachoo ke pitaji kehte the ki Aachoo bahut aalsi hai. Der subah tak sota rehta hai. Pitaji ki Der subah ka matlab subah ke 8 baje hai. Isiliye Aachoo jab nau baje resort panhucha to use laga ki ye log bechaare wait kar rahe honge, ya ab tak kahin chale gaye honge. 
Resort panhuchte hi usne Prasidh ji ko room ke bahar cigarette peete. Aachoo ne uske paas panhuch ke use bola, “Sorry. Thoda late ho gaya.” 
Prasidh ne use ashcharya se dekha. Use laga Aachoo mazak kar raha hai. Aachoo ke chehre par glani matlab guilt dekh ke use ehsas hua ki wo serious hai aur wo has pada, “Nau baje? Late!”
Aachoo muskuraya aur bola, “Haan! Sode ji ne early morning kaha tha na.”
Prasidh thoda aur hasa. Fir apni cigarette peene laga, “Bro, gyarah baje se pehle koi nahi uthne waala.”
Hairan hone ke baari ab Aachoo ki thi, “gyarah baje? Lekin tab tak to…” Usne 2 second socha aur bola, “Chaliye sabko uthate hain, “gyarah baje to dhoop tez ho jaayegi. Prasidh is kaam ke liye tayyar nahi tha, lekin Aachoo ne uska haath kheecha aur Kamre ke andar dhamak gaya. Andar AlwaysRight aur Anirodh so rahe the. Aachoo ne unke rajai kheechte hue bola, “Utho utho chalo. Subah ho gayi hai. HAme bahar jaana hai.” AlwaysRight aur Anirodh, dono hi itni subah uthne ki aadat me nahi the aur rajai kheechne lage. Lekin Aachoo unki ne unki ek nahi suni. Aachoo ko gaaliyan dete hue, sabse pehle AlwaysRight utha aur bathroom ko chal diya. 
Ab ladkiyon ki baari thi. Aachoo unke kamre ke bahar kaafi der tak khatkhatata raha. Thodi der me ladke bhi tayyar ho kar aa gaye. Unko is baat ka gussa tha ki unko to rajai nikaal kar utha diya par ye ladkiyan abhi tak so rahi hain. Wo seedhe darwaaza peetne lage. 
TanManNoDhan ne paseene se tar batar chote kapdon me darwaaza khola. Kamre ki balcony me yoga mat bicha tha aur saamne phone record karne ke liye set tha. Wo gusse me chillai, “What the hell man. Mai apna daily Yoga post bana rahi hun.” Har koi shant ho gaya. Andar se SoDeBabe phoolon se saji safed raung ki sundress, aur ek khoobsoorat sa sun hat pehene hue darwaaze ki taraf aayi, “Early morning bola tha na Aachoo. It’s 9.30 now.” Aachoo kuch Sode ki khoobsoorti se hairan tha aur kuch late ho jaane se pareshaan. TanManNoDhan ne 10 minute me ready hone ka waada kar ke darwaaza band kiya. Baaki log breakfast table ki taraf chale gaye. 

Table par bhinn bhinn prakar ka khaana saja tha. Khaane waale to kewal 5 the, lekin khana 15 janon ka mangwa liya gaya tha. Aur har koi alag alag angle se photuyen le raha tha. Kisi ne Aachoo se poocha, tu nahi lega photo. Aachoo ne ghar par hi apni naashte ki phto le kar social media par daal di thi. AlwaysRight hasa, “Abey to kya ho gaya. YE saari pitcures hum kaunsa abhi post karne waale hain. Kuch abhi karenge. Kuch kabhi aur.” 
Achoo confuse hua, “Kabhi aur matlab? Naashta to aapne aaj hi mangwaaya hai na.” Sode bade pyar se muskurayi. Use Aachoo ki nadaani kaafi ‘cute’ lag rahi thi. Usne bataya, “Dekho! Hum har samay to vacations pe ja nahi sakte na. Lekin hamare followers ko hamesha naya content chahiye. To kahan se laoge.” AlwaysRight ne uska jawaab diya, “Aage 6 mahine 1 saal baad photos daal ke bol denge ki kasar ki short trip ki hai.”
Aachoo ke dimaag me saari baatein acche se ja rahi thin, “Matlab zaruri nahi hai ki mai jis din jo naashta kha raha hun wahi social media par daalun.”
Always right, “Aur kya. WArna roz parathe, bread omelet ki photo dekh ke tere bhi followers bore ho jayenge.”
Photos ho jaane ke baad, kisi ne ek sandwich uthaya, kisi ne paratha. Ab tak TanManNoDhan bhi table par aa chuki thi. Usne kewal fal aur salad ki photuen lin. Bahut saara khaana waste karne ke baad sab chalne ko ready ho gaye. Aachoo ne ek baar ko poocha bhi, ki ye itna saara khaana?
Sode ne apne kandhe uchke aur bole, hamara kya ja raha hai. It’s not like we paid for them. 
Acchoo hairan hua, “To fir?”

---
