================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Road-trip narrative: borrowing from loan shark Danger Singh, working at GeeGro's Badnam Cafe in Himachal, and finding Ghoomti Chappal's fake green-screen setup in Delhi.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

I have decided to keep writing. Explore various ideas. Maybe some of them will work out. 

So initially I had thought of two boys, living in a small town, wanting to be influencers. They would make these stupid videos.

Physical actions and emotional Dilemma. Theme: based on Hero’s flaw. 


Hero wants to be famous. The flaw will come from this desire only. So, what can be the pitfalls of wanting to be famous?
Becoming famous at all cost. The hero will do anything that makes people impressed by him. Will he do embarrassing things to be famous. Does he have any conditions to his desire? Is infamy okay with him. I think not. What if it is? Maybe the hero thinks that it doesn’t matter if it’s embarrassing, as long as people know him or like him, it’s fine. 
Becoming famous at all cost. Embarrassment, infamy. Does the hero think infamy is good. MAybe infamy is good but not embarrassment. Is he hardworking? Lets make the hero hardworking. 

Hero in an IT cell? What if the hero start working in an IT cell. Making posts and promoting propaganda. That will be a political type of story. Uncovering of the truth. Is that our story? No. That’s not our story. 

What if I combine my kidnapping story with this one. A girl fed up with her job and family problems runs away. On the way she finds  a couple of wannabe influencers in need of money. They find out about the girl’s story and decide to stage her kidnapping to get the money they need. This is interesting. So what will they do.

There is this character he calls himself Sosal baba. He runs a company that trains and makes influencers. Our two main characters decide to join the course that sosal baba offers. It’s 1 lakh rupees. There are 15-20 people who have joined this program. OUr heros Acchoo and Dabbu join the program. 
The program is that they will travel from Dilli to Ladakh in a bus. 
*********

Another break in writing. SHould we go back to the hero’s flaw. Achoo wants to be famous. He believes being famous gives you access to things. It solves all your problems. Even his problems will be solved if he becomes famous. Famous people are just better. They know better. They are examples for the rest of the world. He also wants to be an example for the rest of the world. 
IF that’s the flaw. Thinking that famous people are better people. If you are famous, all your problems go away. Then he should encounter situations where he meets famous people. They will all have their own problems. 
The theme then would be: Being famous and popular doesn’t solve your problems. You solve your problems by facing them head on. You have to accept your problems. You have to become a better person, who cares about others rather than others caring about you. 

This would mean that at some point Achoo will become famous, but his problems will not go away. He will still have to deal with them. 

So at midpoint, his desire will come true. Right? Let’s not think about it that way. Let’s go sequentially. 
*********

Once upon a time, there was a boy called Achoo. He was a 24 year old lazy boy. He didn’t want to work. He sat around in his house all day and roamed around with his friend Dabbu. They had other friends also, but they got busy with their jobs and didn’t have any time to hang out with Achoo. Achoo blamed them for this. He thought they are being arrogant and haughty after getting jobs. 

Achoo and Dabbu roam around town all day, making videos and reals. Posting them on social media. They hope that one of these days one of their videos will get viral and they will become famous. Their friends and family have tried getting them jobs, but they don’t listen to them. They don’t want to do a normal job. They believe that if they become famous, they will not have to work a single day in life. 

Their families are fed up of their uselessness. They don’t know what to do with them. Achoo keeps asking his father for money. At first his father would oblige, but now even his father has stopped giving his money. 
Achoo is also frustrated that his content doesn’t get likes and views. If he could get just one viral video, then he would become famous and wouldn’t have to work a day in life. 

He reads about this company that promises to make you an influencer. Achoo and Dabbu decide to get in touch with the owner. The owner, called Sosal baba, promises big things. He tells them that he can get their videos viral for 50k bucks each. Achoo and Dabbu don’t have that kind of money. They somehow take out a loan from a loan shark to pay sosal baba. Sosal baba takes the money and tells them to go and make videos. And he will get them 1 lakh followers in no time. There is another person with Sosal baba Ghoomti chappal. He has 4-5 lakh followers. He ensures them that Sosal baba is like his mentor and he is the reason he has become so famous. 

Achoo and Dabbu are ecstatic. They make many videos. Contact local shops and restaurants for branded content. These business are reluctant to offer them any money. Achoo makes a deal with a travel company owner. He will make content for him and in return he will get them many more new customers. He will do this for free. But he will take a cut in the profits that they make. THe owner agrees. 

After Achoo and Dabbu has made enough videos, they decide to go to sosal baba to get views, shares and followers. But, when they go to the office, sosal baba is not there. They find out that sosal baba is a scammer. He has taken money from others and run away. Now achoo and Dabbu are in trouble. They don’t know what to do. The loan shark, Danger singh, sends his goons to get his money back. Achoo and Dabbu don’t have any money to pay back. They get threatened and given a month’s time to pay back the money with interest. Which comes out to 2 lakh rupees. Achoo and Dabbu are in deep trouble. They have nowhere to go. Achoo asks his father for the money. But his father doesn’t have that kind of money to give him. 

Now there is a threat on achoo and Dabbu’s life. They have no other option but to look for Sosal baba. They have a clue. They decide to contact one of the influencers, that Sosal baba marketed as one of his customers. 

This guy is a travel influencer, so it is difficult to track him down. They see from his post that he is in Himachal. He will be in himachal till 4/20 to celebrate the date. It’s April 1, So, they decide to track him down in himachal. They sell some of their belongings to buy a bus ticket to Himachal to track this guy down. They have about 25K in cash for their expenses. 

They reach Himachal in Parvathi valley, where the travel blogger ghoomtichappal is supposed to be. They see that Chappal has tagged himself at a cafe in a secluded location. Achoo and Dabbu can’t believe their luck. They will find him so easily. They go to that location. It’s a place called Badnam cafe. The cafe is filled with young people. The owner of the cafe is an old guy with a long beard and flowing locks. His name is GeeGro. He tells them that he hasn’t seen the guy anywhere around. 
Achoo and Dabbu are confused. They decide to stay in himachal for a couple of days and look for Ghoomti chappal. Unfortunately, they lose their money. As they are counting how much money they have left, a gust of wind takes the money away from their hands. They are in a pickle. How will they find Chappal. Where will they stay without money? WHat will they eat. Geegrow offers them a job. He tells them that they will have to work a few hours at the cafe. He will give them 1000 bucks a day each, a room to stay and two meals a day. They can work for a week while they look for whoever they are looking for. Achoo and Dabbu are reluctant, but they don’t have any other option. They agree. Gee grow tells them that they will have to work 8 hours a day. 2 hours in the morning, taking care of his farm and 6 hours at night, when the cafe opens for business. In between, they are free to go wherever they like. 

The next day they start working in the cafe and GeeGrow’s marijuana farm and go to different places, Ghoomti chappal has supposedly visited, but no one has seen them. They decide to go to all the hotels and hostels nearby to check if he is staying in one of those places. But can’t find anything. Then, Ghoomti chappal posts about a hostel he is staying in, promoting it to his followers. 

Finally, Achoo and dabbu have a clue. They go to the hostel, but they find that he is not in their reservation/guest list. They think that because he is famous, he must’ve been staying under a fake name. So they decide to wait outside the hostel for the guy. Unfortunately, ghoomti chappal is not there. Dabbu is disheartened, but Achoo picks him up saying that Chappal is a famous and trusted influencer, he will be somewhere in Himachal. They will keep looking till they find him. 
The next day, they see an instagram live of Ghoomti Chappal at a famous picnic spot khudkhushi point. They rush to that place in hopes that now they will finally be able to talk to ghoomti chappal and find out about Sosal baba. 

When they reach the khudkhushi point, they see that no one is there. They are confused. How can this happen? Ghoomti chappal is still live on Khudkhushi point, drinking tea at a tea stall. But the tea stall is empty. They are the only ones there. They don’t understand what is happening. Discouraged, they debate what to do next. The best bet is to go home find a job and talk to danger singh to give them a few more months to repay the loan. 

Achoo is not done yet. He thinks that Chappal lives in Delhi. They can go and track him down there. Dabbu is not so eager. 1. Chappal is a traveler and might not be in delhi. 2. IT was so difficult to find him in Parvathi valley, it would be almost impossible to find him in a big city like delhi. Dabbu does have a point.

Danger singh has been looking for Achoo and Dabbu. He is under a lot of pressure himself. His own boss, Nuclear Khan, is after him. Nuclear pays a surprise visit to Danger and does a quaterly appraisal. He finds out that about a corore rupees are missing from the accounts. Danger is extremely afraid of Nuclear and tells him that two boys had taken loans from him and have now run away. Nuclear tells him to find those guys. Side note: Nuclear is a dangerous person. He is also a big time loan shark. Whoever doesn’t pay their dues, he makes them beg online. Setting up a payment account.
Nuclear tells Danger to find those kids and get his money back or put him in Nuclear’s online beggar party otherwise, he will put danger in danger. Danger singh is afraid and finds Achoo and Dabbu from their social media posts and sends his best goons, Dayan dola, bayan dola and chaudi chaati to look for them in himachal. 

DD, BD and CC go to himachal and find Achoo and Dabbu. IN trouble, Achoo and Dabbu ask Geegro for his help. Geegro gives them their salary and sends them on their way home. Telling them that he will handle the goons. Achoo and Dabbu run away. But they realize that if they go home, Danger will find them and they will be in danger once again. So they decide to go to delhi to find Ghoomti chappal and Sosal Baba. 

Achoo and Dabbu reach Delhi in hopes of finding Chappal. They find out that there is an influencer event happening. They are sure that they will find Ghoomti chappal there. The influencer event is exclusive and is only for influencers above 1 lakh followers. 
Achoo and Dabbu con their way in. Inside, they are mesmerized by the glitz and glamour of the event and the influencers. They meet some of their favourite influencers, who don’t give them any bhav. They ask around for chappal, but nobody has seen him around. Finally, at the end of the party, they are talking to one influencer and find out that this fat guy Ghoomti chappal. They corner him and ask him about sosal baba. Chappal is cornered and tries to send them away, but Achoo and Dabbu are adamant. 

Finally Chappal tells them that he will tell them where sosal baba is. He just needs time. Achoo and Dabbu are not convinced. They follow Ghoomti chappal to his house and force their way in. Once inside, they realize that all of ghoomti Chappal’s content is fake. They find a huge green screen set up in one room, where he makes all his videos. Pretending to be in places that he can’t afford. Achoo and DAbbu are heartbroken by this betrayal. Ghoomti chappal tells them to come back the next day, he will get them to meet Sosal baba. 

Achoo and DAbbu go away, but remember that they’ve left somthing at Ghoomti chappal’s home. They go back to realize that chappal is packing everything up and leaving. They confront him. Right then, Somebody opens the door to come in. It’s Sosal Baba! They have finally found him. They catch him and confront him to give them their money back. Sosal is reluctant at first and tells them that he has spent all of it. Achoo and Dabbu are not hearing it. THey want their money back at all costs or they will take Sosal to the police. 
Side note: Sosal baba’s real name is Sourabh Salil. He used to work at a marketing agency, getting fake likes and fake followers for brands. Making their videos go viral. He realized it is a great way to make money and started his own agency with Ghoomti chappal (real name: Ghulam Chawla). Initially it worked well and they had a few clients. But when sosial media clamped down on bots getting fake likes and followers. Their business tanked. Sosal realized that he can scam young people wanting to be influencers and started his scam business along with Ghoomti chappal. 

Sosal realizes that he has no way out so he tells them that he has a new job/scam. They can make a crore in a month. If Achoo and Dabbu help him with this, he will give them 10%. Achoo and Dabbu are reluctant to do it. Sosal realizes that Achoo and Dabbu are desperate and tells them that if they want their money this is the only way to do it. Otherwise they can send him to the police. They will not get their money back. Achoo asks what the plan is. Sosal tells them that the scam is to write and spread fake posts and blogs wrapped as news on social media and messaging services. They need to gather as many people against the protesters. All he needs from Achoo and Dabbu is to find 

Achoo and Dabbu are in a pinch. They tell Sosal that they will think about it. Achoo and Dabbu debate whether to go with Sosal’s deal or not. They don’t want to scam innocent people like themselves. They decide that they will confront Sosal and take him to the police. If they don’t get their money, then so be it. 

But, Danger’s goon finds them. They are not caught. To get out of the situation, Achoo tells them about the scheme. The goons don’t trust them. They call Danger Singh. Danger tell them that the money they owe will do nothing to solve his problems with nuclear. They need to get the two boys and give them to Nuclear khan.

---

DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/Narrative_Roma_Jogan_250Cr_Deal_and_Convention_Heist.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Prose scenes: Roma Jogan negotiating a 250-crore podcast license with helicopter clause, and Raj/Nawaz dragging her through a live convention sting.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Two good for nothing influencers, have to become super famous in two months to get into a prestigious creators conference.

1. Nawaz getting out of his room in stealth mode. It’s almost as if he is afraid of something, going into the battle field. Dodging imaginary mines and looking for snipers along the way. The audience sees it through a recoding someone is making on their mobile phone. It’s a prank (something falling on top of his head). Raj comes out from behind and reveals that this is another Pahadi Boyzzz successful prank. Nawaz and Raj then watch the video. Nawaz spots a problem in the video. They decide to do it again. THus revealing the fake nature of their “pranks” 

2. Raj is pretty happy with his follower numbers and views. He thinks this should be enough to get into the creators conference. Raj wants to talk to one of the organizers of the creators conference. He DMs him. But there is no response. Repeatedly. Raj and Nawaz decide to stalk him from his stories and bump into him “accidentally”. 

3. But this became tougher that they thought. The organizer would post that he is at a pub called Diesel. But when they reach, he is not there. Even the ambiance of the pace looks different. This would happen a lot. He would post a story about going to a place, but when Raj and Nawaz would reach, he is not there and the place looks a little different. They don’t get what is happening. One day they see a story that he is in france. They lose all hope. If he has gone that far, he must’ve gone for long. They are sad and dejected. They decide to go to a new pub, where they could get some content for their instagram. By chance the organizer is also there. When asked about the France post, he reveals that he went there a couple of years ago. He clicked so many pictures that he posts them every now and then. He doesn’t go out anymore. It’s too hectic. He mostly uses old pictures for posts. Sometimes he goes out when he is running out of content. 

4. They demand that they be a part of the creator’s conference. He tells them, tickets are available in a couple of months. THey can buy those and be a part of the creator’s conference. Raj: Ticket nahi chahiye. Hame part hona hai… Matlab. NAwaz: Matlab panel… Panel speaker. RAj: Haan. Aur Bina panel ka bhi agar ho sakta hai to wo bhi chalega. Nawaz: Keynote speaker. Raj: Haan wo bhi chalega. 
The organizer laughs. He tells them that to be in the panel for creators conference they need to be significant creators. Raj: Arey. To pichle 2 saal me 136 videos banaye hain hamne. trending posts alag. Baaki selfies, wanderlust, sab mila milu ke 1500 ke aas paas post hain hamare. 
Organizer tells him, this doesn’t matter. You need to get views as well. Nawaz: Views to bahut aate hain. Pichle waale video me 2 Lakh views the. Use pichle waale pe 2 Lakh 1 sau 18. Aur usse pichle waale pe 2 lakh 3 sau pachp…
Organizer tells them this is not enough. You need to 100 times more views than that. TO get on the panel they need to get at least 2 crore views regularly on all their videos. All their videos should go viral. 
Raj and Nawaz are a bit daunted. Organizer insults the content they have on their profile and tells them that for keynote, they will have to be Rohit Shetty, Karan Johar, Roma Jogan level. RAj and Nawaz absolutely adore Roma Jogan. She is the rockstar of podcasting. Her podcast is viewd by 20 crore people every month. Most of her listeners are from Small towns and villages. She speaks in Proper hindi. Smokes and drinks on her podcast. Has the most interesting people. Most celebrities vie to go on her podcast. But she is extremely selective about who she calls on her podcast. 
The organizer tells them that they have two months to become a viral influencer. Maybe they get lucky. Or they can try again in 2 4, 20 years and most likely never. 

5. Raj and Nawaz are depressed. They don’t know where to go from here. They drink. When drunk they meet a man who is also drunk. He is drinking because his mother died. Raj tells him their problem is bigger. They Have to go from 2 Lakh to 2 crore views in 2 months. *Comedy: Contrast the misery of mother dying with not being able to increase followers* At the end of the night. The stranger tells them that there are companies that you can pay to increase your followers. You can just google it. 


Raj and Nawaz decide to do their pranks on strangers for some reason. Their pranks are cringy at first. People start following them just to get a laugh at their expense. They think people are following because they like their content. They make more of such cringy videos. More and more people start following them. THey become a meme. 
Raj and Nawaz google the websites and find several. Most website offer 1 to 10 Lakh followers. And even this is quite expensive. Raj talks to his mom, who tells him to do bargaining from the websites. Raj thinks this is a good idea. THey find the address of the company and goes to talk to someone incharge. They tell him that they need at least 2 crore views on their videos. The person tells them that this is highly risky, because social media sites can find out and ban your account. The person tells him that it will be expensive. *Comic exchange where RAj and Nawaz are trying to haggle with the person, and the person is trying to tell them they can’t reduce the price.* Eventually they reach a deal where Raj and Nawaz will work for them for two months without any salary. But during this time they will get 2 crore views on each video as well as more followers. The person incharge agrees. 
There is a section of people who think that Raj and Nawaz are doing social commentary with their pranks. This is art. What Raj and Nawaz are doing is exposing the hypocrisy of the world and the corruption that lies within each of us. Certain sections start thinking of them as modern day philosophers. Gaining even more followers. Their videos start getting viral. Their comment section is divisive. Some people think they are useless clown, while the other think they are some sort of genius. COmparing them to the modern socrates. 
They start making videos. Which they make viral themselves. Whenever people criticized them. They would criticize them back. Talking about how this is art. Other people also start believing this is art. After that is starts growing organically. A section of people think this is art another section thinks it’s cringy. THere is always a fight in comment section. *Somebody advices them. It’s okay if they are abusing you. As long as they keep on abusing. Because when the abuse stops, is where the money stops. ABuse is engagement.” 




Roma Jogan. Talking to a world renowned psychologist. She dreams of buying 150 acres of land to build a house for herself and her 41 kids. Over the years she goes back to a small place called Kasar in Almora. She wants to buy a piece of land. Farm Pears and Peaches. She wants to have 4 dogs and 50 kids. She dreams of opening a school for her kids. *At some point during the conversation the guest jokes how will she get her guests to come all the way to Almora for these podcasts. This makes Jogan think for a bit.*  

We see her with those 41 kids. It’s clear that she loves them and they love her. They all call her Roma. We also see that the kids don’t have enough space in the house. Jogan promises them that soon they will move to a new house which will have a room for everyone. And loads of area to play. 

We find out in another scene that she is about to do a 100 crore deal with a music and podcasting app swar app. Jogan finds out that the land she wants to buy has other interested parties who are willing to pay 65 crore for the entire land. Jogan tells the owner, if he waits for a month, she will pay him 15 crores more than the other parties.  

Jogan’s producer tells her, “Agar 80 crore use de doge to bachega kya.” He tells Jogan to try and get 150 crores in the deal. She are going into a meeting with the swarapp executives. Jogan asks if she can smoke. THe executives tell her yes. JOgan rolls a joint in front of them and smokes it. The executives are reluctant about even paying 100 crore for 3 years. THey think that Jogan can’t walk away from this deal. Jogan ups the price and tells them that now she is demanding 250 crores for 3 years. The executives laugh at her. She takes a drag and tells them, “Mere average listeners har month 4 crore hain. Isme se 20 percent mere exclusive hote hi tumhare app par aa jayenge.” THe executive laugh at the 20% number. Jogan calmly tells her that 20% is a lower estimate. She has had a 30% conversion rate for all her endorsements. The executives shut up. Jogan then calmly explains that she is willing to shave down a further 5% for the overlap (Highly unlikely, because they only have only 5 Lakh paying subscribers right now which means their yearly subscription revenue is 60 crores) that some of her listeners might be on their app. So if they have to pay 100 bucks a month for subscription. Then this deal will make them 700+ crore a year and 2200 crore in 3 years. 250 crores is only gyarah dashamlav something something of that. 
The executives seem impressed. Some of them also seem high. But it looks like they like the deal. They tell Jogan that they will have to confer with their investors. But this looks like it will go ahead. Jogan tells them to add a helicopter in the contract. The executives are confused and ask why. Jogan tells them to bring my guests to Almora. 


***************

At some point in the story. RAj and Nawaz go to meet Jogan. BUt she ignores them. Then RAj shouts, hum KAsar se hain. Jogan stops and takes them in. She talks to them about KAsar. THey exchange notes. Talk about people they both know. THen RAj tells Jogan that he wants to come on her podcast. Jogan laughs at this. She tells them to fuck off. RAj is adamant and asks Jogan why can’t they be on the podcast. Jogan tells them that for them to come on her podcast they need to be some expert or artist, or should have a great story to tell the world. OR reveal corruption or something. Raj tells her that he knows about a troll farm. He knows how it works. Jogan tells him it is interesting. But it’s not enough. THey need to get more. IF they can have hard evidence. Videos, voice recording, pictures. She could maybe think about it. 
Raj and Nawaz will take this up and do a sting operation to get dirt on the troll farm. Make this funny. Put jokes and gags. 

******************

At the end. Roma Jogan is doing a live podcast in front of the fans. Raj and Nawaz manage to get some exclusive information about one particular minister who used the troll farm to spark riots during a protest. Jogan agrees to host them during the keynote address. Jogan will eat some kind of hallucinogenic drug right before the Convention. She has a stupid expression on her face and can’t move her body at all (She can hear everything and can communicate with her eyes?)Raj and Nawaz have to drag her around the conference without anyone seeing her. Maybe the Ministers goons are behind them. 
She then has to do the podcast with these two. THey will somehow get her sober enough to move a little. During the interview she will slur her words. Giggle strangely. 


******************

I also want interactions between RAj and Nawaz and other influencers. How will they come into the picture?

---

DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/Narrative_Tannu_Rikki_Khiladi_Kumar_Origin.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Rich character origin: Tannu's life philosophy modeled entirely on Akshay Kumar movies, cow riding, dam damage, and being exiled by the village.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Rajkumar and Nawaz are two friends in a small village. Their parents are farmers and well to do. Rajumar and Nawaz were 16 years old and had passed 10th when they both got smartphones as their dads promised. They loved their village, but there was nothing to do. 


are both addicted to social media. Rajkumar and Nawaz think they are social media influencers. The village has 900 people but Rajkumar has 1536 followers and Nawaz has 1200 followers. 

**************





Act 1:

1. Opening Image (1)
A village. Tannu(24) and Rikki (26). Social media obsessed. They always fail at whatever they are doing. THey try to do a stunt. But are both scared. Eventually, in attempting the stunt they destroy someone else’s property. The villagers are angry. THey’ve done shit like this before as well. Tannu and Rikki tell them that they are trying to make a viral video to become famous. 

2. Theme stated (5)
The villagers are fed up of Tannu and Rikki’s desire to become famous. The villagers tell them that you need to have talent to become famous. Rikki is worried that they have no talent and asks Tannu about it. Tannu tells him that the villagers are wrong. You don’t need talent to become famous. You can become famous overnight in todays world. 

3. Setup (1-10)
Tannu and Rikki’s family is fed up with them. They do nothing all day, but watch tik tok videos and social media.
Tannu and Rikki are delusional that they will make silly videos and become famous. After that money will pour in and they will just endorse brands and these companies will pay them money. 
Rikki is older but always follows Tannu’s lead. Tannu and Rikki were childhood friends. Rikki was given the responsibility as the elder to take care of tannu. But it was always Tannu who would assume the leadership position and get rikki in trouble. 
Once when they were 10-12, Tannu convinced Rikki to release Sushma aunty’s cow, so they could ride it around the village. They got caught and Rikki got the beating of a lifetime. Tannu could’ve run. But he stayed back and threw rocks at the Sushma aunty. He eventually got caught as well. They were both beaten, then taken to thier families and then beaten by their mothers as well. 
Tannu and Rikki became best friends. Rikki always knew Tannu is going to get him in trouble. But it will always be fun and he was sure Tannu would be right in the middle of it with him. 
Tannu’s mantra to making decisions is, WHAT WOULD KHILADI KUMAR DO? 
Tannu’s dad was an akshay kumar fan. He had all his movies on DVD and would watch the khiladi series, mohra, Akshay kumar fighting wrestlers, riding Airplanes. Tannu became an even bigger fan. His mantra to decide anything is what would khiladi kumar do? He sees a wall and instead of going around it, he would think, what would khiladi kumar do? And then try to jump over the wall. He was never able to do it. He wasn’t particularly athletic or anything. He always failed at these silly stunts, but he never stopped trying. 
As a kid also, he started having a reputation in his class of a spoilt child. It wasn’t Tannu’s fault. He always did what khiladi kumar would do? Homework over cricket? Khiladi kumar would go play cricket. Studying over TV? OF course khiladi kumar would watch TV. Teachers were fed up and parents didn’t want their kids to be friends with him. 
This is when Tannu met Rikki. He thought Rikki was funny, how he says yes to everything. Tannu never told Rikki about his mantra. He wanted Rikki to think he was cool. But he always got Rikki in trouble and always felt bad about it. But Rikki always kept coming back, like a protective big brother. Tannu always thought that because he is so stupid, Rikki is always around him to save him.  
Their families are fed up of the fact that even thought they are 24-26, they don’t work and earn a living. They are becoming a burden on their families. 
They are becoming a nuisance for the villagers, who are fed up of their stunts. They decide that it is time Tannu and Rikki are sent out to the city to make it on their own. 

4. Catalyst (12)
During one of their stunts. They accidentally damage the dam and put the entire village in danger. 
The villagers finally have lost patience with them and tell them to stop their stunts or they will kill someone one day. 
Tannu is also fed up of everybody complaining. He gives a big speech about how everyone is jealous of him. He tells them that he will go the city, become an influencer and get famous. Rikki is taken aback by this. The village is the only place he has known. Tannu convinces him that this is going to be great. No family nagging, no complains from the villagers. Like always, Rikki agrees. 

5. Debate (12-25)


6. Break into 2 (25)
7. B Story (30)
8. Fun and Games (30-55)
9. Midpoint (55)
10. Bad guys close in (55-75)
11. All is lost (75)
12. Dark night of the soul (75-85)
13. Break into three (85)
14. Finale (85-110)
15. Final Image (110)


*******************

DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/Outline_Core_Arc_Delhi_ViralHoax_and_SosalBaba.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Structural outline tracing Aachoo's poverty in Delhi, meeting Sosal Baba, the botched staged mugging, communal TV spin by Abhijeet Rane, and Pataka Guddi.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Acchoo is a podcaster and an aspiring influencer in Delhi. He lives in a small room, in a 3 room house with 2 other flatmates. He is broke and struggles to pay the bills and his share of the house rent. His roommates give him an ultimatum to pay up his previous dues or leave the house. They already have a replacement for him.
Acchoo has been trying to get sponsors for his social media and podcast. But he doesn’t even get a meeting with the brand managers of any companies. After he gets the ultimatum he decides to sneak his way into a meeting with a brand manager. The brand manager is somewhat sympathetic and tells Acchoo that he will need at least 100K followers to get a sponsorship from them. 

Acchoo is desperate and goes to a man called Sosal Baba. Sosal baba is known to increase followers and get engagement for celebrities and politicians. Acchoo makes a plea to sosal baba to get his followers to 1 lakh. 1 lakh is a small number for sosal baba. He is used to dealing with millions of people. But Acchoo is desperate, so he tells him to fork over 5 lakh rupees. Achoo doesn’t have that kind of money but wants the followers. Sosal baba gives him a deal. He asks Acchoo to make the content Sosal baba tells him to and he will get him more than a lakh followers on his social media. Acchoo agrees. 

Acchoo is handed over to another executive Achraj bhai who will be helping him. Achraj bhai gives him a spiel about what kind of content sells. “Fasadon, haadson, jungon me hi hum ek hote hain. Koi aafat na aaye to, koi apna nahi hota.” He tells Acchoo to either be a victim, or controversial. He has an army of people who will share his content everywhere. He will get likes and followers. 

Acchoo goes back to make controversial content. What he says is pretty innocuous. He makes videos with mild criticism or offense. Like irritating people on the road in the name of playing pranks. But, he doesn’t get any likes or new followers. Achraj bhai is under a lot of pressure from Sosal Baba already from not meeting his targets. He tells them to do something big. Dramatic. Filmen wagerah nahi dekhte kya. 

Dhenpu has an idea. He tells Acchoo that they should hire actors, who will pose and goons and Acchoo can beat them up. It works on heroines in movies. It will work for him too. Acchoo agrees. 
Acchoo aconvnices his two roommates to do it. After a lot of negotiations, they agree.

On the day itself. Acchoo’s roommates come in a disguise. One wearing a beard and the other a burkha. Acchoo is horrified to see the guy in a burkha. He tells them that he can’t beat up a woman. It will look bad on the video. The roommates are irritated by Acchoo’s unreasonable demands and beat him up instead. In the chaos of it all. One of the guy’s mustache falls off. He looks like a maulvi. The whole video is recorded. 

They show it to Achraj Bhai. Disappointed that they have failed. But Achraj tells them that this is gold. He tells them to post the video. Acchoo does. 

The video goes viral. Acchoo makes more videos. Talking about the trauma of the whole situation. Everyone is talking about it. Aacchoo is praised as brave. His followers increase to higher numbers. Almost 5 lakh. 

The video catches national attention. Abhijeet Rane, news anchor manipulates the narrative to make it a hindu muslim issue. 

Acchoo is called to studio to share his views. He has truly become famous as his follower count is still shooting up. HIs relatives are calling back. People in the street are recognizing him. His dreams are coming true. 

He is on TV, Ads. Making appearances on daily soap, saying his catch phrase.

He goes on the show love beach for an episode as a special guest. There he meets Priyadarshini. Who gives him a piece of her mind, how fake he has become.  
He tells her that she is fake as well and she has no right to tell him anything. 

*********************

Pataka Guddi urf Priyadarshini Singh is an aspiring actor and a social media influencer with 15 lakh followers. She has cultivated her audience with her dance videos, fashion and semi nude pictures posted with a thoughtful messages. She has been trying to get an acting role for years, but all she gets are roles in B grade movies with provocative scenes. She refuses to do them.
Although popular, she has been struggling to make content now. And her movie actor dream seems to be getting further away as she is getting in her late twenties. She has been making faking in most of her content. She has a green screen at her home where she pretends to be in places she can’t go anymore. Even her boyfriend is fake. All this comes out and she is revealed as fake. Her followers are going. Even brands that were working with her seem to distance themselves from her. Apart from that she is getting a lot of online hate. 
 To get back her standing she contacts sosal baba, who tells him it will take 20 lakh rupees to get her back on track. But, she will have to do everything he says. Priyadarshini agrees. 
He tells Priyadarshini to start posting semi nude photos of herself, like she used to do before. She is reluctant, as she thinks this will hamper her chances of getting an acting job. Sosal baba has an idea. A Reality show!
Sosal baba tells her that reality show is a great opportunity to get attention. And if she can create drama, she will get to showcase her acting skills as well. 

Priyadarshini has seen many nobodies go into reality shows and make it big. So, she decides to take part in a reality show called Beach love. She goe in as a wild card entry and straight away starts regaining her followers. She is convinced that, after the show is over, there will be many film offers for her. 
Priyadarshini goes on love beach. The other contestants don’t like her. The producers make her do sleazy stuff. Like seducing guys, wear short clothes, talk bitchy. SHe has no choice but to accept and do it. 

Her popularity is increasing. She finally gets a movie also. But it’s B grade movie. Everyone around her advices her to take the movie. But she is not sure. 
On the show, she is not doing so well. She wants to go, so she starts creating a scene and fights with other contestants. 


**********************

A generational politician, Puran Singh Rawat, from Dehradun has never won an election. He has never even gotten a ticket. He has always dreamt of becoming an MP and maybe become a minister one day. 

He meets the party president to plead his case. After a lot of effort, he gets a meeting but gets told that he is not going to be their party candidate as he’s not known. Very few people know him, noone talks about him. According to the party president, jo dikhta hai, wahi bikta hai. He tells Puran that there is still a month before they chose the candidates for elections. If Puran can do something to create noise and become known, then they will consider his name. 

Puran singh meets Sosal baba. Sosal baba tells him that it’s going to be expensive. Money is not an issue for Puran. He just wants to be known and talked about. Sosal baba tells him that he has a plan for him. He will have to follow it to the T. Puran Singh agrees. 

Puran singh Rawat is invited to the number 1 news show Nation already knows. Puran SIngh thinks he will be interviewed in a studio, but he is told that it will be through teleconference. As the show begins he realizes he is just one of the many panels on the screen. He only gets 10 seconds to speak, and his voice gets lost in the crowd. 
Sosal baba tells him that he has to go again, till he makes some kind of impact. Puran singh goes again and again, but he is not getting the air time he wants. 
Getting frustrated, during one of the debates, he says something derogatory. That starts a fight between him and another panel member. Soon this clip goes viral. Abhijeet Rane calls him on another show. Gives him more airtime. 
This time he has been coached by sosal baba to be as divisive as possible. 
Puran singh talks about hindu muslim issue. HIs vitriol gets a lot of hate online. Puran singh is devastated. But Sosal Baba tells him that this is what he wants. Puran singh did something right. Although there is a lot of hate, but everyone knows his name. And there is support from the other side too. 

Puran singh realizes that this is working. But he hates that he has to lie so much to gain this popularity. 

Puran singh tells sosal baba that he is done doing things his way. He wants to 


***********************

---


================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/Outline_Core_Arc_Viral_Decay_and_SweetSejal_Breakup.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Decay of initial viral dance from 200k to 12k followers; breakup with lifestyle influencer SweetSejal; roommate Dabboo seeking escape from corporate IT.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Acchoo is an influencer wannabe. 2 years ago one his idiotic dance video got viral. He gained thousands of followers overnight. He thought he had become famous. But his fame lasted for 2 seconds. For that little time, suddenly he got endorsements, he was being called for events, he made a bit of money. He left his job unceremoniously, closing all doors to ever go back. 

For a while people enjoyed, laughed, copied his viral dance, but soon they got bored. Acchoo had nothing to follow up his tiny success. How long can people really enjoy nonsense. He tried everything to keep his followers. He made skin care tutorials, travel videos, followed all the trends, took up all the viral challenges. Nothing worked. His followers slowly went away. At it’s peak, he had more than 2 lakh followers, but now he has 12,383… And decreasing further.

He desperately wants to become famous, because he believes they live such lavish, adventurous and unique lifestyle. All common people do is follow a pattern. Get up at the same time everyday, go to the office, come back home, eat dinner and sleep. Repeat the same thing over and over. While famous people live such fabulous lives. Viewed and loved by millions. He wants to do the same. 

He heard someone once say, fake it till you make it. He follows that principle to pretend he is what he wants to be. Rich and famous. He will go to any lengths to achieve that. Go to fancy restaurants and hotels to pretend he is staying there. 

He has a girlfriend, SweetSejal, who has over 2 lakh followers. SweetSejal, is a fashion and lifestyle influencer. Her goal is to become a heroine in Bollywood. Sejal initially dated Acchoo because she thought he had great prospects, but now, with his followers at 12000, she want to break up with him. 

Acchoo doesn’t have new content to post. He has been posting the same thing over and over for a while. He has to beg for likes from people he knows, how can he expect other people to like his content. He has to do more. He realizes he needs to do exciting things to become famous. 

Acchoo goes to an agency to ask for a collaboration and endorsements. The agency guy, his friend, tells him that he has too few followers for any collab. For him to work with them, he will need to have at least 2 lakh followers. He suggests Acchoo to become socially conscious. Try to post about the things going on in the country, take a stand. 

**********
Dabboo is Acchoo’s roommate. He works in an IT company. He is bored at his job. He finds Acchoo’s life really exciting. At office he is like a drone. Doing what he is told. Same kind of work again and again. He hangs with Acchoo to get a taste of the life he wants. 

**********

---


================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/Outline_Full_3Act_PR_Agency_and_Whistleblower_Climax.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Complete narrative spine: Aachoo rescuing the corrupt MP, joining Sosal Baba's agency, discovering the botnet farm, and the live whistleblower finale.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Fame is everything. It is the pinnacle of human existence. It is a rush to be known by thousands of people. To be adored. To be loved. If you’re famous, opportunities just fall on your feet. Fame opens closed doors. You get respect from people if you’re known by hundreds of thousands of people. 
And it’s not just for yourself. You can really make a difference in the world if so many people are listening to you. You can achieve good in the world. Ordinary people can’t do that. Even if they try to do something good, there is no one watching it. No one is getting inspired by it. What’s the point of that? If people don’t see what you’re doing then is there even a point to doing it? 

Opening:
Acchoo is a small time influencer. A few thousand followers of his vlogs. He pretends to have a great lifestyle. Eating fancy food, staying in expensive resorts, driving SUVs and stuff. He justifies it as giving aspiration to his followers. If they see him live this life, they will work hard to achieve it. 

He has other influencer friends, who have more followers than him, drive fancier cars and wear expensive clothes. Acchoo feels less-than with them and wants to become a bigger influencer than them. 
1. Sneaking his way into a hotel, clicking pictures and running away. 
2. Going to the same cafe because people recognize him there. 
3. Convincing people into doing what he wants
4. Fake gym shots. 
5. True support from family and friends. Large number. Some incident happened. He live broadcasts and 100s of people show up. 
6. Dissatisfied. He sees other influencer living their best life and his own life feels empty. He is faking everything while other people are actually living. 

Inciting incident:
He is in an event where an MP has come for inauguration. 
Protesters surround the area, demanding explanations from the MP for a scandal he is supposed to be involved in. The MP is blocked inside the event and can’t go out. 
Acchoo comes to his rescue convincing the protesters to let the MP go. 
MP tells one of his advisor, sosal baba, to talk to Acchoo. 
Sosal Baba approaches Acchoo and offers him a great opportunity. To really influence people, since he wants to be an influencer. 
Acchoo refuses, but sosal baba tells him that he needs people like him who can really make a difference in the country. 


Acchoo goes back home. His interaction with the protesters has been recorded on video and is getting viral. 

At first Acchoo is excited by this. But then he sees abuses being hurled at him in the comments. He is being heavily criticized. His followers start dropping. He panics and posts an apology video. The video is even more offensive and puts him in more and more trouble. He loses most of his followers.



First Act break:
Acchoo has no idea what to do. He panics. He DMs his influencer friends, but they don’t reply and block him. He has no other options left.  Then he remembers sosal baba’s offer. He gives sosal baba a call. Sosal baba asks Acchoo to meet him tomorrow. 

Acchoo meets sosal baba at an office. Sosal baba shows him around and tells him that this office that they are about to start, it has a studio to shoot videos, equipped with the best system. He tells Acchoo that this is a Communication and PR agency.
Sosal baba wants to hire Acchoo to work for him in the agency. Acchoo is reluctant and tells him, “Office me hi kaam karna hota to influencer thodi na banta” 
Sosal baba explains, “Tujhe content banane se koi nahi rok raha. You’ll still make content. You can make content in the studio. There is a whole production team to help you. Here you have a chance to influence lakhs, even crores of people. 
He introduces Acchoo to one of the employees. Acchoo recognizes him. He is an influencer called Bollybuff. 
BollyExplains that when he joined, he had 4000 followers. Now he has 2 lakh. 
Acchoo doesn’t understand what is the catch. Sosal baba tells him that there is no catch. THe videos he has to make will be for the clients of the agency. Like Bolly buff’s clients are Akshay Kumar and Bobby deol. So he promotes their movie, their brands. FOr example when there was a controversy about Akshay endorsing a gutkha brand, then Bolly buff made a series of videos defending him that went viral. You just have to think of it as, the client is the brand you are endorsing and make sponsored posts. 
Acchoo understands this. This is a win win situation for him. He agrees. 
Sosal baba is happy and shows him the IT department. IT’s the most important part of the office. These are the people that make your videos viral. 

2nd Act:

Sosal baba tells Acchoo that his first client is the MP that he helped at the inauguration. It is only logical that Achoo would talk about him after the whole controversy. 

Acchoo makes a video praising the MP and defending him from the protesters. Making shit up, and lying in the video. Sosal baba is impressed. 

Acchoo goes home. While watching TV, his phone starts buzzing. He thinks it’s a call. But it’s notification coming one after the other. His video has gone viral. Viewed by lakhs of people, and liked by thousands. Aacchoo Is ecstatic. 
He makes more videos. Defending other clients. Telling lies. 

His follower count is also increasing. He is being followed by lakhs of people. He is being called to collab with other big influencers. He moves into a better house and now can post real pictures of his house. 


But apart from the love he is getting from his followers, he is also getting a lot of hate. This starts bothering him. 

He shrugs it off as now he is being called on news network to give his opinion, as a youth thought leader. Acchoo is loving it. 

He finds out about one of the incidences he was involved in where the people got seriously hurt. This affects him. HE starts questioning what he has been doing. 

Midpoint:
He talks to sosal baba, who reveals his dark side and tells Acchoo to continue with the work he is doing. 

Acchoo is disturbed and visits the protesters against whom he spoke and other victims.

He then sees the IT department and how it works by inflating followers and likes by using fake accounts and bots. There is a whole sub division of junior IT associates, whose job it is to start arguments in comments and troll people. These trolls are dissatisfied with their lives and disillusioned. 


Acchoo realizes the folly of his ways.  

He hatches a plan. He decides to use the agency’s resources to make a series of videos about the plight of the victims and the whole manipulation game. 
The IT department, doesn’t see what the video is. So they will make it viral. More and more people will see the real issue. 
He makes the videos. IT causes even more chaos and riots.

2nd Act break:
Sosal baba realizes what Acchoo is doing and stops him. Sosal baba’s boss, Mr Jagani talks to Achoo. He gives him a spiel about the principles the world runs on. There is a queen bee, who everyone follows. Whatever they will do is determined by the queen bee. That is their role. To tell the bees of the world what to do. If people are not told what to do or think, then things like this would happen. So, we have to do our role so that they know what their role is. 
Acchoo doesn’t have a response. He has lived all his life on the same principles. How can he say otherwise. 

3rd Act: 

Mr Jagani gives Acchoo a chance. He needs to make another vlog and tell the world what he did was fake. If he doesn’t then, Acchoo will lose all his followers. It will not be a problem for someone with his resources to suspend his accounts permanently. His entire existence will be wiped clean from the internet. 

Acchoo doesn’t have a choice. He has to do what Jagani has said. 

Something makes Acchoo remember the words his mother said before. It’s not about influnce or following, it’s about trust. How many people trust you. Trust in you that is important. Because that is the hard thing to do. Even if 1 person trusts you. You are extremely lucky. 

Acchoo has many people, the victims and the protesters who trust him to do the right thing. He goes to the studio for a live broadcast on his mobile and reveals the entire plan and the agency. 
He runs around the office with Sosal Baba, Mr Jagani and other goons in tow. He records and broadcasts everything. Revealing the workings of the entire office. 
Sosal Baba finally catches hold of Acchoo. Right at the time, police arrives. Acchoo is relieved that the real culprits will be caught now. 
At the end we reveal that Acchoo is in jail, More famous than ever. 

The last shot of him going to the jail, with several people crowding him and clicking pictures from their phones. A slight smile on his face.

---

DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/Outline_Tannu_Rikki_Bhowali_and_IT_Cell_Tribalism.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Two village creators kicked out to Dehradun, interning in a political cyber-agency divided into warring partisan cubicles.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

2 young boys, wannabe influencers + are kicked out of their village because of their actions + they vow to become famous and go to the city + only to find out they have no talent or skills to do so. 


2 young boys, wannabe influencers + learn of an opportunity to make money + they leave for the city to become famous + but they enter a cutthroat world, that they weren’t ready for. 


2 young boys, who want to become influencers, and do crazy things to become famous + They are kicked out of their village after a stupid stunt goes wrong + They go to the city with a dream of becoming famous + only to find out that it takes more than talent to make it. 


************

Tannu and Rikki are friends, living in village Bhowali. They both live with their parents. They do nothing all day. They are Vloggers without too many followers. Their parents are fed up of them doing nothing but recording themselves all day. Tannu and RIkki tell them that they are going to become influencers and get famous. Rikki is scared and wonders whether they can become famous. It’s not like they have any talent. Tannu tells him that today you don’t need to be talented to become famous. 

Both, Tannu and Rikki’s parents plan to get rid of them. They give them some money, book them a ticket to Dehradun and throw them out. They tell them to find jobs or become influencers or whatever, but they need to fend for themselves. 

THEREFORE

Tannu and RIkki go to Dehradun. They find a PG to live in.  There are other kids around them, who are going to college and trying to get jobs. They try to do one of their videos. But fail and cause a lot of damage to the PG. The landlady warns them and tells thenm that she will throw them out if they continue this nonsense. Now they don’t know what to do. One of the kids in the PG tells them that a local agency is hiring influencers and will pay according to their following. This seems like a job that they are willing to do. 

Therefore

They go to the agency and interview for the influencer position. The interviewer sees that they only have 1200-1500 followers and laughs them off. He tells then that they won’t be hired with these many followers and will have to be at least at 10K.

But

 Tannu and Rikki are desperate and tell the interviewer that they are willing to do anything. Endorse any brand, say anything they want them to say. The interviewer tells them that he might have a job for them and calls one of his subordinates. He tells the subordinate to put them in the budding influencer IT Cell. 
The subordinate, Dinesh Negi, shows them around the office. There are cubicles, but they are segregated based on teams. A celebrity team (Farhan khan - big team, Reetika RAducone - medium team), Political team (Indian people party (IPP) - big team, Bhartiya RAshtriya conference (BRC) - big team, and Common man party (CMP) - one person) 
Dinesh gives them their cubicles and tells them that they will now be a part of CMP IT team. Their job will be to track all the conversations around them and reply back. Share articles about the party. 

Tannu and Rikki learn the tools of the trade. THey see how the place is working. There are rivalries between teams about their celebrity/political party. The rivalry turns to sabotage. Everyone is trying to get dirt on the other. Tannu realizes that you can lie online, say anything you want without any concequences. One time, Tannu gets too far. But his boss is impressed that he took initiative. The boss puts him in the CMP team. 

Tannu is happy, They lie about the other parties. Sudden;y everyone is talking about CMP. Tannu has done a great job. 

The local leaders of BRC and IPP come to the agency and complain to the owner. THe owner shows them that he is not favouring CMP. The team still has only 3 people. The leaders are not satisified and want Tannu on their own team. There is a fight to get tannu on their team.

---

DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/Outline_Vidya_SharonStoner_Unmasking_and_Kidnap.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Hustling to hit 50k followers for brand pay, getting unmasked by Vidya Sengupta (@JamesBong), and an accidental kidnapping getaway.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

1. Rajkumar and Nawaz are feuding on social media. Their followers enjoy seeing them fight. It’s like a joke for them. But in real life they are friends. 

2. Fe find out that they have been in the city for 11 months now. Their parents had given them 12 months to prove themselves as influencers and become famous. But now only a month is left and now they haven’t started making even a penny yet. 

3. They find out about this influencer marketing agency that hires influencers to endorse products and brands and pays them a monthlyh fee. They go to apply, but find out that they don’t have enough followers. The interviwer tells them taht for now they can endorse their brands for free, but as soon as they reach 50K followers, they will start getting paid. 

4. This is like a ray of hope for both of them. They start getting other influencers involved in their feud as well. CAlling out and abusing popular accounts with lakhs of followers. Unexpectedly, this works. Their followers start increasing. THeir follower count reaches 48k and 49k each in just a week. THey are sure that they will have 50K followers by the end of the month. 

5. A meme account, @JamesBong with millions of followers exposes them. This person always wears a sharon stone mask. THey had feuded with the account calling him a bot account and getting fake followers. In retaliation @JamesBong makes a compilation of all their videos, showing their fakeness and finally a short video of them hugging and hanging out with each other. 

6. Their followers are angry as they feel cheated. People start unfollowing them. Their follower count suddenly goes from alost 50K to 20K and is decreasing every hour. Their dreams shattered. They want revenge. They drink together and decide to confront @JamesBong. They find out that she is doing an event live from a location. 

7. In their drunken state they follow her and go to the location. After a lot of trouble they finally meet @Sharonstoner and confront her. They take the mask off and find out that @JamesBong is a girl Vidya Sengupta. 

8. As they are talking. A couple of goons kidnap them and take them away. They were after Vidya as she was trying to expose something sinister. 

9. With the help of RAjkumar and Nawaz, Vidya manages to run away with them. She doesn’t know who they were but now all three of them are in trouble. 

10. They go to Vidya’s friend, who is the leader of the indian Wing of the hacker group @Incognito. These guys wear joker masks and expose corporations and politicians.

---

DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/Research_Real_World_Influencer_Deceptions_and_Grifts.md

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** 32 documented real-world influencer scandals and flatmate testimonies: fake locations, green screens, sponsored proposals, and perineum sunning.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

1. Influencer tried to eat a live octopus on chinese social media. The octopus resist4ed. Stuck to her face and ripped off some skin. 

2. An influencer couple asked their followers to raise 10000 euros for their vacation. Got abused online for their “fund-raising”

3. An influencer couple. Scavenger hunt. The guy proposes at the end. People find out that it was all planned with advertisers. A marketing deck unearthed. 

4. Expecting free services like wedding photography in exchange for mentions in posts, networking opportunity, and shoutouts. 

5. Go to the lowest rate salon and lie that she developed a skin infection. 

6. Made a lifelike replica of their 3-4 month old baby and tried to sell it online. 

7. Made transphobic comments, then backtracked and falsely said she was trans.

8. A vlogger used beauty filters to show that she was a younger woman than she originally was. (difference 58-24)

9. Bella Thorne’s team tipped off paparazzi about her whereabouts. 

10. 13-14 year olds posting suggestive pictures. Pretends to be 18-19
A conversation with her mother. Character is friendly and charming and wins over the mother. The get talking about her life. She tells the character that she got married pretty young. When she was 24 and had the girl when she was 25. The character is surprised that she doesn’t look over 40 at all. The mother is surprised and asks why would I be over 40. THen realizes that her daughter is pretending to be 18. So she should just go along with it.

11. Landlord exposes influencer’s filthy apartment when she pretends to live a glamorous life.

12. Coming out as a transgender coz she is attracted to gay men. 

13. Instagrammer, Scam, giveaways. Louis vuitton bags. 

14. Reality stars would say just about anything as long as they were paid to be on television. They commented on truly ridiculous fabricated stories like the invention of time travel, leaving fridge doors open to combat climate change, and a charity that teaches underprivileged African children how to ski.

15. Metaphysical megan claimed, "perineum sunning", boosts energy, regulates harmones and strengthens organs.
She said aiming her butthole at the sky for five minutes per day regulated her circadian rhythm and increased creativity.

16. Lying about experiencing paranormal activity. Dust flying. Wind. Shapes running. 

17. Stepping on plants to get a good picture, with people trying to get her off it. 
18. Comparing her life to a rice worker. Wearing a bikini. INsensitive. Humble brag. You have to be in your underwear to think deep thoughts. 
19. Doing yoga on a memorial.
20. INstagrammer shames other influencers for destorying national parks.

21. THIRST TRAPS!!! For victims of xyz.

22. Tattoo artist influencer. 

23. WHAT IS YOUR BRAND???? happiness, well being, travel. Positivity

24. Key opinion leader.

25. We’re all just using each other for something. Only goes for events that further her business. 

26. Eating fake food
27 Badly in debt to pretend they are rich and buy followers. 
28 Clicking sexy pictures in a disaster area.
29 A middle aged influencer mom saying that her child gets the least likes in her family. 
30 Died taking selfies. Shot her boyfriend during a prank gone wrong. 
31 Has 2.5 million followers but sells 36 t shirts. 
32 Sells her own farts.
33 

**********************
Stories from flatmates. 

1. He would be home, cooking dinner. His instagram post would be dining in Greece. 
Early thirties. Few lakh followers. DOes life coaching. Makes no sense because parents pay rent and doesn't have a proper job. Gets loads of freebies (expensive food and drinks from various events and companies.)
Posts photos of him in fancy hotels. Location tagged as 'Home sweet home'
Or he is at home, cooking dinner in london, while his IG story is partying in greece.  

2. She would take me to things with free clothes. Free stuff, beauty products, tooth whitening strips and face masks. 
WAtching Tv and scrolling on the phone. Gym her only down time. 
She looks so polished, going to fancy dinners and events. In reality, she lives above a shady massage place and pay loads of rent per month. 
Borrowed my clothes and asked me to style her in them. WOuld send over bunch of pictures and ask me to choose captions for them. 
When they went out, she would be interested in getting the best pictures and the best locations. Not really interested in living it. 

3. Green hair. Splodges all over the flat. Glamor model on instagram, but her room is gross. Everything in her room, covered in grime. 
Around 3 lakh followers. No stable income. 
Made maazon wishlists for her fans to buy her things she needed. Even groceries. She even created a wishlist to get housewarming gifts. She got suitcase full of flavoured teas and coffee syrups. 
When she got into an accident, she crowdfunded her rent and bills. Didn't have the right bank accounts setup. 

4. Influencer with 20000 followers. Super high maintenance. Crazy likes on her post. Brocasting her best life. But she never really socialised with anyone. Either with roommates, or at college, or outside. 
Seemed lonely. She was sat in her room most of the time. 

5. Micro influencer. Treats it like a part time job. Practical money making thing that helps her career. 
Surprising how much she says no to brands. Choosing based on their own principles. 

6. Fast fashion influencer. Gets stuff for free. Got veneers done for free. People have offered her lip fillers. Out of the things she gets sent, she sells most of it. Unless she really likes it. Expensive shoes every month, she will have to wear the shoes, take a picture with them, post it and sell the shoes for whatever money. 
She is very generous. Gives things to even the lady who does her threading across the street. 
When we're going out. SHe has to take pictures. Puts her make up on. Instagram outfit and hair extensions. Even when going out with boyfriend, she has to plan ahead. 
WAry of her confidence. Self image problem. Doesn't turn off. 
If she posts something at 8, we can't do anything else for the next feww hours, because she is replying to all the commments for good engagement.

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis

> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Chapter 1: Vigyapan, Shohrat aur baaki Drugs

“Mai famous to hun, par abhi sabko batana baaki hai”
- Aaroh Chaurasia (Deleted)
- @AachaGuy1999

1. The camera explores the dingy bathroom, a cacophony of sounds. Dance music, Slow Music, Comedy clips with laughter tracks, Someone’s skincare routine (Lingers), sponsored post. The camera finally stops at Aaroh, sitting on the pot, scrolling through his phone. He keeps scrolling with a disinterested look. But he keeps scrolling. The speed of his scrolling increases. It seems like it will never stop. And we keep staring at him doing this mindless thing to the point of irritation. THe scrolling picks up speed. He doesn’t stop even for 3 seconds. It looks like he is in a trans. 
Door banging. “Kar Li potty?”
Aaroh’s trans is broken, he puts the phone on the basin and puts pressure on his sphincter. He shouts, “Ho Gaya. 5 second.”
He takes the handshower to clean his ass. 

The door opens. Aaroh nods to his roommate, Kungfu. Kungfu has a glass of protein shake in his hand. He telle Aaroh to cut some fruits for breakfast. Aaroh tells him okay. 
He goes to the kitchen. Picks one apple, two banana’s, an orange. His phone beeps. It’s a message on a whatsapp group. He opens it. It’s a video of a Anjali Baba teaching ways to get amazing shiny hair by natural means. You need to use Reetha, Sheekakayi, Neem and Cow urine and Anjali baba ji’s saliva. Mix it all up and wash your hair with it. But Thankfully, Anjali Baba has already done all the work for you. Presenting, Baba Anjali’s Thookesh Kranti Shampoo. He is about to click on the link. 
Kungfu comes back, “Tune ab tak fruits nahi kaate?”
“Research kar raha hun.”

2. Profound podcast. The ingenuity of it all. Manaviya avishkaron me sabse behtareen. Mardon ki chaddi. Aur elastic to kamaal ka avishkar hai. 

2. 
Acchoo goes to a protest. He gets hit. He becomes a hero. 

************

Chapter/Adhyay 1: Vigyapan Shohrat aur anya drugs

1. Acchoo’s father. Watching Television. Wants bournvita after watching the ad. 
Mummy! Mai doodh tabhi piyunga jab aap isme bournvita daalogi. 
A sequence of ads for colddrink, toys and candy. 
Mummy!
Mummy! 
Mummy!

DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/Scene_Draft_Interrogation_Jailbreak_and_Corpse_Heist.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Begging the cop to let him record his interrogation; attempted hacking of police CCTV; escaping jail with a fan-gangster; Inspector Dev Kanungo's live chase.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

The interrogation sequence: 
1. Acchoo is in interrogation for some reason. He wants to put his mobile to record as it is his first interrogation and he wants to share it with his fans. The cop doesn’t let him. 

2. After the interrogation, Acchoo wants to get his interrogation tape copy so he could post it on social media. He asks the cop for a copy. Cop laughs and sends him off. 

3. Acchoo breaks in the police station with an IT engineer and asks him to hack into the police computer and get a copy of his video. The IT engineer keeps telling him he can’t hack but Acchoo forces him to try. 

******

How do they reach this? What must’ve happened for them to be interrogated. A murder is what I was thinking. 


Acchoo can do anything for likes. 
 SI: Acchoo agrees to walk someone’s dog. Warned by Col. Uncle in B203 - not to let Tinku singh pee in the corridor. But he pees. To cover it up, they want to make it look like it was some other big dog. So Acchoo and Dabboo try to pee on the wall themselves by crouching down. 
Uncle sees pee and scolds them. They try to give excuses. 



Samachaar working with the current MLA Ramesheshwar Baba against the opposition hopeful, Sehgal Saab. 

*******

Acchoo is frustrated that they don’t do anything. If they don’t do anything, it will be impossible to generate content. Kab tak ye faaltu ke photos, videos banate rahenge. 
 
Get’s an opportunity to be at a huge party/Award event. Lot’s of celebrities are going to be there. He will get selfies with them. This will give him things to post for a whole month. 

Who gets killed? 
Someone both baba and sehgal would want something from. That someone gets killed. Who is that someone? Who is it? Election commissioner or election related individual. Some other influencer. Maybe some RAvish type figure. Some political influencer? THie person dies. 

INterrogation happens.
Attempted Hacking happened. 
Acchoo and the hacker in the CCTV footage. 
Police arrests them. 

Again interrogation happens. Acchoo is not permitted to use the camera. THey are put in lockup without access to their phones. 


************
At the end a scene with the dead body in a reality show. 
************


Achoo goes to jail for a few days. The jail seems scary, but he has a fan. A Gangster. He becomes friends with him. He has a phone in the jail. (scene where he asks the gangster if he intends to have sex with him. Sex to nahi karunga, par teri gaand zarur maarunga.)
Escapes jail with the gangster and his sidekicks, right dola, left dola. 

Offers sposorship for his gangster services. Kisi ko huul dena ho, pitwana ho ya tapkana ho. Contact The gangster. Kidnapping ke charges firauti ki percentage me liye jayenge. Acchoo makes a video promising to clear his name.
He has no idea how to do it. THe gangster might help? 

*****
At this point, Inspector Kanun enters. 
******

Ispector kanun trying to fugure out, where Acchoo will go. 

Acchoo goes somewhere to figure shit out. Not serious about it. Loving the fact that his followers are increasing. 
Inspector Kanun chases. 
Instagram live. Doston mai is samay police se bhag raha hun. 
Acchoo finds the body. Falls down from a height. The body saves him. 


Later Acchoo decides to give the body to the police as an anonymous tip. The police takes custody of the body. But it falls in Acchoo’s hand. 

Acchoo takes it to a friend’s place. 2 million followers. Stoner. Makes videos of himself crying on love songs. 

*****************

Acchoo what does he want. He wants millions of followers. He wants to just make content. Be famous. This, he believes, is the easiest job he can do. He doesn’t want to work hard. He sees so many people just posing in front of fancy things, wearing fancy clothes and thinks he can do the same. It doesn’t require too much effort.
So what does he do on a daily basis. 
He gets up. Goes to the bathroom. Bathes, puts on all sort of creams and stuff. Takes ages to get his hair right, then gets back into bed and does his morning wake up routine. 
He rushes out to the gym, where his friend is already there working out. He sprays water on himself and does several poses of working out. Then leaves without doing any actual work out. 
Goes to a cafe to do his weekly follower meetup. Nobody actually comes, but he makes it look like there are several people to see him there.
