DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/Scene_Draft_Juhi_Chawla_Dream_and_SmallTown_Grift.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Daydream of marrying Juhi Chawla for selfies; threatening local cafe owners with bad reviews; fake proposal with mother's borrowed ring.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

1. The dream. 
	A. Aaroh is going on his Scooty, when he sees a car broken down on the side of the road. He stops to investigate and finds Juhi Chawla. “Aila Juhi Chawla”. Juhi tells him that her car has broken down and she is stuck in the middle of nowhere. She asks if Aaroh would give him a lift. Aaroh first asks if he can take a selfie with her. Juhi agrees. She gets on the scooty, but Aaroh is still posting the selfie on the internet with hashtags and stuff. 
He drops Juhi at an ad film location. 
	B. Aaroh and Juhi out for a coffee. Where Juhi is pouring her heart out. Aaroh is taking picktures of the croissants. Juhi is slightly irritated. 
	C. Aaroh and Juhi at a KKR game. He meets Shahrukh and boasts about himself. During the match Juhi takes selfies with Shahrukh, celebrating KKR win. This irritates Aaroh
	D. Aaroh and Juhi meet outside, where Aaroh tells her that she should take selfies with only him. Juhi proposes that they get married. Aaroh has a lightbulb moment. He agrees. 
	E. Juhi and Aaroh are at a mandir, They are about to put varmalas on each other, but the camera guy is fucking up. Aaroh stops the ceremony to fix the camera. They are about to put varmalas again, but the lighting is wrong. He fixes the lighting. Now everything is set. They are about to put varmalas on each other when suddenly. 

2. The reality. 
Aaroh is being woken up by his father. He is disappointed that he was about to become famous and get loads of followers. His father doesn’t understand. He tells him to get a job. Aaroh tells him he already has a job.
	A. Aaroh washes his face. Applies cream and makeup. Then goes back to bed again. HE pretends to wakeup and on his camera he tells his 2000 followers that he has just woken up. He boasts about the glow he has in the morning because of his nightly routine, that he will post later. 

3. Aaroh’s life/ Day to Day
He is on his phone all day. His mother and father keep telling him to do some house work, but he tells them he is working. He keeps messaging big influencers for collab opportunities. He has sent hundreds of messages to people, but without any reply. 
He has a few friends that he has convinced that he is a big shot. In fact, for them, he is a big shot. These are young men with 100, 200 followers. So Aaroh with his following of 2000 people is a big deal for them. He believes that he will become an even bigger influencer. The only problem is his circumstances. He is in a small town with limited opportunities. If he was in a city like dehli or Bombay he would do much better. He tells people that he will go to the big city, when the time is right. In reality, he has no money to go anywhere. 

4. Sponsorship. 
Aaroh doesn’t have money. But he likes to go to restaurants and cafes. He often works out deals, where he gets free food and drinks in exchange for posting about the resturants online. In reality, he cheats. 
For example: He goes to a mountain view cafe and talks to the owner about a brand collab. The owner doesn’t need it. IN fact, he believes that Aaroh can’t possibly help his business. Aaroh tells him that he can show the owner the power of his influence. He posts online that he is at the cafe. Soon enough his friends show up. Telling the manager/owner that they have come here on Aaroh’s recommendation. THe manager is impressed, but still reluctant to offer free stuff. Aaroh finally threatens him. He tells him that if he can recommend the business, he can very easily destroy it also. He can cancel the restaurant. They have a showdown, where, Aaroh writes a post criticizing the cafe. HE tells the owner that he is going to post it online if he doesn’t get the free service. THe owner finally relents. 
Aaroh takes pictures and posts them online. Showing what a lavish lifestyle he is living. 

5. His detractors
He is often criticized by his relatives and neighbors. In response, he cancels them. You and I would think, what would that even achieve, but in his world, by canceling people like that, they in turn are criticized and shunned by people in their circle. 
For example: His chacha comes to his house, bringing a job opportunity for him, in Delhi. This irks Aaroh. Not only does he refuse the job, he even cancels his chacha online. His chacha now, is treated badly at home. He is refused ration at the local general store. Even when he goes to a restaurant, he gets poor service. 
Finally his chacha has to apologize to Aaroh. Of which Aaroh makes a video and posts online. 

6. The girlfriend. 
Aaroh is in a relationship with Stuti. He is planning to propose to her. He calls Stuti to the lovers point for a date. But hte whole thing is staged. Stuti starts the conversation about Aaroh and her future. Aaroh decides, this is the right time to propose. He has his friends ready to take a video of the whole thing. He goes down on one knee and proposes to Stuti. He shows her  a ring and tells her to marry him. But he fumbles. He is extremely irritated and throws a fit. He tells her that he has to do it again. Stuti looks at the ring and asks Aaroh how much did it cost. Aaroh tells her that it’s his mom’s ring. He has borrowed it only for this proposal video. He needs to return it to her. Stuti is shocked. Aaroh does another take but Stuti stops him. SHe breaks up with him telling him that he needs to grow up. This influencer business is not his career. It isn’t even his real self. She can’t be involved with someone who pretends to be someone else all the time. Does things to show other people. Aaroh is shocked. As Stuti is leaving, he stops her and tells her that it’s fine if she doesn’t want to marry him. She just has to say yes. He has done a lot of preparations for this Proposal video. HE has a great idea for a background score. All he needs from her is to say yes, right now, then she can break up with him. Stuti is flabbergasted and leaves.

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Opening scenes: stealing father's bike keys, temple selfie crowd, working shifts at Badnam Cafe, and evening drinks with Chauhan Ji.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Acchoo wakes up in the morning and with tiptoes goes to his father’s room and steal bike keys, tied to his naada. 

Acchoo goes to the gym, hoping to convince the gym owner to let them in. After a lot of negotiations on how many equipments he can touch, the owner agrees. Achoo changes into gym clothes and does a photoshoot with dumbbells. Posts it on his social media, with an inspiring caption. 

Golu devta, for followers. He gets flocked here. Lots of people crowd him. He feels good about that. Young girls and boys want to get their selfie clicked with him. This makes him feel good. He comes here especially for this adoration. 

Later, Acchoo and Dhenpu discussing what place to go for breakfast. They’ve already gone to every place in the entire district too many times already. Now no one will offer them free meals anymore. They decide to go to their usual adda Badnam cafe and convince GeeGro Saab to give them a free breakfast. 

At Badnam cafe, Achoo tries convincing Geegro. But Geegro is not ready to give them anything for free. So, Achhoo offers to work for a day at the cafe. Geegro agrees. 

Acchoo clicks a few breakfast pictures. #Naashta #Influencer life. Now Achoo has to work at the cafe. His friend Dhenpu refuses to help and makes fun of him all day. Achoo also tries to sneak a few pictures with other people’s food, but Geegro is keeping an eye on him. 
Now, because Achoo has posted pictures of himself on the social media, more people start coming in to the cafe, increasing Acchoo’s workload. 
Acchoo doesn’t want people to know that he is working at the cafe. He pretends to be hanging around, and making lame excuses for why he is bringing people food, and cleaning tables. At the end of the day, Achoo and Dhenpu get another meal. Achoo is so tired that he doesn’t even bother to click photos. 

When he comes home, expecting a scolding, his father is drinking. His father offers Achhoo a drink as well. They talk about Achoo’s future. His father asks what does he expect to do in the future. Dad gives him the freedom to choose what he wants to do. Even whatever this influencer thing is. He believes a person should be free to choose what they are going to do for the rest of their lives. And Achoo has to decide now. He can be this influencer, digital creator, but there needs to be a plan. It cant go on like this. His dad wants him to be active and go after what he wants. THe way he sees it, he has been here, in Almora for all his life, and it has


Acchoo and Dhenpu talk about the future. Acchoo wants to

================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/Scene_Script_Delhi_BunSamosa_and_Roommate_Friction.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Grounded scene draft: checking bank balance (Rs 3,199), cutting the food cart down to a Rs 52 bun samosa, and dodging roommates demanding rent.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Scene 1: Small room in a Delhi. 
A man about 33 is sitting on the bed looking at his phone. On his screen, his bank balance, 3,253 Rs. He closes the window and opens a food delivery app. Total cart vale: 264 Rs. He removes several items like omelette, mango shake and chicken puff. Only one item remains. Bun Samosa. Total cart value: 52 Rs. 

Scene 2: Flashback: 3 years ago.  
A party going on at a bar. It’s Aaroh’s Farewell party. We see he was in a corporate job, with several colleagues revealing their envy at Aaroh leaving the corporate job. They tell him they could do that as well. Aaroh laughs. He looks happy. We find out that Aaroh has already lined up a couple of regular gigs. Everybody has to start small, he says. The goal is to have my own album. The bill comes. It’s 12K. Everybody starts calculating how much they each owe. Aaroh magnanimously takes the bill and pays it. A notification comes, he has more than 10 lakh rupees in his account. 

Back to Scene 1:
Aaroh takes a breath and orders the bun samosa. He packs his guitar in a case. The name Aaroh Chauhan written on top.
He goes out of the room, into the living room looking at his bank balance now: 3,199 Rs. 

Living Room:
A man dressed in formals is sitting on the couch. Some mindless youtube video of a vlogger playing on the 29 inch LCD TV on the wall opposite the couch. The man, Mohit Bhandari, is scrolling on his phone. He sees Aaroh, urf Aachoo, as he calls him, and tells him, tu ghar pe hi hai na aj. Ek list daali hai fridge pe, le aana. Acchoo looks at his balance, looks at Mohit and says, meri meetings hain. Mohit is suspicious and asks kaunsi meetings. Aaroh lies. Mohit sighs and nods, thik hai mai Dilbagh ko bol dunga. Another roommate, Dilbagh Khan, comes out of his room and plops down on the couch next to Mohit, grabbing the remote from him and changing the video. Mohit is irritated and tells him that he was watching that for work. Dilbagh refuses. In their argument we find that Mohit works in a job in marketing and works with influencers. Mohit snatches the remote back and puts the previous video. Dilbagh shrugs. Acchoo is about to sit when Mohit reminds him that he has a meeting. Aachhoo remember the lie he has told and putting his guitar case on his shoulder, he leaves. As he is going Mohit calls him out again reminding him to pay him his share of the rent and the rest of the expenses for the month. Aaroh nods and leaves. 

Scene 2: The street in front of the building. 
Aaroh comes out of the building and takes a sigh of relief. He takes out tobacco and paper and rolls a cigarette. He lights it. Taking a drag, he looks at his phone and sees the food delivery guy almost reaching. He takes a couple of more drags and throws the cigarette down as the delivery guy comes. 
Beatboxing in the background. Aaroh takes the packet from the delivery and takes a left.

---


================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Aachoo_Online/Raw_Archive/Worldbuilding_Bilas_Gautam_and_Billionaire_Influence.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Aachoo Online]]
> **Core Focus:** Character profile of multibillionaire Bilas Gautam: software origins, foundation philanthropy, pharmaceutical influence, and co-opting creators.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

A billionaire is an influencer. Influences decisions on a global scale. They are controlling health policies, economic policies, and lives of not just countries but continents and the world. 
For example, Bill gates has a charity that supposedly help eradicate malaria in poorer countries. We assume that he is a philanthropic and charitable man. But what if he is not. Can you really trust an individual person to think about the group and other people before themselves. Because to reach that stage where you have made billions of dollars, you need to think about yourself. How can you profit from the market, the things you’ve invested in. What is the worth of YOUR time. You can’t get that rich without thinking about your own personal gain.
You’re giving vaccines and medicines to the poor. Everybody praises you. You’re the new messiah. The people you’re helping are happy. They think you’re the reincarnation of whatever god they worship. You’re a media darling. You come on television interviews around the world talking about the good you’re doing. 
Behind the scenes, you have a huge empire that is growing even quicker because of this. You have invested in companies that are providing the medicines that you’re buying and supplying. Because this the stock of the company goes higher. The PR it gets from your philanthropic activities and association sends the stock further flying. You’re even richer. Even though you’ve spent half your wealth towards charities.
You’ve now invested in health organizations around the world. Global organizations like WHO and doctors without borders. This helps you get the endorsements of these organizations that everyone trusts. There are some shady deals going on. You’re paying people off in addition to funding their organizations. They don’t oppose you and endorse your work and your opinion. 
If today you say a certain medicine is required, these organizations with agree with you. A loarge stock of these medicines will be ordered around the world. If you see sales dropping, you can get vaccines mandated. Changed government policies around the world so that it is compulsory for citizens to take those vaccines in order to live their lives. They can’t go to public places without it. They can’t take flights, trains and any public transports. You’ve made sure that the vaccines are ordered around the world on your say so. People don’t know this, but you’ve done this behind the scenes. Paying off WHO executives and state heads. In turn, you’ve gotten richer than anyone else in the world. 
This is TRUE INFLUENCE. Influencer with millions of followers and fans, celebrities, experts think they have influence and say over general public. They think their followers will do whatever you want them to do. THey think they have power over them. But do they really. It takes one old post, one slip of the tongue, one offended person to crush your influence. The billionaire’s influence is not like that. It’s real. He can sway countries, continent and the world to do whatever you want. 
But are you satisfied with all this? Of course not. This is a game for you now. You want to make as many points (money and wealth) that you possibly can. You want to win. You will do anything to win. Rules of the game don’t mean anything to you now. You make your own rules. If there are rules and laws that stop you from accumulating more points, you just disard them and pay the rulekeepers to make new rules. 
*******
Considering all this, our character, Bilas Gautam, is an Indian multibillionaire. He is in the top 5 richest people in the world. He wants to be at the top. This is a game for him. He wants to win and accumulate as many points he can. 
Bilas Gautam came from an upper middle class family. HIs father was a businessman, so Bilas got a good headstart. After graduating from London School of business, he started his own business, manufacturing and selling computer chips and hardware. After the company became successful, he started making software. His software became ubiquitous. One out of 4 people in the world have his software on their phones and computers. He retired when he was 50. He realized that the company ran itself, and he didn’t need to be there. HE can make more points working somewhere else. 
He announced that he will donate more than half of his wealth to the poor in his own country India, and around the world. He created a Gautam foundation, in which he put more than 50 billion dollars. He started with providing medicines and vaccines to developing nations for free. IN the meanwhile investing in the companies buying the vaccines from. He made loads of money doing that. But people didn’t really know that. 
Then the pandemic hit, Bilas had the opportunity of a lifetime. He bought even more shares in the pharma companies around the world and pressured them into developing the vaccines. When the vaccines were made he pressured WHO and heads of state to endorse the vaccines. When people protested and questioned them, he simply pressured the same leaders into making the vaccines compulsory. 
THe pandemic made him the 3rd richest person in the world.

He developed world class research facilities, where he employed the world’s experts to develop medicines and vaccines. The pandemic taught him one thing: He can’t rely on a global catastrophe to make money. He needs to be more proactive. He needs to find out what are people’s ailments. What are they likely to suffer from. 
He has an idea. He wants to develop a vaccine that can insert a microchip in people. This microchip can not only track people, but can be programmed to find out other things. It can find out people’s blood pressure, heart condition. By sending signals to different parts of the body, it can find out what will people get sick from. 
He has developed the vaccines and tried it out of humans as well. Although this was done secretly. He paid poor individuals to be a part of the trial. A lot of people died at first. But now slowly but surely he has perfected the vaccine. 

Now he is driving a propaganda campaign to get people injected witht he microchip. He has already tested the whole thing in Somalia. A lot of people died at first. But now he can see his microchip working perfectly. He is now bringing it to the rest of the world. 
He has not told anyone what this is. He has published research on a generalized vaccine against all viruses. Articles have been published in magazines and research journals. The scientist who questioned the papers have been paid off. USA and Europeean countries are willing to vaccinate their population. India has been a little difficult, but that has been taken care of. 

The only problem remaining is the people. They are divided. Half of the people around the world are willing to get the vaccine and endorse it. But the other half don’t. They question the need to get vaccinated with somehting they don’t really need. There are conspiracy theorists in these as well. But nobody takes them seriously. Even people who are against the vaccine. 

When the story starts, Bilas Gautam knows that he can’t execute his plan unless he gets people on his side. His plan is to buy influencers and endorse getting vaccinated, and discredit people who don’t by calling them anti vaxxers.

---

# Chakra: Master Story Bible

## 1. Premise & Characters
- **Protagonist:** Parikshit Roy (32), broke, indebted gambler fleeing Delhi.
- **Antagonists / Companions:** Rocky (in a saree) and Rambo (t-shirt and jeans), enforcers for trans mob boss Bhaiya Ji.
- **The Mystery:** Dhananjay Roy (grandfather) wills his 70-crore estate on condition of retrieving an heirloom from the unmapped Himalayan village of Chakra / Padmaview.
- **The Anomaly:** When Parikshit is shot by Rambo for trying to leave, he wakes up intact the next morning. Time resets every night at midnight; no one ages, and no one can leave.

DOCUMENT: Chakra_Time_Loop/Raw_Archive/Outline_3Act_Cursed_Village_TimeLoop_Full.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible]]
> **Core Focus:** Full 3-act outline: fleeing Bhaiya Ji and enforcers Rocky and Rambo, will reading in Shimla, and getting trapped in the reset loop of Chakra village.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

3 act structure
ACT 1: SETUP
Sequence 1 – Introduce Main Character/Status Quo
Parikshit is out of job and broke. He doesn’t even have money to pay his rent. He has gotten into gambling and owes a lot of money to some bad people. These people have repeatedly threatened him. He always tries to get away from them.
Two of the creditor’s goons, Rocky and Rambo, catch hold of him. Rocky and Rambo are two ferocious women, who are dangerous and feared by all. They take him to their boss, Bhaiya ji, a transgender man. Bhaiya ji tells them to beat him up and break his bones so that he can be put at some traffic signal to beg and pay Bhaiya ji back. 
Parikshit is scared and reveals that his grandfather died recently and he will inherit all his money and estate. He can pay Bhiaya ji back very soon. Bhiaya ji doesn’t believe him but Parikshit promises that if he lets him go to his hometown, he will pay him double the amount he owes. Bhaiya ji agrees and tells him that he will take 3 times the money. Parikshit has no choice. He accepts. Bhaiya ji has another condition. He tells Parikshit that he will send Rocky and Rambo with him so he doesn’t run away. 

Plot Point #1: Inciting Incident/Point of Attack
Parikshit goes back to Shimla for his grandfather’s tehrveen. Everyone is surprised to see him. They ask him why he didn’t come to his grandfather’s funeral. He was supposed to light his pyre. Parikshit gives some lame excuse. 
During the will reading, Parkshit finds out that his grandfather, Dhananjay, left him everything, but he has to get a family heirloom from a village deep in the mountains called Chakra. 
Parikshit refuses to dance to his grandfather’s tunes. As a child he had to do things as his grandfather told him. Live his life the way he wanted. Now even after his death, he is making his do his bidding. Rocky and Rambo threaten him to do it, otherwise they will be forced to kill him. Parikshit has to go with them. 

Sequence 2 – Set Predicament/Establish Main Tension
THe problem is, they can’t find the place. It’s not on any maps. They have some hazy directions from his grandfather. They try to find the place but can’t. Parikshit wants to give up but Rocky and Rambo push him to find the place. 

Plot Point #2: The Lock In 
THey finally find the place. They try to find the heirloom, but can’t. They look everywhere. The people in the village are behaving strangely. They ask all these weird questions about what’s happening in the world. Current prime minister, current affairs etc. They tell the villagers that they will leave the village as soon as they find what they came looking for. THe villagers laugh at them and behave strangely. Telling them, they can’t leave. Parikshit is frustrated and tells Rocky and Rambo that he wants to go back. He can’t find the heirloom. It’s not here. 
Rocky and Rambo had been given instructions by Bhaiya ji to kill Parikshit if he tries to run. Rambo shoots him. 
But he wakes up next day with a shock. He doesn’t understand what happened. THe village head explains to him that this village is cursed. Anyone who enters it cannot go out and time resets every night. SO, even though he was killed, he came back because the day reset to the previous day. 

ACT 2: OBSTACLES
Sequence 3 – First Obstacle/Raise the Stakes 
Parikshit doesn’t believe any of it. He is in shock and wants to leave as soon as possible. Even Rocky and Rambo agree with him. They want to leave as well. Their car is broken so they decide to walk out of the village. THey walk and walk for hours, until they see another village. THis one looks similar to the one they left. AS they approach the villagers, they realize it’s the same place. 
PArikshit freaks out and runs out again. But he comes back to the same place. The villagers tell him that it’s like this only in this village. Everyone here is trapped. They can enter, but they can never leave. 
Rocky, Rambo and Parikshit try various ways to get out of the place, but to no avail. Rocky and Rambo finally give up and tell PArikshit to give up too. 
Parikshit also gives up and starts living the village life. He makes friends with the villagers finding out about their life. One of the villagers becomes his friend and helps him deal with the fact that he is stuck here. Many many months, maybe years, pass while he is still there. No one ages. Rocky and Rambo have fun with the villagers. Bullying them, killing them with no consequence. ON small irritations like, didn’t like the sound of someone’s voice, someone asks too many questions. They know that these villagers will come back later. So they don’t care. 

Sequence 4 – Higher Obstacle
Parikshit finds out how the villagers live here. He gets to know their stories. He finds out that they were all brought by someone, who somehow left the place. There are people who have gotten used to this world. They don’t remember what it was like in the real world. Some people desperately want to get out. THey have children, wives, husbands, family and friends that they need to get to. Even thought they know it’s been several years since they were stuck in this place. Some are depressed. THey don’t do anything and sit around moping. Have no hope things will ever change. Then there are some people who don’t want to get out. They think that god has sent them here. But even among them, there is no consensus. Some think this is swarg, others think this is hell. 

Laziness sets in. Parikshit goes on his old ways. Rocky and RA
mbo are fed up of this place now. They both miss Bhaiyaji. Rambo misses her mother in law and her home. They ask Parikshit his grandfather must’ve come to this place and left. They want to know if he has mentioned any way in which they could leave too. Parikshit tells them that he has nothing left in the outside world. He was a loser there. People like these two were after his life. Nobody valued him. Here at least there are people who like him. RAmbo threatens him, but he just tells them they are free to kill him. He’ll just come back the next day. 
Rambo takes up the challenge and shoots him. HE wakes up the next day. She shoots him again. She stabs him the next day. She keeps on killing him in different ways. Parikshit begs her not to kill him. Even though he wakes up from his death everyday, the act of dying is extremely painful. RAmbo tells him to take her deal and find a way through. She will make sure that Bhaiyaji doesn’t kill him. Parikshit agrees, in return, RAmbo will not kill him either in the village or outside. Rambo agrees as well. 

Plot Point #3: First Culmination
Parikshit tries to get out. Formulates a plan. 

Sequence 5 – Subplot/Rising Action
Parikshit finally finds a way to get out. 

Sequence 6 – Highest obstacle
The way to get out of the place is by doing something horrible. He doesn’t want to do it. Because of his reluctance he gets stuck and cannot return even to the village. The way to get out is take the new born baby from a woman who is pregnant and 

Plot Point #4: Main Culmination
Rocky and Rambo go to save him. After a lot of struggle they finally get him out. 


ACT 3: RESOLUTION
Sequence 7 – New Tension
Now that they are out. They have to leave the village. How would they do it?

Plot Point #5: Twist


Sequence 8 – Resolution



**************

Parikshit is lazy, unmotivated and broke. He has a lot of debt on him that he can’t pay back. He is in trouble with the people who have loaned him the money. They are after him and mean him harm. He will

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible]]
> **Core Focus:** Parikshit fleeing over the 4th-floor balcony with his weed bag; saree-clad Rocky and Rambo pulling him up and driving him to his grandfather's will reading in Rishikesh.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Father name: Dhananjaya Roy/Panda


Who is the protagonist?
Parikshit Roy is a 32 year old man. He grew up in a middle class family

What is the situation? What is the hero’s personal condition at the beginning? How will that condition be changed, for better or worse, by the hero himself or by the antagonistic force?
What is the protagonist’s objective? At the beginning, what does the hero want? What moral (or immoral) choices will she have to make in her attempt to gain that objective?
Who is the opponent? Who or what stands in the way of the hero achieving his objective?
What will be the disaster? What misfortune will befall the hero as the result of her attempts to achieve her objective?
What’s the conflict? What conflict will result from the hero’s reaction to the disaster? And what is the logical flow of cause and effect that will allow this conflict to continue throughout the story?


*************

A child is sitting in an full auditorium. He looks excited. The principal announces an award for Parikshit. Parikshit gets up and walks towards the stage. The elder man is his grandfather. He happily applauds. The principal compliments Parikshit saying how well he has done and he expects him to do great things in his life. 

Parikshit wakes up as we hear a loud banging on the door. He is afraid as he gets up and gathers his clothes. He sees a half smoked joint. He lights it and smokes while preparing to leave. The banging gets progressively louder. Parikshit picks up a bag and shoves clothes in it as he shouts, “Aa raha hun. Nanga hun. Kapde pehen raha hun” 
The banging turns into someone trying to break the door. Parikshit picks up his wallet and his phone. He goes over the balcony railings. He looks down. It’s the 4rth floor. He looks up and tries to get down on the downstairs balcony. He has forgotten something important. He comes back up. Looks at the door. It’s almost come off. He struggles with the decision whether to go in or not. HE finally goes in. He runs towards the bed and finds a bag of Weed under it. He picks it up as the door breaks. HE leaps towards the balcony. With a joint on his lips, he smiles at the two shadows barging in and with one hand salutes goodbye. But his hand slips and he falls. 
Two women barge in. Rambo is in a tshirt and jeans and Rocky in a saari. THey are disappointed as they look over the balcony. PArikshit is hanging on the ledge. The woman in the saaree offers to help. THe other woman tells her to let him fall. They’ve come here to beat him up anyway. They were told to break a few bones so, if he falls, he will break a few bones. The other one thinks that he may die from this height. THey disagree on whether Parikshit will die falling from this height. Eventually, they come to a conclusion that they can’t take a chance and pull him up with a saaree pallu. As soon as they pull him back up. THey start beating him. 
WE find out that Parikshit owes 20 lakh rupees to their boss “Bhaiya ji”. Parikshit argues that he owed money to Didi. Who is this new Bhaiya ji? Rocky slaps him and tells him that Didi is now Bhaiya ji. (Didi janam to aurat hui thi. PAr unko sareer me, mard jaisa lagta tha. Agar unko lagta tha to hum kaun hote hain rokne waale)
PArikshit is told to fork out the money or they will break his bones. They discuss which bone to break. Parikshit tells them that he has the money. He just needs a little more time to get it. He will pay them 30 instead of 20 if they give him another week. Rambo calls Bhaiya ji and tells him the deal. He tells them that his grandfather died a couple of weeks ago. And since his tehraveen has happened, his lawyer will read his will. His grandfather is rich and would have left him a lot of money. HE can pay them 30 lakh instead of 20. 
Bhaiya ji agrees, but he insists that ROcky and Rambo should accompany him. Rocky is disappointed as she will have to explain all this to her saas. She beats Parikshit a little more as he has put her in this mess. 


Parikshit reaches his hometown, Rishikesh, with Rocky and Rambo. He goes to his grandfather’s massive house. There are a few relatives still there. They ask why he didn’t come for his grandfather’s funeral. We find out that Parikshit didn’t come to see his grandfather before or after he died. 
In flashbacks we see that Parikshit and his grandfather Dhananjay had a fight. Parikshit desperately asks his grandfather to help him. Dhananjay refuses and sends him away. 
THere are pictures of Dhananjay and Parikshit on the walls around the house. They are mostly when he was a kid in school or college. THere are trophies sitting in a display with PArikshit’s name on it. Dhananjay loved him. But it looks like they had a falling out. Parikshit hadn’t talked to his grandfather in months. 

The will is being read. Rocky and Rambo are sitting next to Parikshit. They are excited. A painting willed to a duur ki bua. A sculpture willed to a duur ke chachaji. PArikshit is given an old car. His grandfather’s first car. Now the estate worth 20 crores and the rest of the wealth of 50 crores is given to Parikshit. Rockya nd RAmbo are impressed. But, the lawyer adds, Parikshit will have to get something from a village called Padmaview. Parikshit is disappointed, but Rocky and Rambo cheer him up and then threaten him to convince him to go to the village. 

The village is not on any map or anything. They have to ask people, and ride around. Hours become days, days become weeks. Rambo calls Bhaiya ji, who tells them to bring him back. They will sell his car and break his bones and make him beg on the streets till he recovers the money. Right then they find the village. Padma view. 

It’s a lonely road as they drive towards the village. There is no one on the way. They reach a pllace where a man stops them. The man takes them to a small hut and interviews them. Asking them questions like - why do you want to go? Who are you? What is your background etc. At the end of it he rejects them and asks them to leave. Not everyone can enter Padma view. 
Rambo points a gun at the man. He has to let them enter. 
As they enter, another man tries to stop them but they go past him. Some of the villagers have gathered and scold them for coming to their village. It was so irresponsible of them. Parikshit tells them that they have come here for something very important and they won’t leave without taking it. 

****
Parikshit finds Dhananjay’s house. He looks around, but can’t find anything. The house is organized. All his grandfather’s things are in place. Rocky and Rambo help him, but they can’t find the missing artifact. Parikshit wants to give up and go back. Rocky and Rambo stop him and tell him that they aren’t going anywhere without getting the artifact. They decide to spend the night there. 
Rocky tries to call Bhaiyaji, but she isn’t getting any signals. 
Parikshit can’t sleep.

DOCUMENT: Daddu_Damdar/00_Story_Bible.md
================================================================================

# Daddu Damdar: Master Story Bible

## 1. Premise & Thematic Engine
- **Genre:** Superhero Action-Comedy / Domestic Drama (8 Episodes x 30 mins).
- **Setting:** Dharampur, Uttarakhand (a tight-knit hill town near Dehradun).
- **The Core Paradox:** An amnesiac 80-year-old homeless man whose body is failing him possesses ancient superhuman strength. A 16-year-old working-class girl whose school life is built on elaborate class lies possesses razor-sharp tactical survival instincts.
- **The Central Moral Tension:** 
  - *Survival vs. Righteousness:* Piddu wants to use Daddu's power to earn money, dress well, and secure promotions for her father. Daddu insists that wielding absolute power requires absolute selflessness—no bribes, no stolen gang cash, no shortcuts.
  - *The Succession Curse:* The power is ancestral. It cannot simply die; if not transferred, it keeps the aging host trapped in deteriorating physical agony.

## 2. Power Mechanics & Succession Rules
- **Abilities:** Kinetic redirection, shockwave dissipation, bullet-resistant tissue, burst super-speed (visual blur), and superhuman leaps (flight has deteriorated due to aging joints).
- **The Activation Rule:** Powers remain locked in ordinary life. Daddu cannot pass a gym fitness test or lift a 5kg dumbbell. His abilities unlock exclusively when Piddu is under lethal threat.
- **The Auditory Compass:** For 25 years, a psychic shriek/ringing in Daddu's ear has forced him from city to city, directing him toward crisis events. Disobeying causes debilitating headaches.
- **The 30-Minute Synthetic Drug:** Jass Dhillon's corporate empire synthesizes performance-enhancing drugs from Daddu's harvested blood, granting ordinary mercenaries superhuman power for 20 to 30 minutes before metabolic crash.
- **The Lineage Transfer:** The ancestral power must be willingly transferred. In the climax, Daddu saves a fatally wounded Piddu by bequeathing the power to her, freeing himself to live his final years as a normal, peaceful human.

## 3. Alternate Concept Stubs & Mythic Seeds (From Earlier Drafts)
- **The 9th Chiranjeevi Myth:** An ancient immortal granted power during Kaliyuga whose memories and boons were cursed and sealed into a gem by the demon Manoj in 1947.
- **The Chitragupta Library:** A comic celestial archive where Chitragupta has spent 2,000 years practicing stand-up comedy routines while cataloging human karmic records.
- **The Age-Reversing Devi:** An injured woman who goes comatose in an Uttarakhand village, aging backward over 38 years until becoming an infant who re-awakens.


================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Daddu_Damdar/01_Character_Roster.md
================================================================================

# Character Roster

### 1. Daddu (The Forgotten Weapon)
- **Age:** ~75–80.
- **Disposition:** Unflappable, gentle, deeply pragmatic ("chalta hai", "adjust kar lenge"). Lacks moral rigidity in survival, but strictly rejects using superpowers for personal gain.
- **Vagrancy Rules:** Developed strict survival rules over 25 years of street life (Rule #1: Always secure food first; Rule #2: Never steal unless past 9 PM with no food).
- **Equipment:** Tattered tweed coat worn as a cape, plastic grocery jhola containing a binder of 50-year-old newspaper clippings detailing forgotten miracles, aluminum walker.

### 2. Padmalakshmi "Piddu" (Akshi) Gupta
- **Age:** 16 (Class 11/12).
- **Physicality:** 5'2", 45–52 kg, wiry, athletic, roller-skater, martial arts trained (karate).
- **Style:** Thrift-punk / DIY street fashion. Cuts her father's 96kg constable pants into oversized genie shorts with police brass buckles; mirrors tears across shirts with needlework to create high-fashion illusions.
- **Class Conflict & Hustle:** Sells weed to schoolmates and college hostellers (earning 10k/month); sells Netflix passwords and forges signatures to maintain the fiction that her father is an elite IPS officer and her mother an interior designer.
- **Ambition:** Started investing in mutual funds at age 8 with the singular goal of hiring scientists to build her a superhero suit.

### 3. Father (Hawaldar Gupta)
- **Role:** Low-level police constable.
- **Personality:** 6'3", 96 kg, gentle giant. Former decorated hero cop demoted after confronting a corrupt superior. Incapable of taking bribes; often ends up giving his own money to distressed citizens. Dream: retire quietly and open a village general store.

### 4. Mother
- **Role:** Domestic maid / nanny in wealthy households.
- **Personality:** The household disciplinarian. Constant friction with her husband for not taking bribes like other constables; saved 2 lakh rupees painstakingly for Piddu's education.

### 5. Jass Dhillon ("Damdam" Syndicate)
- **Role:** Billionaire pharma tycoon.
- **History:** Captured Daddu 25 years ago, running inhumane clinical extractions on his blood to launch a corporate sports-science empire. Uses autonomous surveillance drones and 30-minute serum squads to hunt Daddu.

### 6. Baba Damdam
- **Role:** Ascetic yogic physical trainer / preacher.
- **Platform:** Delivers moral discourses ("pravachan") while performing high-intensity calisthenics and power feats on stage before thousands of young disciples.
- **Conflict:** Daddu's chosen apprentice from 25 years ago. Believes giving the power to Piddu is a curse that will ruin her life; wants Daddu to let the power die with him while his ashram uses synthetic Ayurvedic strength enhancers to enforce justice.

### 7. Vibhor Bora
- **Role:** Football captain, school heartthrob; wealthy, sweet, dumb. Intimidated by Piddu’s fierce attitude, but fascinated by her connection to street justice.


================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Daddu_Damdar/02_Episode_Beat_Sheet.md
================================================================================

# 8-Episode Series Arc

## Episode 1: The Chaperone & The Bear Enclosure
- **Cold Open:** Gandhi Market explosion. A runaway serum subject destabilizes, exhibits kinetic havoc, and detonates. A corporate drone films the scene. In the Prime Minister's intelligence office, officials realize someone is marketing superhuman suicide bombers to international cartels.
- **A-Story:** Piddu navigates her double life at school. She protects the homeless man (Daddu) from street thugs and shares her tiffin under his peepal tree. Facing mandatory chaperone rules for the school zoo trip, she gives Daddu a haircut and suit, passing him off as her retired grandfather.
- **Climax:** At the zoo, Daddu wanders into the bear enclosure during a fugue state after crushing the steel lock. Piddu steps in to extract him; when the bear attacks, Daddu moves in a sonic blur, deflecting the beast and jumping them outside. Vibhor sees the rescue and hails Piddu as a hero.

## Episode 2: The Glitch & The Loan Shark Raid
- **A-Story:** Piddu tests Daddu’s abilities in a gym. Total failure—he cannot press 5kg. Piddu deduces that his power only awakens when she is in mortal danger.
- **The Moral Rift:** Vibhor brings a classmate whose father is being brutalized by loan shark Anil Rai. Piddu manipulates Daddu into confronting Rai’s gang. Daddu’s powers unlock when the goons attack Piddu; he dismantles the gang and ties them up. 
- **The Theft:** Piddu swipes ₹5,000 from the loan shark's safe to buy a new dress and treats Daddu to ice cream. Daddu is furious: *"Power comes with responsibility; you cannot steal."* Her constable father discovers the tied goons and receives credit.

## Episode 3: The Miracle of Dharampur
- Daddu reveals his plastic jhola containing a binder of 50-year-old newspaper clippings reporting unexplainable rescues. He takes Piddu to a town library where an archivist quietly preserves his legend.
- Daddu and Piddu execute vigilante busts against drug cartels and local mafia, leaving criminals tied for her father. The press dubs it the "Miracle of Dharampur." Her father is promoted to Head Constable.

## Episode 4: The Fall & The Drone Ambush
- The bust wave backfires. Criminals insist they were beaten by a little schoolgirl and an old man with a walker.
- Police internal affairs suspects Hawaldar Gupta of running an extortion ring and fabricating arrests. Her father is arrested for corruption and conspiracy; Piddu is detained.
- Jass Dhillon deploys an elite mercenary unit dosed with the 30-minute synthetic serum. The mercenaries engage Daddu in a brutal slum alley fight. Daddu holds his own but is overwhelmed as his dementia flares. Piddu uses smoke bombs and local terrain to stage an escape.

## Episode 5: The Road to Damdar
- On the run from both the police and Dhillon’s private security, Piddu and Daddu trace the locations from the old newspaper binder across Uttarakhand hill towns.
- They meet elderly villagers who remember being saved by Daddu decades ago. They discover the tragedy of 25 years ago: Daddu was ambushed and vanished during a high-profile rescue.

## Episode 6: The Ashram of Baba Damdam
- Captured in the mountains by ascetic enforcers, they are brought to the high-altitude ashram of Baba Damdam.
- Revelation: Damdam was Daddu's chosen apprentice 25 years ago. Damdam reveals his legion of trained disciples, demanding Daddu inspect them to pick an heir.
- The Ideological Clash: Damdam warns Daddu that passing the mantle to Piddu will destroy her childhood. He reveals his Ayurvedic strength enhancers and urges Daddu to let the ancestral power expire.
- Breach: Dhillon’s air-mobile strike team assaults the ashram using heavy artillery and combat drones. Daddu is tranquilized and captured.

## Episode 7: Infiltrating the Extraction Lab
- Baba Damdam and Piddu forge an uneasy alliance. Damdam provides tactical support; Piddu uses her small frame and stealth to infiltrate Dhillon’s fortified pharmaceutical facility.
- Piddu discovers the medical wing: hundreds of synthetic vials synthesized from Daddu’s spinal fluids. She frees Daddu from an automated life-support chair, but the facility locks down.

## Episode 8: The Lineage Transferred
- The final showdown in the corporate lab. Baba Damdam and his senior students engage Dhillon's 30-minute super-soldiers.
- While Daddu battles Dhillon, Piddu slips back into the core incinerator to destroy all master cultures and formulas so the weaponization dies forever. A blast collapses the chamber, fatally wounding her.
- Daddu pulls her from the rubble. Refusing to let his surrogate daughter die, he initiates the ancient generational transfer, passing the full ancestral power into Piddu.
- **Resolution:** Piddu awakens with superhuman vitality. Daddu, now a fully ordinary, peaceful 80-year-old man whose mind is finally quiet, sits comfortably on a garden bench as Piddu takes up the mantle to protect the streets.


================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Daddu_Damdar/Raw_Archive/AltBranch_Folklore_Age_Reversing_Devi_Kadambari.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis: Alternate Lore
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]
> **Core Focus:** Folklore alternate draft: a comatose woman in an Uttarakhand village who regresses in age over 38 years into a newborn named Kaddu.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

She was a strange girl. She was 12-years-old but behaved like she was 40. Ordering everyone around. Scolding people when they weren’t doing things how they were supposed to be done. She lived in an orphanage in a small town in the hills of Uttarakhand. The town was isolated by the other towns and villages, separated by a small bridge. The bridge was narrow and only one car could pass at a time. The girl was called Piddu wielded such power over everyone that the Manager and the staff would need her permission to do things. Even in the town, she was known by everyone. Residents, shopkeepers, labourers. Everyone was also very protective about her.
But she had a secret. She never aged. She had been 12 for the past 50 years. She was brought to this village 50 years ago by an older man. He was dying and carried a woman in his arms. The woman was wounded as well. The woman seemed to be about, 30 years old at the time. She was handed over to the village head. The woman remained in coma for 18 years. No doctors or medicines really worked on her. She didn’t need water or food. All she did was slept. 
Her injuries, though severe, did not kill her. They slowly got better on their own. But, it came at a cost. Her body started changing. She was getting younger and younger every year. Her physical body was changing. Her limbs getting smaller and smaller. The village head, Mananiya Sri Bisnu Chand Joshi, showed her to the district doctor, who was his tai’s nanad’s (sister in law) son. The doctor tried for a month, but couldn’t help the girl. He advised Bishnu to send her to a hospital in the city. Like Dehradun, maybe even Delhi. The doctors there could test her and find more about the girl. 
Bisnu was a simple man. He decided to take the doctor’s advice. The day the girl was supposed to be taken was October the 12th. The winter had started to set in. Temperatures were around 1-2 degrees. It was sunny. Bisnu had arranged a van from the nearby town. The bus already had a flat tire on the way over to Bisnu’s house. It had started raining heavily. The cold rain was hitting the body like cold ice needles. Almost piercing the skin. When it was time to take the girl to the van, Bisnu couldn’t pick her up. It was as if the girl had suddenly gained immense weight. Bisnu asked the driver for help, then Bhuppi, gyani and Jeetu. An hour later there were 20 people trying to lift her. But they couldn’t move the khatiya. They tied the khatiya to a rope attached it to the van, but the van couldn’t move. Bisnu took it as a sign that there was some godlike power at work here and let the girl remain in his house. 
THe girl was given a room. SHe required nothing. No food, no water. Just a khatiya and a blanket. The room became like a shrine. The villagers would come and light dhoop and agarbatti. Leave flowers outside the room as a sign of respect for what they thought was a Devi. Bisnu would come and visit the girl everyday. He took care of her for 15 long years. He didn’t have to do much. Then he died of an illness. His son, Mananiya Sri Jainendra Joshi, took over the care of the woman, whose body was now of a 20 year old girl. Jainendra himself was about 38. He was younger than the girl when she was brought to his house. He was upset with her because she took his room. But eventually he felt pity for the girl. Her age was reducing every year. No one knew what was going to happen to her. If this kept on going she would become a baby one day. What would happen after that? No one knew. 
The villagers kept it a secret among themselves. They didn’t want anyone to take her from here. As the years passed, the girl’s body kept shrinking. She eventually became a baby 20 years later. The prayers intensified. They all thought that now the baby is going to die as it’s body was shrinking to a new born baby. The day was arriving. The doctor told everyone that the girl was now a prematurely new born baby and can die anyday. A jagrata was help. People singing songs. Playing dholaks. Jainu (Jainendra) sat next to the khatiya all this while. At some point during the jagrata and songs. The baby’s breathing stopped. Jainenedra checked the heartbeat. It wasn’t there. Jainendra had gotten attached to the little baby, like it was his own. He wept. His sobs drowned in the noise of the songs being sung by the villagers. Slowly the news of the baby’s death spread. The singing stopped. Silence all overthe mountain. A few sobs could be heard as everyone wanted to get a glimpse of the baby’s body. 
Then suddenly, the baby started crying. It was awake. For the first time in 38 years. It was awake and crying. The whole village became silent, then erupted in joy. Jainendra couldn’t believe his eyes. He took her in as her own daughter and named her Kadambari (kaddu for short). 
She was now a normal child. Growing up normally. No abnormal behavior. The villagers knew what a miracle this girl is and always treated her special. Jainendra spoiled her rotten.

---

DOCUMENT: Daddu_Damdar/Raw_Archive/AltBranch_Folklore_Mountain_Van_Fire_and_Miracle.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis: Alternate Lore
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]
> **Core Focus:** Mythic backstory scene: Bisnu riding a scooter in freezing rain, the mountain van crash fire, and the miraculously unburnt girl.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Some back story with god themes. 
Bisnu didn’t know what to do. He prayed to the gods, he begged the girl to help him. They tried one last time. This time the khatiya moved. Everyone who saw this was stunned. They believed that the girl was some kind of devi. Bisnu didn’t agree. He wanted to get rid of his troubles. He sent her to the city hospital.
That night, the rain was incessant. It got so cold that all the quilts could not keep the villagers warm. Bisnu was restless and tried sleeping. But the cold was too much to bear. Suddenly the earth started shaking. It was an earthquake. Bisnu ran outside to see all the villagers gathered on the road. Bhuppi’s mother shouted that this is all because the devi was sent out of the village that all these calamities are happening. People standing on the road, in the cold winter rain. Shivering and shaking like the earth a few moments before. Bisnu started to believe that the girl was indeed a devi and needed to be brought back. He took bhuppi on his scooter to go and get the girl back, although he knew that there was no way he could catch upto the van. 
Bisnu was determined. Dehradun was 12 hours from the village. He knew that the van would reach Dehradun by 8 in the morning. WIth the pace he was going, he could reach Dehradun by 10 then take the girl and come back to the village by 11 in the night. It might be too late by then. He rode his scooter fast. AS he got out of the village the weather was nice and cool. No sign of rain here. He twisted the accelerator and rode even faster. 
It was 3 o’clock. Bhuppi was dozing on the back seat. Although Bisnu himself was sleepy, he kept telling Bhuppi to stay awake. Bisnu’s eyes were closing and he could be forgiven for not seeing a man waving in front of his scooter. For a split second that he slept, he had a dream where he was going somewhere and the girl grabbed his hand. She told him to take her with him. Bisnu refused. The girl was begging almost. Bisnu jerked his hand away. He turned and walked, the girl appeared in front of her. Her hair flying. THe silhoute of her body immense from up close. Everything black, except the eyes. The eyes were wide and looking at Bisnu.
This woke up Bisnu from his sleep and he saw the driver of the van waving in front of his scooter. The scooter skid and hit the mountain. Luckily Bisnu and Bhuppi were fine. They had minor scratches. The driver thought that Bisnu is going to yell at him for causing the accident, but he was relieved to see him. He asked where the girl was? Where was the van? The driver tells him that ever since he left the village, he has been having trouble. His van sputtered to a halt. His fuel was leaking and his tire got flat again. So he had to stop. He got out of the van to check the tires and the leak. The van started moving down the slope and went down the mountain. 
They looked down the mountain and saw the van burning in flames. Bisnu was filled with guilt. Devi or no devi, his decision had led to this girl’s death. The three of them went down the mountain to have a look. Bisnu wept seeing the van, thikning about the tragedy. They sat there till the fire went out. Most of it out of guilt, but also because the fire provided them with some warmth. As the sun started rising around 6. The fire also slowly went out. Bisnu and Bhuppi had slept for a bit till then. IN the morning Bisnu wanted to check if there are any remains of the girl to take home. He looked inside the now burnt van. He almost shat his pants seeing the girl still alive. The clothes on her body were burnt. THe previous injuries were still there. But she was otherwise unharmed. It was a miracle.
Bisnu covered the girl’s body in his shawls and blankets and brought her back to his village. The sun shone brightly and the village was back to normal.

---

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis: Alternate Lore
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]
> **Core Focus:** Speculative treatment: Daddu as a cursed immortal who must die for 60 seconds to consult Chitragupta in an infinite comic bureaucracy.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Act 1: 1-3 Set up
4-6 stakes
Act 2: 7-12 success - 12 is midpoint. Disillusionment.
Proactive. Strength
13-18 Downward spiral. - Broken, lost all hope, sabotaged.
Reactive. Weaknesses
18 - Forced to see a secret path that they couldn’t see before. Often something that their character flaw prevented them to do. (Enlightenment). They make a clear, extremely risky plan. (Final strategy)
Act 4: 19-24 Transformation.
Emotional truth. Embracing the truth. 
Stakes should staring be right in the face.
All wants replaced with needs. All fears are faced. All lies revealed. Every choice leads to a single Climax.
22-24 - New world. Character or world has changed. New perspective.  Or adapted in a unexpected way. 

Superhero story about an 80 year old homeless guy suffering from dementia, who thinks he is a superhero and a 12 year old girl who can’t remember her past feels a strange connection with the guy and decides to help him. 


1. Hook> PP>> 12-year-old CG sees this old homeless guy living under the bridge, and is always curious about him. She lives in the building in front of the bridge and sees him everyday. He punches walls and in turn injures his hand, he stacks up boxes and tries to fly, but falls down. He does this almost everyday and fails, but he never fails to do these things. One day CG sees that a bunch of boys are bullying him, she feels entitled to help him and goes down to shoo the boys away. The boys turn on her and will probably beet ehr up, but the older guy stops them and says that he is a superhero and will beat the shit out of these boys. Ofcourse he fails again and gets beaten up. CG feels indebted to him as he saved her from the boys. 

2. The girl takes him to the hospital, where some tests are done. They are told to come back later to find the results. The girl takes her home to get him cleaned up and feed him. She hides him from her parents at first. She can’t let him go to the streets. The old guy tells her that he is a chiranjeevi, but he can’t remember which one. He has trouble with his memory. He can’t even remember his name. CG doesn’t believe that this guy is a superhero. She thinks he is just a crazy old person who needs help. She plans to keep him hidden in the house. The old guy’s wounds heal very fast. In 2 days the wounds are gone. This surprises CG and she is in doubt. But her parents find out and throw him out of the house. He goes back to the street. CG feels bad for him, but she can’t do anything. 

3. Impetus> PP>> CG goes to the hospital to collect the old guy’s reports and finds out that he has cancer. He doesn’t have too long to live. CG is sad. She goes back to talk to the old guy. She tells him about his reports. He tells her he knows that he is dying. But he only needs to get his powers back, so he can heal himself. CG thinks it’s his craziness talking. She brings him back to her home telling her parents about him. He talks to the parents and tells them that he is a chiranjeevi. CG’s father is an expert in myths and pauranic legends. He tells them that there were 8 chiranjeevis. But there was supposed to a 9th as well. About 3000-4000 years ago. But this one was unique in some way. There is very little written about this chiranjeevi. He was given his powers to save humanity. Gods had given Humans full autonomy. Kaliyuga. Before leaving the world for humans, gods gave one of the humans the power to live forever and help and save humanity. She gives him her tent. He lives outside, but more comfortably. She is giving him food and water and talks to him everyday. He talks about stories where he had defeated people over the years. Like in 1500 BC he might have saved some king from danger or something. He took part in wars etc. SO what happened? CG asks. The old guy, let’s calls him BV, tells her that something happened 80 years ago. His powers went away. His memory as well. He just remembers fragments of it. For fun, she decides to entertain him by pretending to train him with different exercises to bring his powers back. THey bond over this. BV doesn’t really get his powers back but he likes spending time with CG. 
The girl knows he is only going to be alive for a few months. His health is deteriorating. She sees him cough blood. She desperately wants him to be healthy again. BV keeps telling her that if he gets his powers back, he will heal himself and become as good as new. CG doesn’t really believe that his powers will return, so, she is sad that soon BV is going to die. One day, during a superpower training exercise, CG’s life is in danger. BV saves her. There is a glimpse of his power as he shows his strength. This makes CG believe that there might be some truth to BVs claims. This means that if he is telling the truth then he probably will get better if he finds his powers. This option seems to be working. 

4 She asks her dad how can a chiranjivi die? If that was a real thing, how would she be able to save a chiranjivi. Her dad tells her that Chiranjeevis live forever, so if a chiranjivi is dying, that means that the boon came with a curse. The chiranjeevi broke some rule in the chiranjivi boon, that got triggered. CG asks BV if he remembers something about this. BV doesn’t have any memory of it. Her dad tells her that the only other way to find out is by finding Chitragupta. He has records of everything. He will know how the CHiranjeevi lost his powers. But where to find Chitragupta?  The only way to find Chitragupta is to die. He will meet you after your death and give you an account of your life. This sounds like a dead end again. What good would it be if they had to die to meet Chitragupta. 
Can they die for a minute and come back. They would have to go to a tantrik to do this. They go to a couple of tantrik’s who try to con them out of money. But during one of the rituals by a fake tantrik, Something triggers and an explosion happens. THe tantrik sees something in both of them and gets scared. He refuses to do anymore rituals. He tells them about another tantrik. A real one. They should go an talk to him. 
CG has something to explore, she asks BV what could he have done? BV doesn’t quite remember. There must be someone they can ask. Someone who might know from that long ago. BV doesn’t know anyone. BV has a locket that his mother gave him years ago. He still keeps it around his neck. He has forgotten his mother. 
5. They are scared of this tantrik, but when they meet him they find out that he is a ganja smoking, guitar playing, cartoon watching, tattooed 40 year old guy. They are apprehensive if he will be able to do antyhing. But He promises that he can arrange a meeting with Chitragupta. Should they do it? THey do it. Of course they do it and they meet Chitragupta while they are at it. 
I imagine the scene with Chitragupta in a huge library. CHitragupta is a fun guy, who has been trying out humor for the past 2000 years, but failing miserably. These two meet him and ask him about BV’s curse. CHitragupta is confused, but he tells that the 9th chiranjeevi lost their power in 1947. The chiranjeevi was supposed to be save all humans from calamities and evil. This was the chiranjeevi’s sole purpose. He was supposed to do this till the end of the yuga, which is about 4,30,000 years, when the dashavtar comes and ends the Kaliyuga. But the lord of the time, Demon Kali has many servants all over the world. One of them, a powerful demon, called Manoj, found the chiranjeevi, but he couldn’t kill him, so with a ritual of some sort, he locked his power and made him forget. SO how do they get the power back? They will need to steal the gem in which he has stored the memories. 
6. Dramatic question> PP>> How do they steal this thing from Manoj? Can they really
********************************
7. Subplot> PP>>
8
9
10
11
12. Midpoint> PP>> CG is chiranjeevi. BV is just a guy. 
********************************
13. Subplot> PP>>
14
15

18. Lowpoint> PP>> CG finds out that BV is her son. She had settled down with a husband and a child and let her guard down. MAnoj caught them, killed the husband and locked her powers and made her into a 12-year-old. Her parents were essentially spies who were feeding her medicines to keep her
*********************************
19

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis: Character Lore
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]
> **Core Focus:** Exhaustive psychological and demographic dossier: Dharampur setting, weed-dealing campus hustle, social insecurities, and class shame.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Padmalakshmi AKA Piddu never really thought about her class and social status till her parents admitted her to a private school. She had always lived in her small 2 bedroom house that was too small if more 2 guests came over. 

physical attributes

Gender: Female.
Age: 16
Ethnicity: Indian
Height: 5’6”
Weight: 52
Hair color: Black. Long.
Eye color: Brown
Complexion: Wheatish
Teeth: White and normal.
Eyesight: Normal. 
Physical attractiveness: Good looking, but doesn’t have the means to do makeup or dress up.
Any deformities, handicaps, or distinctive marks: None
Hair style and color: Ties at the back. Mother puts oil on it every morning before sending her off to school.
Facial hair: None
Clothing style: Wears clothes handed down by her relatives. Her mother brings hand-me-downs from the houses she cleans and cooks in. Akshi has started doing small hustles to earn some pocketmoney. Selling weed to kids at school. Even teachers.
Accessories: Ear rings. Nose ring. 
Jewelry: Ear rings given to her by her mom. Nose ring. 
Tattoos and/or piercings: Ear pierced. Nose pierced. Wants to pierce her lips too. Mother has threatened her not to do it. 
Hygiene: Clean. She is used to keeping things in place in her small house. Her mother says, it makes the house look bigger. 
Posture: Straight. Not really looking people in the eye. She fidgets a lot. She is always observing her surroundings. 
Contacts or glasses: None. 
Type of vehicle he uses: None. Public transport and walking. 

Background

Birth order: Only child. 
Number of siblings: 0
Parents and their occupation: Father is a constable in police. He is tame and hasn’t had a promotion in years. Mother is a maid/nanny. Earns her living by cleaning houses and cooking. Used to be a Senior Inspector. Caught a 
What was important to the people who raised him: They tried to save every penny they could. Mother would always tell the father to take bribes or get better at taking bribes. SHe would compare him to other hawaldars who have been taking proper bribes and have made bigger houses. Father is kind and empathetic fellow. He tries, but ends up giving money rather than taking. 
THey have made sure that their daughter studies well. They put her in a private school, even thought the fees was out of their budget. They have saved up 2 lakh rupees for her education. 
Level of wealth or poverty: Poor. Lower middle class. 
Whether she lived in the city or the country: North. Dharampur (Fictional small town) 
Marital status of parents: Married and happy. 
Culture: Indian. Hindu. Mother is religious. Akshi not so much. 
Emotional climate in the home: Happy. But tensions because of poor financial conditions. Her parents can’t get her even essential things. SHe has to make do with make shift objects, or hand me downs, or second hand from kabadi. 
Education: In class 12th. Intelligent. Doesn’t show. Looks like she doesn’t pay attention in class. She doesn’t really studies at home. But she has been in top 3 rankers in her class. 
Relationship with parent of the same gender: Her mother is the disciplinarian in the house. SHe keeps both Akshi and her father in line. 
Era: Contemporary. 2022. 
Societal backdrop: NA
Country and region: India. Northern part. Uttarakhand. 
Politics: She isn’t into politics. She knows that all politicians lie and steal, so she is against all of them. But her political knowledge is limited. Nrither is she interested in it. 

Inward Attributes

Intelligence: Top 3 in her class. She is street smart and book smart. 
Natural gifts or talents: She is athletic and fit. 
Love language:Acts of service. Gift giving. 
Self-esteem: Low. SHe has insecurities about her social status and wealth. Everyone in her class comes in a car or a bike. She walks more than 6 kms to school. Always getting late. 
Zeal and/or religiosity: Not religious at all. 


Love language

Gift giving
Acts of service
Quality time (spending time together in meaningful, eye-to-eye conversations of the heart)
Physical touch (hugs, caresses, handholding, and simply sitting together on the couch, even if no conversation occurs)
Words of affirmation (expressing words of thanks and appreciation and love)


Other Details

Friends: A couple of friends in her colony. They’ve known her for a long time. They used to play football in the colony. One friend in school. She likes the strange and rough nature of Akshi. But she doesn’t know much about her. She has told her that her father is a high ranking police officer and her mother is an interior decorator. She also has acquaintance with drug dealers. One of the dealer is her old childhood friend. She buys weed from them for 1000 bucks a packet and sells it to students in her school for profit. She makes about 10K every month doing this.
  She is very popular in college. People think she is the hippest person. She wears all these torn and rugged looking clothes. People think this is the new hipster fashion. SHe is basically fooling everyone with this. THat’s why she doesn’t have too many close friends. Because she cant share her real life with them. She has a boyfriend in 1st year college. He is a lazy guy. She basically uses him to get in the hostel and sell weed to college boys. This is how she makes money to somehow maintain the illusion of being hip and trendy. 
How do people view this character: She seems mysterious to people. No one knows that she sells weed. She doesn’t talk about her life with anyone. She doesn’t hang out with them. She can’t afford to. 
Lives with: Her parents. 
Fights with: Her mother. Akshi has gotten emotionally distant from her parents. She always stays in her small room. Her mother was afraid that she might fall into bad habits and might mingle with wrong people. That’s why she put her in a private school. So that she would be with the right kind of children. Something she could aspire to and be ambitious about. But ever since then Akshi has become even more distant. 
Spends time with: Alone. As the story starts, she becomes friends with Daddu. 
Wishes to spend time with: Vibhor Bora. A boy in her class. He is extremely popular. But Akshi can’t seem to talk to him properly. She thinks that he is too good for her. 
Who depends on him and why:  Usually her father does whatever her mother tells him to do. She is the decision maker. But sometimes they disagree on somethings. Whenever her parents have an argument, she is the deciding vote. They know how smart their daughter is and they, especially her father, value her opinions. 
What people does she most admire:
Enemies: There is someone at school who suspects her. She doesn’t know what Akshi does, but she knows that there is something strange about her. 
Dating, marriage: None
Children: None.
Relationship with God: Almost non existent.
Overall outlook on life: People with money have it easy. If you don’t have money you have to struggle in life. So, you should make money if you want to be happy in life. 
Does this character like himself: Yes. Kind of. 
What, if anything, would he like to change about his life: She would like to change the financial aspects of her life. She has had to pretend to be something she is not to fit in. If she had money, she wouldn’t have to pretend. 
What personal demons haunt him: She is pretending to be someone she is not. This takes a big toll on her emotionally and physically. She is always afraid of someone finding out her truth.
Is he lying to himself about something: She is lying about her financial condition to he friends and school. SHe is also lying about where she makes money. She tells herself that if she is cool and popular people will like her. If they find out that she is poor, they won’t. She ties her self worth to her net worth. 
Is he optimistic or pessimistic: Optimistic. 
Morality level: Flexible
Confidence level: High
How is he viewed by others: Mysterious and cool. Students in her school think of her as someone who is cool as fuck. There are a few people who can, sort of, see through her charade. But most people believe her lies.

---

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis: Character Lore
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]
> **Core Focus:** Defining Piddu's aesthetic: DIY thrift rebellion, sewing 96kg constable pants into 'genie shorts', and wearing father's oversized brass buckles.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Logline: When an old homeless ex-superhero, with memory issues and no powers, saves a 16-year-old skater girl, they go an adventure to rediscover his superpowers. 

Genre: Comedy, action, superhero

Duration: 30 min, 8 episodes

Synopsis: Padmalaxmi AKA Piddu is a 16-year-old, in the final year of her school. She is a small, frail but fiery girl. She doesn’t let her size get in her way when it comes to standing up for herself and others. Her father used to be a hero cop, who was demoted when he got on the wrong side of some powerful people. Now he is a constable. Piddu’s mother works as a nanny to make ends meet. This lack of money and his family’s situation embarrasses Piddu. She gets into fights regularly when she is teased about this. Piddu’s father has had enough of that hero cop life. He wants to live a peaceful life with his family. He has been saving money so that he and his family can retire and go back to their family home, where he plans to open a general store. Piddu is not satisfied with this and keeps  prodding her father to do something about his situation. Catch some bad guys, solve murders so that he an get a promotion. But it keeps falling on deaf ears.

Piddu is bothered by the lack of money in the house. Having to cut down costs. She can’t buy new clothes. So, she has to wear either her old clothes, that are too tight or torn, or she has to wear her father’s hand me downs. Tshirts, shirts. She wears these clothes creatively to create an illusion of style. For example, wearing a Tshirt torn at shoulder, she would tear an exact hole on the other side, then stitch it up neatly, even paint to create a unique look. Another time she got a trouser from her father, who was 96 Kgs, 6ft 3 to Piddu’s 45 kg, 5ft 2, skinny girl.  Piddu cut of the trouser at the thighs, then sewed the bulky part on the sides to the waist and called it the genie shorts. She would wear it with her father’s belt with a huge buckle. 

Piddu had created an image of this girl who doesn’t give a fuck about anyone. She does care about what’s fair and what’s not. She thinks that she and her family haven’t been dealt a fair hand. So, she often gets into a fight when she sees someone bullying someone else, or someone doing something unfair. Despite her size people were afraid of her because she wouldn’t hesitate in confronting people. People know her as someone who will get into fights for small things. So they stay away. Some boys and girls do try to instigate her to have fun. She gives in to their taunts. She has a crush on a boy at school. Even he is afraid of her. She tries to act normal in front of him, but it never works.

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis: Character Arcs
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]
> **Core Focus:** Outlining potential arcs: the reluctant retired hero, the experienced elder mentor, and the legacy torch-passer.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

The character arc of the elderly superhero in your story could take many different forms, depending on the specific details of the story and the direction you want to take. Here are a few suggestions for potential character arcs for the elderly superhero:

The "reluctant hero" arc: In this arc, the elderly superhero starts out as someone who has retired from their superhero days and wants to live a peaceful, quiet life. However, circumstances (such as the appearance of a new villain, or a call for help from a former ally) force them to come out of retirement and use their powers once again. As they face new challenges and battles, they rediscover their sense of purpose and heroism, and ultimately find fulfillment in their role as a superhero.

The "mentor" arc: In this arc, the elderly superhero is a wise and experienced hero who has been around for many years. They take the 10-year-old girl under their wing and teach her the ropes of being a superhero, while also facing challenges and battles together. As the girl grows and learns, the elderly superhero learns new things as well, and their relationship deepens and strengthens.

The "legacy" arc: In this arc, the elderly superhero is the last of a long line of heroes, and they feel a strong sense of responsibility to carry on their family's legacy. They are determined to pass on their knowledge and skills to the next generation, and see the 10-year-old girl as their successor. As they work together and face challenges, the elderly superhero helps the girl to become a powerful and capable hero in her own right, and ultimately passes the mantle of hero onto her.

Overall, the character arc for the elderly superhero in your story can take many different forms, and it is up to you to decide which one will be the most effective and meaningful for the story you want to tell.
