A great conflict in the story about the elderly superhero and the 10-year-old girl could take many different forms, depending on the specific details of the story and the direction you want to take. Here are a few suggestions for potential conflicts:

The conflict between the elderly superhero and the new villain: In this conflict, the elderly superhero and the 10-year-old girl must team up to stop a powerful and dangerous villain who has emerged on the scene. The villain might be someone with a personal grudge against the elderly superhero, or someone who wants to take over the city or the world. As the hero and the girl face the villain and their minions, they must overcome obstacles and challenges, and ultimately come up with a plan to defeat the villain and save the day.

The conflict between the elderly superhero and their own mortality: In this conflict, the elderly superhero is getting older and starting to feel the effects of their age. They may be slower, weaker, or less able to use their powers effectively. This puts them at a disadvantage when facing villains, and they may struggle with the idea of retiring and handing over the mantle of hero to someone else. As they deal with their own mortality and their declining powers, they must also find a way to continue being a hero and making a difference, even as they face their own limitations.

The conflict between the elderly superhero and the 10-year-old girl: In this conflict, the elderly superhero and the girl start out as allies and friends, but eventually have a falling out. This could be due to a misunderstanding, a clash of personalities, or a disagreement about how to handle a particular situation. As they deal with the conflict and try to resolve their differences, they must also continue to face villains and challenges, and ultimately learn to trust and rely on each other once again.

Overall, the conflict in the story about the elderly superhero and the 10-year-old girl can take many different forms, and it is up to you to decide which one will be the most effective and meaningful for the story you want to tell.

What else? Hero vs people? Power to many vs power to one? Seeing the power withing you? Wish you could fight every injustice in the world? You see someone kicking a dog, or being mean to it. You want to slap that person. But you can’t. People being horrible to others. You can do it now. 
What’s wrong with superhero movies. Superpowerful heros. I like that part. What about a superhero power that changes hand from one to the other. THere have been 100s of such superheros in history. 
I’m quite excited about the history of the superheros. 
What about the girl? What do we want to see from her? A mentor? We’ve seen mentors. Even the novelty of the mentor being a little girl will be short lived. Friend? A little girl friends with a superhero? It’s interesting. But not exciting yet. How can this be exciting? Something about the girl. How is she impactful? Memorable? Is she feisty? WHat difference does that make. She can boss superhero around. WHat will that achieve? Maybe he will start using his powers again and do what he was supposed to do. But that’s not exciting still. 

Something about the girl. The girl is shy? THe superhero will protect her from things. SHe will learn to have confidence. Not exciting. 

The girl is a thief. She might use the superhero to steal. She will have to manipulate him or something like that. 

What do I already know about this?

The superhero is old. 75-80 years. He has superstrength, can run fast and fly. He can’t fly anymore because of old age. 
Comtemporary story. IN the world of anytime anywhere mobile cameras, social media, TV news and virality. 
He will not be able to hide well.

Consider the theme or message of your story. What do you want to say with your story? Are you trying to explore the power of friendship, the importance of never giving up, or some other theme or message? Think about the setting of your story. Where does the story take place? Is it set in a realistic city or town, or in a fantasy or science fiction world? The setting can have a big impact on the story and the characters, so it is important to choose a setting that feels right for your story.

Develop the characters in your story. What are the personalities and backgrounds of the elderly superhero and the 10-year-old girl? How do they interact and relate to each other? By giving your characters depth and complexity, you can make them more relatable and interesting to readers.

Plan out the plot of your story. What are the key events and challenges that the elderly superhero and the girl will face? How will the story build to a climax and a resolution? By planning out the plot in advance, you can ensure that your story has a clear structure and a satisfying ending.

Overall, there are many things to keep in mind as you explore the idea of an elderly superhero and a 10-year-old girl. By focusing on themes, setting, characters, and plot, you can turn your idea into a fully-realized and engaging story that readers will love.

Backstory: She was born in a poor neighborhood. At a young age she was told by her parents that she has to live within her means. Her parents also told her that honesty isn’t always the best policy. You have to be clever in this world to survive. As long as you aren’t caught doing something, you are not wrong. So the key is to not get caught. 
She used to study in a public school, with students from her own social level. But students were into gambling and cards and would often get into trouble. Her parents wanted her to get a good education and a good job so they admitted her to a private school, with a high fees, through a needy student scholarship. 
In this new school, she felt out of place. There were students who would come in cars, their own scooters and bikes. She would walk to the school to save the bus fare and would often get late. She always tried to hide the fact that she was poor. She told students that her father was a high ranking police officer and her mother was an interior decorator. She would hand out with other students, but could never go to the places where they would go, like clubbing, weekend getaways and parties. Pretty soon she became an outsider. Always in the periphery but never in the inner circles. 
She liked this boy Vibhor Bora. He is the football team captain and one of the most popular boy in school. Everybody likes him and invites him to any gathering or activity they are going to. She wants to be one of those people who are invited to everything. So she wants to date Vibhor, so she could also be popular and known.

Akshi Gupta is a 16-year-old girl. Rich family. Dad owns factories. In school. Class 11th. Not a popular kid. SHe is not that great in studies. Smart kid. Gets into trouble all the time. 
Old homeless guy. Thinks he is a superhero. Probably 75-80. He doesn’t know how old he is. Early onset of dementia. Forgets things. 
Akshi is like the gunda of the class. She sells cigarettes to boys in class. Netflix subscriptions - one time payment. SHe gets into fights. Gets punished, suspended. 
She has to spend time at home. Her father has made donations to the school that’s why they haven’t rusticated her. They’ve taken away her credit cards as punishments. She calls a couple of her friends to hang out, but they are disappointed in her for using violence. She is stuck at home. She sees this old homeless person across the street, living in a house made of tarpaulin and sticks. She sees him behave strangely. Trying to uproot a tree with his bare hands. Trying to jump off of a wall and falling flat on his face. She becomes curious when she sees that the guy is doing it again and again. Failing but now stopping. She talks to him and observes that he zones out from time to time and calls himself a superhero. SHe thinks he is a crazy guy and decides that he is not worth the time. 
One day, she is walking around her neighborhood. She sees the old guy practice his jumps off the wall. She stops to observe. A few boys come around and make fun of the old guy. They push him around. He warns them not to bully him, or it will turn ugly for them. They laugh at him and destroy his house. This angers Akshi and she decides to butt in. She tells off the young boys, who now turn their attention to her. She fights a couple of them off, but they over power her. The older guy now puts his torn coat as a cape and tells the boys to get away from the girl, or he will have to do it himself. THe boys laugh at him and decide to finish the job by beating him up.
The old guy doesn’t remember where he is or who he is. He is lying there beaten and battered. Akshi takes him to the hospital to get him checked. The doctor tells her that the old guy has dementia.

Daddu is old school, follows rules, believes everything the government says. He has been doing his superhero thing a long time back. When he first got his superpowers, he was an eager kid. He would stop accidents from happening, catch muggers, robberies, even help old people and kids cross the street. Police tried to catch him, but couldn’t. THen eventually they started asking for his help. Unofficially, of course.

This was unacceptable to the brahmin samaj, and they tried to kidnap the baby from the house many times. Saam, daam, dand, bhed. The priest would always thwart the plans. This priest was BV. The character arc of the 10-year-old girl in your story could take many different forms, depending on the specific details of the story and the direction you want to take. Here are a few suggestions for potential character arcs for the girl:

The "reluctant hero" arc: In this arc, the girl starts out as someone who is content with her ordinary life and has no interest in being a superhero. However, circumstances (such as the discovery of her own powers, or a call for help from the elderly superhero) force her to become a hero and use her powers to save the day. As she faces challenges and battles, she grows in confidence and learns to embrace her role as a hero, and ultimately finds fulfillment and purpose in her new identity.

The "coming of age" arc: In this arc, the girl is a young person who is just starting to discover her powers and potential. She is eager to learn and grow, and looks to the elderly superhero as a mentor and guide. As she faces challenges and battles, she learns valuable lessons and experiences significant growth, both as a person and as a hero.

The "sidekick" arc: In this arc, the girl is a young hero who teams up with the elderly superhero to take on battles and challenges. She is eager to prove herself and prove her worth as a hero, and is willing to take risks and make sacrifices to help the elderly superhero. As she works with the elderly superhero, she learns new skills and gains confidence, and ultimately becomes an equal partner in their adventures.

Overall, the character arc for the 10-year-old girl in your story can take many different forms, and it is up to you to decide which one will be the most effective and meaningful for the story you want to tell.

DOCUMENT: Daddu_Damdar/Raw_Archive/Outline_Core_Arc_LoanShark_Raid_and_Arrest.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis: Structure & Beats
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]
> **Core Focus:** Crucial narrative spine: staging the loan shark raid, swiping Rs 5,000 for a dress, Hawaldar Gupta's arrest, and the Baba Damdam Satsang.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Akshi Gupta is a poor girl in a rich school. Her parents are lower middle class people. Mom works as a maid/nanny in other people’s houses and dad is a low level government servant. She is smart but regularly gets in trouble at her school, usually because she has gotten into a fight with someone. She always believes she is doing the right thing, but it always backfires. Akshi has a crush on a boy in class but she doesn’t have the courage to talk to him. She has insecurities about her social status. She has a friend, Naomi, in class who encourages her to talk to the boy. Even creates situations for her to get close to him. But Akshi always gets tongue tied. 
Akshi has told people at school that her mom is an interior decorator and her father is a high ranking police officer. She saves money by hustling people and maintains the charade that she is well off. But even with the money she saves she cannot compete with other students in the school. SHe often has to refuse offers to go to clubs and weekend getaways. So she has become an outsider. SHe thinks that if people found out about her she will lose their respect. They will treat her as garbage. 

Akshi meets this strange old homeless man near her house. She talks to him out of curiosity. The old man is cagey about who he is and where has he come from. Akshi starts calling him daddu and develops an affection when she sees how helpless he is. She sees that he also gets these attacks where he forgets who he is. Daddu realizes that the Akshi is a sweet girl and tells her that he is a superhero, but, due to old age, his powers don’t work the way they used to. Akshi, like any sane person would, thinks he is crazy. She decides to stay away from him. 
One day, she sees a few boys bullying him. They have taken down his tarpaulin tent and are kicking him. Akshi cannot stand this and confronts the boys. The boys rough her up as well. Daddu calls them out and tells them that now he will teach them a lesson. HE shouts at Akshi to run. Akshi runs away to get the corner traffic police. WHen she returns the boys are gone and Daddu is on the ground in a fugue state. Akshi takes him to a government hospital to tend to his injuries. He has none. The doctor tells her that he has dementia. Akshi feels bad for him and takes him to his tent and puts it back up for him. She gives him her blanket to sleep comfortably. 
When Akshi returns back to school, she finds out that there is a school trip arranged. All students have to bring one guardian along to come. Naomi encourages Akshi to somehow bring any relative. This is a golden chance to get close to Vibhor. Akshi is in a pickle. His mother and father both refuse. They can’t take leave from their jobs. Akshi decides to bring Daddu. She gives him a bath and a haircut and takes him as his grandfather. 
At the zoo, Akshi talks to Vibhor. Vibhor is dumb and sweet. Vibhor’s friends take him away. Naomi goads Akshi to talk to Vibhor. She forgets about Daddu to go talk to him. She attempts to get into the group to get close to Vibhor. Vibhor talks about how he wants to become a police officer, but his parents keep telling him that he has to take over their multimillion dollar business. Life is so hard! Tch tch tch. Akshi blurts out that her father is in the police. Akshi lies that her father is a top ranking official and he has caught many big name criminals like, (made up names) Pappu dadiyal, Danger kaana, Bahut chota rajan etc. Vibhor is impressed. He is behaving nicely towards her but she can’t get any time alone with her. At the end she makes a fool of herself and runs away when people are laughing at her. 

She walks around the zoo alone, vowing to get back at everyone. At the end of the trip, when everyone has gone, Akshi panics when she can’t find daddu. Vibhor sees her and offers to help. THey look around the zoo. An aunty standing alone outside the bear enclosure sees daddu inside. Akshi sees that daddu has opened the gate of the enclosure. She sees that the lock is crushed and daddy is inside in his fugue state. She sees that the bear has not yet noticed daddu so she can probably go in and bring him out. She tells the aunty to get some zoo authority. Aunty is panicking at this point. She runs to get someone. 

Akshi walks carefully, but she accidentally steps on a twig. This catches the bear’s attention. He first notices daddu and goes to check on him. Akshi panics and makes a noise to get the bear’s attention. The bear runs towards her. She runs towards the gate. SHe runs around the enclosure trying to get rid of the bear and telling daddu to go out. Daddu isn’t listening. THe bear finally traps her. He is about to attack her. In a second, she is out of the enclosure, with the bear fallen on the ground. Daddu is panting next to her. She can’t believe it. Daddu does have superpowers. The aunty returns with the someone and is relieved to see both outside. She announces that Akshi has saved the old guy from being eaten by the bear. Vibhor has also come and seen the bravery Akshi has shown. He praises her and wants to talk to her but Akshi is freaked out and leaves with daddu. 
Akshi asks daddu about his superpowers and he tells her that he had been saying this all along. Akshi doesn’t now how to process this. Next day she goes to the school and everyone is praising her. They’ve all heard how she bravely saved her daddu from the bear. Vibhor has told the entire school about it. Vibhor is also interested in Akshi. Akshi likes this new found attention. The school bullies are also afraid of her. 

Akshi goes to daddu after school and tells him about it. She asks what kind of superpowers does he have. She asks about why has nobody heard about him. Daddu tells her that he was active when internet hadn’t taken off yet. Phones didn’t have cameras. He didn’t want any atttention either. There are a few articles about him in local newspapers. He takes her to a library. WE get a sense that the librarian knows him. She has all the newspaper prints where he was mentioned. THere were aonly a couple of blurry photographs. This had become an underground legend/myth about this superhuman person, who was going from city to city and saving people. Serious journalists had dismissed this as a rumor, likening it to a Yeti. Akshi is fascinated by all this. 

SHe asks him why has he stopped saving people. HE tells her that his body and memory had deteriorated. He didn’t want to put lives in danger after he She pitches him ideas that they can use to make money. Daddu rejects all of them. Daddu doesn’t want to use his powers for selfish reasons. Akshi tells him that if he can’t use his powers for himself, then what good are the powers. Daddu believes that a superpower comes with certain responsibility and someone who wields it cannot be selfish. 

A few days later, in school, Vibhor brings a girl to Akshi. THe girl is crying and tells Akshi that her father is having some problem with a loan shark. He had taken a big loan for his business, from someone. Now that person is sending gundas and harrassing them. THe other day they beat up her father in front of her. SHe begs Akshi to talk to her dad and get this gunda behind bars. Akshi panics. She can’t do anything. But Vibhor also pleads and she accidentally agrees. 

She goes home and asks her dad about the goon, Anil Rai. Her dad tells her that Anil RAi is a dangerous guy. The police has several cases against him, but can’t arrest him. THe police is afraid of him. AKshi doesn’t know what to do. She asks Daddu to help. But Daddu refuses. He has stopped his superhero activities and doesn’t want to start. Akshi pleads, but Daddu is adamant. 

Akshi deides to tell the girl that her father is busy in other big cases and won’t be able to help. But the this time the girl also has bruises on her face. She tells her that her father has decided to help her. 
Akshi goes back to daddu and manipulates him into going to Anil rai’s adda. Daddu is angry that she is trying to make him to this. HE tells her that he will under no circumstance go inside to fight the goons. Akshi runs inside and challenges the goons. Daddu has to go in and save Akshi. THey tie up all the goons. Akshi calls her father to the location and hides. Her father catches the goons and gets all the credit. 

Akshi is happy and excited. Daddu is angry. Akshi takes him shopping. She buys a dress for herself. Buys icecream for daddu. Daddu is surprised that Akshi has so much money to spend. Akshi tells him that she swiped some money from Anil Rai. Daddu is angry and disappointed. Akshi tells him that it was only 5-6K. There was so much money in that adda. BUt she only took what she needed. The money is going to waste anyway. Daddu disagrees. The girl calls Akshi and thanks her. Daddu is somewhat glad that he helped the girl. 

Akshi is popular in school. Everyone knows what she did for the girl by getting her father to arrest the bad guys. Akshi likes this. Vibhor is also interested in her. He wants to meet her father, the hero. Akshi tells him that he is busy and travelling most of the time. But she will soon get him to meet him. She has money to go clubbing with her classmates. She wears the new dress she bought. BUt the girls already know that even though it cost 2000 bucks for her. It is a cheap dress. Akshi feels bad about it and leaves. 

**************
WE have a baba. He is called Baba Damdam. He has big satsangs, where he teaches fitness to young boys and girls. He goes on stage with thousands waiting to see him. Then at the same time everyone exercises with baba as he is teaching them about morality and justice(nyay) and Injustice(anyay) and motivating them to take action. These boys and girls are ready to do anything at his command. He has millions of such followers in different cities. He is a believing atheist. He believes that god exists. But he has put humans on the earth to decide right from wrong and give punishments. He performs feats of extraordinary strength on stage. 
His story is that he was a straight, normal kid. But at 15 something happened that gave him a purpose. So he started teaching his values to boys eventually became this hugely popular baba. 

**************

Akshi wants to do more, daddu is reluctant. Akshi tries to convince him that he only said that this power comes with a responsibility. Now it’s his responsibility to use it for good. Daddu reluctantly agrees. 

Over the next few days. Daddu and Akshi catch several gangs, drug dealers, mafia and all. Each time her father is the first on the scene. The newspapers are calling it the miracle of dharampur. The criminals confess that it is a little girl and an old guy with a walking stick that beat them up. But nobody believes them. THe reporters are calling them crazy. Akshi is making money out of these raids. Her father also gets promoted to head constable. 
*********

Baba damdam hears of this. This gets his interest and he asks someone to find the little girl and the old guy.

After one of their superhero activities. DAddu and Akshi have tied all the goons. Daddu is struggling. All these activities has had an adverse effect on his health. A nosy journalist clicks pictures of Akshi tying up the goons and taking care of daddu. A few boys 20-24 show up and respectfully ask them to come with them. Daddu is in no position to fight so they have to go with them. 
Daddu and Akshi are waiting in a room. Baba damdam shows up. He is excited to meet Daddu. Daddu has a faint memory of him. He doesn’t remember much. Baba is polite and grateful for the work daddu is doing. He tells Daddu that daddu saved him when he was 15. After that he looked everywhere for daddu, but couldn’t find him. But he had found his purpose. He had seen the power of gods in daddu. And since then he has carried his work forward by helping the needy and punishing evil. 
He asks them to join him. People send him complaints from all over the world of bad men making their lives miserable. THey need justice as well. They can at least start from their own country and get rid of evil. He tells them to take their time and think about it. 

*********** 
Daddu’s condition is getting worse. He thinks he won’t last much longer. He tells her that he has chosen her as the next superhero. He will soon transfer his powers to her. Akshi is excited by this and tells him that in that case they should accept Baba Damdam’s offer. This will give her practice to learn how to do superhero stuff. Daddu doesn’t believe in working under people. That’s the reason him and his predecessors never worked for kings and governments. Akshi somehow convinces him to accept Damdam’s offer. 
************

They bust a couple of smuggling rings in other cities. The same reporter who had taken their pictures,follows them everywhere and has taken loads of pictures. Their involvement becomes national news. Everyone wants to know who these people are. THe police is after them. But don’t have a clue. 


************
Akshi’s photographs have come out in the newspaper. Everybody thinks she is doing something to catch these goons. DOes she have some kind of power. Were the goons telling the truth about a little girl and an old man kicking their butts. All these questions are floating in the media. Akshi goes to school and everyone is looking at her differently. Some are scared of her, most want to know what is she doing? How is she beating all these villians? Student’s parents are crowding the school, asking her to solve their problems. THe media is outside her school. SHe has become famous. 
Her father is arrested in alleged involvement and she is taken into custody as well. Her father is charged with corruption and conspiracy. Akshi is interrogated to find out who is the other person with her. She refuses to tell. Because she is a minor, she is let go. 
Akshi goes to daddu for help. She wants him to break out her father. But daddu refuses as it is against the law. Baba damdam brings them over to his ashram. He tells them that he has gotten her dad out on bail. His top lawyers will be on the case and will ensure that nothing happens to her dad. Akshi is happy and grateful to damdam. Damdam then tells her that unfortunately, because of this she will have to recuse herself. 

************

The raid goes as planned. Baba damdam accompanies Daddu. He captures all the goons alone. Daddu doesn’t have to do anything. Daddu is quite impressed and asks him how is he so strong. He takes daddu to one of his ashrams and the good work they’ve been doing with the young boys and girls. Making them fitter. Giving them a purpose. He also shows Daddu his ayurvedic lab, where he makes ayurvedic protein powders and other medicines. He then takes him to another room, where he has a strength enhancer. He takes it everyday to increase his strength. He also gives it to his disciples. 

Later Damdam reveals to daddu that he knows that daddu wants to transfer his powers to Akshi. Daddu is surprised and asks how he knows this. Damdam reveals that 25 years ago, he was the boy Daddu had chosen to transfer his powers. HE was training him. But one day he just left. Without saying anything. Damdam searched for a long time, but couldn’t find him. After that he tried to spread daddu’s message of justice. 
He tells daddu that by giving Akshi these powers, he will make her life hell. She will have to make sacrifices that she doesn’t know about yet. She will never be able to live a normal life. Her life has already had so much chaos by the rumour of her having some powers. It will be even more difficult if daddu gives her this responsibility. Daddu realizes that this is true. He hasn’t this. Daddu remembers fragments of his time with damdam but can’t remember everything. He is suspicious of his intentions. He thinks that Damdam is saying all this because he wants the power for himself. Damdam tells daddu that this is not the case. He wants daddu to not transfer the power to anyone. AS he has demonstrated to him, he is carrying on the work on his behalf. He is trying to create a world where his power doesn’t exist. What if it falls in the wrong hands? What he wants from daddu is to keep his power to himself so that it dies when he dies. Daddu is surprised by this admission. He considers doing that. Goes to the librarian and tells her that he is considering this. The librarian tells him that the decision is upto him. She advices him against it, telling him that it is a divine power and meant to be passed on. It won’t let him die otherwise. He will live a miserable life aging till eternity till the power finds a new home. 


**********

Akshi is enjoying the popularity she is getting in the school. Everyone wants to know how she did it. How she caught so many bad guys. Akshi has no answer. She misses daddu. She hasn’t seen him for a few days now. She goes to his tent but he is not there. SHe calls Damdam. HIs secretary picks up and tells her that baba damdam is busy with a satsang. She wants to talk to daddu. But the secretary cuts the call. 

Akshi goes to the ashram to see daddu. SHe is intercepted by damdam. She blames him for trying to keep daddu away from her. Damdam assures her that he isn’t doing anything like this.

Episode 1:
Prologue: Something that happened years ago: Option 1: 15 years ago, someone escaped a high security establishment. Dangerous predicament. Action. 

Piddu: 15 years old. In class 10th. Her father is a police constable, mother works as nanny/maid. She is in a reputed school. All her classmates have rich parents. She doesn’t want to reveal that she is poor, so she has been lying to her classmates that her father is a high ranking senior police officer and her mother is an interior decorator. There is a school trip to the zoo where the students have to bring a parent/guardian with them. Piddu can’t get her father or mother. Her classmates will know she is poor and has been lying to everyone. 

Daddu: 80 years old. Frail and homeless. Begs for money, eats out of garbage. A vadapav guy sometimes gives him something to eat. People push him around, berate him etc. He doesn’t mind. Everyday, he tries to hug a tree, that he lives under and get on a platform to jump, get in front of moving cars. 
He lives around Piddu’s house. Piddu sees him and finds him weird. But she is curious. She also feels bad for him. 

She doesn’t want to eat her tiffin in front of his classmates. After school, she enters her colony and before going home, she eats her tiffin. Here she sees Daddu, feels bad for him and gives him some of her food. Daddu reveals, he is a superhero. Piddu realizes he is crazy, so doesn’t believe him. She asks him to show his powers, but he is unable to do so. 

Piddu has decided to tell her teacher no. A boy who she has a crush on also shows interest in her. He is looking forward to sees her at the zoo and spending some time with her. Piddu is escited, but she can’t go. 
Sequence: Her struggles with what to do about it. 
She finally decides to take daddu with her. Disguised as her real grandfather. She finds her fathers nicest clothes, gives Daddu a simple makeover. (Bath, clothes, shave and haircut etc)

At the zoo, her plans to be with the boy doesn’t work. She loses Daddu. Animal enclosure scene. Daddu saves her, revealing his superpowers. THe boy, Vibhor has also seen it. 
They make their way back home. 

 

***********

Episode 2:

Prologue: Daddu’s early history. Fighting something/someone/natural disaster. 
Piddu and daddu think about what to do with the superpower. They realize that he can’t use it at will. THey don’t know how it works. Piddu thinks that it will work only when it’s need arises. Like it did when Piddu was in danger. 

Piddu and Daddu decide to use his powers for something good. They don’t know what. Piddu has seen movies and read comic books. She thinks that superpowers should be used to fight crime. 

Need to introduce Jass Dhillon. Show him in good light. He is fielding questions maybe? Inaugurating a cancer research facility? 

Argument about secret identity. Daddu doesn’t want a secret identity. Piddu does. They compromise. Daddu goes as he is. Piddu goes in a mask. 
They try to find crime but can’t find any. They go to crowded places, cinema halls, malls. But can’t find any crime. THey get into trouble a few times mistaking things for crime. 3 times we’ll do this. 
They finally find someone stealing something and try to catch him. The thief has only stolen a toy. He is poor and can’t afford toys. So this is the best way to get it. They decide it is useless to look for crime like this. They should follow her father from now on, to find crime. They find small pickpockets, and people who pee on the side of the road.

  Piddu asks her father. He makes a joke about it. THey decide to follow him. Piddu’s father encounters criminals/bodyguards of an evil guy. 
Piddu and Daddu save the day. Piddu’s father gets the credit. 

Daddu has now used his powers twice. Each time he used it he got a flash of his past. He starts remembering stuff.
Where should we start? He maybe sees a village. Sees the village’s name or something. And decides to go there. 

Damdam finds out about it. Sends men to investigate. 


***************

Episode 3: 

Prologue:

Daddu has to go to the village. He doesn’t know how to find it. He has a few markers in mind. A statue. NAme of the village is damdar. They look it up. Piddu isn’t convinced they should go. Vibhor wants to know more about Daddu’s powers. Piddu wants to keep him away as much as possible. Otherwise her secret will be revealed.

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis: Structure & Beats
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]
> **Core Focus:** Earliest 24-beat structural treatment with prototype protagonists CG and BV; establishes the danger-only power manifestation rule.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Rajkumar and Nawaz. 
1. Living in the city for a 9 months. They have a individual social media accounts where they are in constant feud with each other. They try to one up each other, or bring each other down. They fight publically, calling each other names. Criticizing their posts. Both of them try to flex to each other. But in reality it’s all fake. Their posts are fake, their rivalry is fake. In fact they are old childhood friends. This is a strategy that they are employing to boost their following. 

2. They are not satisfied as they are not making any money. We see them make videos and posts of fake places. For example, they would put a huge poster of a beach behind them, wear swimming trunks and make videos. THey also make videos to defend people they are following like @Indian_Bhai - who does right wing type videos, @thesassygirl - does lifestyle and fashion videos (Rajkumar has a crush on her.) 

2. They don’t make any money. Rajkumar keeps on assuring Nawaz that soon they will get endorsements from brands, but he doesn’t believe it either. When Nawaz tells him that the villagers also told them that they will never become famous because they don’t have any talent, RAjkumar tells him that you don’t need talent to be famous nowadays. 

3. But they are getting desperate. THey are running out of money, and their parents are forcing them to come back. They had asked their parents to give them a year to prove themselves. IF they don’t become famous and start making money from it, they will come back. Rajkumar will get into farming and NAwaz will marry whatever girl his parents put in front of him. The year is about to end. They have only 3 months left.

4. They find out about this social media and influencer agency that is paying influencers to endorse things for a monthly payment. Rajkumar is deperate to join this agency. They both go and talk to the hiring manager, but the manager shoos them away. He doesn’t think they are popular enough to be hired for something like this. They will need at least 50K followers to get hired. 

5. Rajkumar knows that it is impossible to get that many followers this quickly. So, he decides to do something different. He starts picking up fights on social media. Diss people, abuse them and fight with them. He starts getting engagement and followers. But still not enough. 

4. They find out about this influencer agency that hires influencer and pays them on a monthly basis. 
4. A social media meme page called @SharoneStoner exposes them and 
4. A group called incognito is creating havoc around the world. It’s an anarchist group that makes videos wearing a joker mask, threatening corporations and politicians around the world. They are now after politicians and GHAI (Global health advancement Institute) who are pushing for a new vaccine to be mandated around the world. They have hacked emails from a politician Mickey Chump, Anuj Sahi and GHAI executives, that has created doubts over the vaccine. They have also found emails from a  notable scientist Dr Raja Pancho to the GHAI, where he has expressed doubts over the effectiveness of the vaccines and lack of testing. Soon after this Dr Raja PAncho has disappeared creating even more doubt in their minds. 
They have leaked the emails about these people taking money from a shell company to mandate the vaccines as soon as possible. But because of lack of evidence, nobody takes it seriously.

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]
> **Core Focus:** Full 8-episode pitch document capturing the complete narrative arc.

---

Logline: When an old homeless man with memory loss, claiming to be a superhero, saves a 16-year-old girl, she must help him awaken his powers and save him from powerful people out to capture him. 
   
   Genre: Comedy, superhero.
   
   Duration: 30 min. 8 episodes.
   
   Synopsis: Akshi is a 16-year-old girl who lives in a poor neighbourhood with her parents. Her father is a constable and his mother works as a nanny in rich people’s houses. Her father used to be a hero cop but had to take multiple demotions after he pissed off a senior official in police. 
   Akshi lives a normal life of a school girl, having crushes on boys and insecurities about her appearance. She is also ashamed of her financial condition and has told her classmates that her father is a high-ranking police officer and her mother is an internal decorator. Akshi has a strong distaste for authority and authority figures. She gets into fights with students and teachers in school whenever she sees someone misusing their powers and influence. She often gets suspended for her behaviour. Her parents try to teach her to be flexible and let things go, but Akshi is strong minded and stubborn. 
   Akshi meets a mysterious, old homeless guy living on the streets. She sees him doing strange things like climbing a platform and jumping (as if trying to fly), and hugging a huge peepal tree. She sees him being beaten up by street bullies and saves him. She finds out that the old guy has severe memory loss. He can’t remember things that happened to him before 25 years ago. He sleeps on the streets and carries a ‘jhola’ that has a binder with newspaper clippings of miracles of a damdar aadmi that have happened many many years ago. When asked about it he claims to be a superhero who has done all those things. Akshi takes as ramblings of an old man. 
   The old man has lived in this condition for over two decades now, so he has rules to live by. For example, Rule number 1: Always find food before doing anything at all. Rule number 2: Don’t steal, unless you can’t find food till 9 at night. Etc. Akshi doesn’t agree with these rules. She thinks it is wrong to steal in any condition. The old guy tells her that this is not the case. Sometimes things go so bad that if he doesn’t eat, he dies hungry. Akshi takes pity on him when she saves him from street bullies. And starts sharing food from her tiffin and makes him a temporary shelter made of tarpaulin. Lovingly, she starts calling him daddu. 
   Daddu is frustrated by his lack of memory, he can’t use his powers, so he questions whether he really is the superhero that the binder and the newspaper clippings says he is or has he made a story for himself. Things take a turn when Akshi is in trouble and the old guy saves her using his superpowers. He has not used his powers in 25 years, so this surprises and excites him. He tries to do it again, but he doesn’t know how to. Seeing his powers for herself, Akshi decides to help him awaken his powers. She thinks that his powers can be used to help the poor and needy against powerful people who use their influence and power to do bad things.
   This begins their journey to discover Daddu’s superpowers to help the poor and needy. They start discovering his past. Meeting people who have seen him in action in his youth. Their activities make them famous and bring them on the world’s Radar. People calling the duo Daddu Damdar. Daddu comes on the radar of billionaire moghul, Jass Dhillon and a godman baba Damdam. They both have a history with daddu, that we will uncover through the story. 
   They both have their own agenda with him and  send their men to find and capture him. Akshi and Daddu find out that the men after them are physically powerful and have the ability to capture Daddu. Daddu puts his life on the line to save Akshi and is almost about to get captured. But Akshi is stubborn and somehow manages to save daddu. Now, they have to go on a run to hide from these people. 
   Akshi decides that if they want to find more about daddu’s life and why these people are after him they will need to go to the places mentioned in the newspaper clippings. They go on a journey to find out about Daddu’s history through towns and villages where daddu had saved and helped people many many years ago. We also find out that daddu’s superpowers are ancient and have been passed on generation to generation. Daddu also has to transfer his powers to someone he deems worthy.
   
   We also reveal that Jass Dhillon wants to extract daddu’s powers for himself. He had captured daddu 25 years ago, to do experiments on him and has created a pharma empire from synthesizing his blood and creating performance enhancing drugs. He also has a special drug made out of daddu’s blood that gives people tremendous physical powers, but only for 20-30 minutes. He uses these drugs to fight and capture daddu. 
   We also see that baba damdam is a sanyasi physical trainer, who gives ‘pravachan’ while excercising on stage. He is tryng to create the next generation of physically fit, morally strong men and women. WE reveal that Baba damdam was the guy chosen by daddu 25 years ago as his successor. When daddu disappeared, damdam started his own ashram to teach physical education and daddu’s ethics of saving people in need. 
   Damdam has a secret agenda. He thinks he is now too old to receive daddu’s powers. So, he has created an army of morally strong men and women for daddu to choose from to transfer his powers. Akshi doesn’t agree. She thinks that no one should get this power. It will eventually corrupt whoever gets it, like how any kind of power corrupts people. 
    Will Daddu get his memory back and find out how he helped the world? Will Akshi be able to save Daddu from Jass Dhillon? Will damdam be able to provide a successor to Daddu?
    
   Episodic brief:
   
   Ep1: Introdcution to Akshi and Daddu. Revelation of Daddu’s superpowers. 
   
   Ep2: Introduction of Jass Dhillon and Baba Damdam. Akshi and Daddu trying to figure out how to use the powers. 
   
   Ep3: Akshi and Daddu decide to use the powers for good. They fight local gundas creating problems for the poor bastiwaalas.  At first daddu fails, but eventually Akshi helps him figure out how to use his powers. We see a hint of Jass and Damdam’s history with daddu.
   
   Ep4: Akshi and Daddu become famous. Their deeds are in the newspapers. Jass and Damdam find out and send their men to bring him to them. Akshi and Daddu’s confrontation with the men. They find that these goons are super powerful and have to run from them. 
   
   Ep5: Akshi and Daddu run from the goons. They go to places in daddu’s binder to find out more about his past. They meet people who daddu has helped many years ago. THey can’t seen to find what happened 25 years ago that daddu had to stop. 
   
   Ep6: Daddu and Akshi are captured by Damdam. We reveal that damdam is a good guy. He was the successor chosen by Daddu many years ago. But he has a secret agenda. An attack happens at Damdam’s ashram. Daddu is captured by Jass Dhillon and taken to his secret lab. 
   
   Ep7: Akshi and Damdam plan to free daddu. The story of what happened to daddu 25 years ago is revealed. Jass had performed experiments on him and created new performance enhancing drugs to build his vast empire. Akshi infiltrates Jass’s lab to free Daddu, but both of them are trapped. 
   
   Ep8: Akshi and Daddu are trapped. Damdam comes to save them. Akshi is able to free daddu. But she dies when she goes back to destroy all the samples collected by Jass Dhillon. Daddu saves her by transferring his powers to her.

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis: Series Pitches
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]
> **Core Focus:** First unified 8-episode series pitch outlining the 25-year memory gap, newspaper binder clippings, and the Jass Dhillon/Baba Damdam threats.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Logline: When an old homeless man with memory loss, claiming to be a superhero, saves a 16-year-old girl, she must help him awaken his powers and save him from powerful people out to capture him. 

Genre: Comedy, superhero.

Duration: 30 min. 8 episodes.

Synopsis: Akshi is a 16-year-old girl with a strong moral sense. She gets into fights with students and teachers in school whenever she sees something wrong happening. SHe often gets suspended for her behavior. Her parents try to teach her to be flexible and let things go, but Akshi is strong minded and stubborn. She meets an old homeless guy, being beaten up by street bullies and saves him. THe old guy has severe memory loss. He can’t remember things that happened to him before 25 years ago. He sleeps on the streets and carries a ‘jhola’ that has a binder with newspaper clippings of miracles that have happened in the past 50 years. 
The also claims to be a superhero, which Akshi takes as ramblings of an old man. Frustrated by his lack of memory and believes that he will die without making any difference in the world. The old guy is She takes pity on him, and takes him under his wing. Protecting him from street gundas and providing him food and a temporary shelter made of tarpaulin. She starts calling him daddu. Things take a turn when Akshi is in trouble and the old guy saves her using his superpowers. Even he doesn’t know how to use them. 
This begins their journey to discover his superpowers to help the poor and needy. They start discovering his past. Their actions make them famous and bring them on the world’s Radar. People calling the duo Daddu Damdar. This includes a billionaire tech moghul, Jass Dhillon and a godman baba Damdam. They both have a history with daddu and their own agenda. 
They both have their own agenda with him and want to find him and send their men after them. Akshi and Daddu find out that the men after them are physically powerful and have the ability to capture Daddu. She wants to save daddu, so they go on a run and find out daddu’s history as they travel through towns and villages where daddu had saved and helped people many many years ago.
Eventually we find out that Jass Dhillon wants to extract daddu’s powers for himself. He had captured daddu 25 years ago, to do experiments on him and has created a pharma empire from synthesizing his blood. We also find out that Baba damdam was the guy chosen by daddu 25 years ago as his successor. We reveal that daddu’s superpowers are ancient and have been passed on generation to generation. Daddu also has to transfer his powers to someone he deems worthy. 
Will Akshi be able to save Daddu from Jass Dhillon? Will baba Damdam be able to become Daddu’s successor? Will Daddu get his memory back and find out how he helped the world?
 
Episodic brief:

Ep1: Introdcution to Akshi and Daddu. Revelation of Daddu’s superpowers. 

Ep2: Introduction of Jass Dhillon and Baba Damdam. Jass and Daddu trying to figure out how to use the powers. 

Ep3: Jass and Daddu decide to use the powers for good. They fight local gundas creating problems for the poor bastiwaalas.  At first daddu fails, but eventually Akshi helps him figure out how to use his powers. We see a hint of Jass and Damdam’s history with daddu.

Ep4: Akshi and Daddu become famous. Their deeds are in the newspapers. Jass and Damdam find out and send their men to bring him to them. Akshi and Daddu’s confrontation with the men. They find that these goons are super powerful and have to run from them. 

Ep5: Akshi and Daddu run from the goons. They go to places in daddu’s binder to find out more about his past. THey meet people who daddu has helped many years ago. THey can’t seen to find what happened 25 years ago that daddu had to stop. 

Ep6: Daddu and Akshi are captured by Damdam. We reveal that damdam is a good guy. He was the successor chosen by Daddu many years ago. But he has a secret agenda. An attack happens at Damdam’s ashram. Daddu is captured by Jass Dhillon and taken to his secret lab. 

Ep7: Akshi and Damdam plan to free daddu. The story of what happened to daddu 25 years ago is revealed. Jass had performed experiments on him and created new performance enhancing drugs to build his vast empire. Akshi infiltrates Jass’s lab to free Daddu, but both of them are trapped. 

Ep8: Akshi and Daddu are trapped. Damdam comes to save them. Akshi is able to free daddu. But she dies when she goes back to destroy all the samples collected by Jass Dhillon. Daddu saves her by transferring his powers to her.

---

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis: Series Pitches
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]

---

Logline: When an old homeless man with memory loss, claiming to be a superhero, saves a 16-year-old girl, she must help him awaken his powers and save him from powerful people out to capture him. 

Genre: Comedy, superhero.

Duration: 30 min. 8 episodes.

Synopsis: Akshi is a 16-year-old girl who lives in a poor neighborhood with her parents. Her father is a constable and his mother works as a nanny in rich people’s houses. Her father used to be a hero cop but had to take multiple demotions after he pissed off a senior official in police. 
Akshi lives a normal life of a school girl, having crushes on boys and insecurities about her appearance. She is also ashamed of her financial condition and has told her classmates that her father is a high ranking police officer and her mother is an internal decorator. Akshi has a strong distaste for authority and authority figures. She gets into fights with students and teachers in school whenever she sees someone misusing their powers and influence. She often gets suspended for her behavior. Her parents try to teach her to be flexible and let things go, but Akshi is strong minded and stubborn. 
Akshi meets a mysterious, old homeless guy living on the streets. She sees him doing strange things like climbing a platform and jumping (as if trying to fly), and hugging a huge peepal tree. She sees him being beaten up by street bullies and saves him. She finds out that the old guy has severe memory loss. He can’t remember things that happened to him before 25 years ago. He sleeps on the streets and carries a ‘jhola’ that has a binder with newspaper clippings of miracles of a dmamdar aadmi that have happened many many years ago. When asked about it he claims to be a superhero who has done all those things. Akshi takes as ramblings of an old man. 
The old man has lived in this condition for over two decades now, so he has rules to live by. For example, Rule number 1: Always find food before doing anything at all. Rule number 2: Don’t steal, unless you can’t find food till 9 at night. Etc. Akshi doesn’t agree with these rules. She thinks it is wrong to steal in any condition. The old guy tells her that this is not the case. Sometimes things go so bad that if he doesn’t eat, he dies hungry. Akshi takes pity on him when she saves him from street bullies. And starts sharing food from her tiffin and makes him a temporary shelter made of tarpaulin. Lovingly, she starts calling him daddu. 
Daddu is frustrated by his lack of memory, he can’t use his powers, so he questions whether he really is the superhero that the binder and the newspaper clippings says he is or has he made a story for himself. Things take a turn when Akshi is in trouble and the old guy saves her using his superpowers. He has not used his powers in 25 years, so this surprises and excites him. He tries to do it again, but he doesn’t know how to. Seeing his powers for herself, Akshi decides to help him awaken his powers. She thinks that his powers can be used to help the poor and needy against powerful people who use their influence and power to do bad things.
This begins their journey to discover Daddu’s superpowers to help the poor and needy. They start discovering his past. Meeting people who have seen him in action in his youth. Their activities make them famous and bring them on the world’s Radar. People calling the duo Daddu Damdar. Daddu comes on the radar of billionaire moghul, Jass Dhillon and a godman baba Damdam. They both have a history with daddu, that we will uncover through the story. 
They both have their own agenda with him and  send their men to find and capture him. Akshi and Daddu find out that the men after them are physically powerful and have the ability to capture Daddu. Daddu puts his life on the line to save Akshi and is almost about to get captured. But Akshi is stubborn and somehow manages to save daddu. Now, they have to go on a run to hide from these people. 
Akshi decides that if they want to find more about daddu’s life and why these people are after him they will need to go to the places mentioned in the newspaper clippings. They go on a journey to find out about Daddu’s history through towns and villages where daddu had saved and helped people many many years ago. We also find out that daddu’s superpowers are ancient and have been passed on generation to generation. Daddu also has to transfer his powers to someone he deems worthy.

We also reveal that Jass Dhillon wants to extract daddu’s powers for himself. He had captured daddu 25 years ago, to do experiments on him and has created a pharma empire from synthesizing his blood and creating performance enhancing drugs. He also has a special drug made out of daddu’s blood that gives people tremendous physical powers, but only for 20-30 minutes. He uses these drugs to fight and capture daddu. 
We also see that baba damdam is a sanyasi physical trainer, who gives ‘pravachan’ while excercising on stage. He is tryng to create the next generation of physically fit, morally strong men and women. WE reveal that Baba damdam was the guy chosen by daddu 25 years ago as his successor. When daddu disappeared, damdam started his own ashram to teach physical education and daddu’s ethics of saving people in need. 
Damdam has a secret agenda. He thinks he is now too old to receive daddu’s powers. So, he has created an army of morally strong men and women for daddu to choose from to transfer his powers. Akshi doesn’t agree. She thinks that no one should get this power. It will eventually corrupt whoever gets it, like how any kind of power corrupts people. 
 Will Daddu get his memory back and find out how he helped the world? Will Akshi be able to save Daddu from Jass Dhillon? Will damdam be able to provide a successor to Daddu?
 
Episodic brief:

Ep1: Introdcution to Akshi and Daddu. Revelation of Daddu’s superpowers. 

Ep2: Introduction of Jass Dhillon and Baba Damdam. Akshi and Daddu trying to figure out how to use the powers. 

Ep3: Akshi and Daddu decide to use the powers for good. They fight local gundas creating problems for the poor bastiwaalas.  At first daddu fails, but eventually Akshi helps him figure out how to use his powers. We see a hint of Jass and Damdam’s history with daddu.

Ep4: Akshi and Daddu become famous. Their deeds are in the newspapers. Jass and Damdam find out and send their men to bring him to them. Akshi and Daddu’s confrontation with the men. They find that these goons are super powerful and have to run from them. 

Ep5: Akshi and Daddu run from the goons. They go to places in daddu’s binder to find out more about his past. They meet people who daddu has helped many years ago. THey can’t seen to find what happened 25 years ago that daddu had to stop. 

Ep6: Daddu and Akshi are captured by Damdam. We reveal that damdam is a good guy. He was the successor chosen by Daddu many years ago. But he has a secret agenda. An attack happens at Damdam’s ashram. Daddu is captured by Jass Dhillon and taken to his secret lab. 

Ep7: Akshi and Damdam plan to free daddu. The story of what happened to daddu 25 years ago is revealed. Jass had performed experiments on him and created new performance enhancing drugs to build his vast empire. Akshi infiltrates Jass’s lab to free Daddu, but both of them are trapped. 

Ep8: Akshi and Daddu are trapped. Damdam comes to save them. Akshi is able to free daddu. But she dies when she goes back to destroy all the samples collected by Jass Dhillon. Daddu saves her by transferring his powers to her.

================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis: Scene Drafting
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]
> **Core Focus:** Daddu's earliest memories: breaking out of an exploding lab, waking in an impact crater like a fallen asteroid, and swallowing pride to eat leftover rotis.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Piddu: What will she become. She will have to start from the opposite end. 
What does she want?
World peace. To change the world. To get somthing done. Something specific.
Should she have a cause. Like One single mission. To save someone. Or to get justice for something. 
One single mission:
She wants to get her dad promoted. Maybe she wants something for herself. Not a cause then.
How will she go about getting her dad promotion if she had a superhero?
One of the plans I initially thought was catching bad guys. Getting his father to the scene to make the arrest. Her father gets all the accolades. Seems like a reasonable plan. 

Let’s say this is the case. Then Daddu needs to have a want as well. Hopefully opposing. He is trying to find his identity. Maybe he has been looking for it for a while. He broke away from the lab 25 years ago. When he came out, he didn’t remember anything. But he had a powers. He remembers flying away. He remembers falling to the ground clearing trees and creating a small crater as if an asteroid had fallen. He remembers this and thinks that he has powers. But he can’t use them because he doesn’t know how to. 
He wanted to find out more about himself. Who was he? Where did he come from? Did he really have powers? Was that a hallucination? 
For the first few days, he didn’t know what to do. Scared of what might happen. He went back to the crater. HE looked around to find a huge structure, completely burned down. He didn’t know if this was the lab or not. He went inside to check. Everything had been burned out. He looked around 

He was tired and weak. He wandered around the town wearing dirty and torn white overalls. Looking like a beggar. He hadn’t eaten for a few days now. He thought about asking someone for some money or food. But couldn’t bring himself to do it. Although he didn’t remember anything, he still had some pride left in him. 
But this broke too. Having not eaten for days, he collapsed on the ground in the middle of the market. Ongoing pedestrians thought of him as a homeless beggar, threw food and money at him. He survived eating the leftover rotis. He discarded his ego and decided this to be his way of living. He found a bag in the garbage along with other things that he would carry from town to town. Living off of kindness of strangers. If that didn’t work he had to beg. If that didn’t work he had to pick stuff out of the garbage. 
He was living an aimless life. He didn’t know who he was. People would call him “oye”, “buddhe”, “baba”. He didn’t name himself anything. He had tried to remember but couldn’t remember anything. He remembered fragments of memory. Couldn’t make sense of it at all. He remembered a big lab of some kind. He remembers an explosion. He remembers his flying out. He remembers crashing into a tree that broke in half. THen he remembers waking up to see that tree broken.

DOCUMENT: Daddu_Damdar/Raw_Archive/Scene_Draft_Daddu_Auditory_Compass_Indore.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis: Scene Drafting
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]
> **Core Focus:** Daddu wandering for 25 years following a loud ringing voice in his head; encountering the locked white shutter in Indore and faking burglary.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Memories are strange things. Things we remember exactly as they happened may not be the way someone else might remember it. Details change, perspectives shift, and suddenly the main character is someone else. Sometimes you build your own narrative around how things happened. Fill gaps that you don’t remember with things that give shape to that narrative. Making you the hero of the memory. 
So many things happen to us throughout our lives. We don’t remember most of them. Humans forget more than we remember. Our brains are wired to filter out useless information. Memories and details that are not used over time. Even everyday details are forgotten. It’s a great credit to our species that we’ve managed to remember and pass on so much crucial knowledge. Writing is a crucial part of it. Writing and passing off knowledge. If someone is in the habit of forgetting then a system must be devised for them to remember things.
Imagine if a superhero forget who he was. Will he even remember that he was a superhero? He will go through his life trying to find out who he was before he forgot. There hasn’t been anyone who could come and tell him. No one to guide him. He will be alone. Wandering the cities and villages. Where would he even start? What does he remember? If there is a sense of duty in him. Saving people. Stopping crime. Would he go about doing that even if he doesn’t have powers? At some point it would be a hindrance to him. Getting beaten up, insulted, pushed and shoved. Would he not question his sanity? Would he not start thinking he isn’t who he thinks he is? A superhero. There is no evidence of him being that. Then how does he know? Has he done anything recently that suggests that he is a superhero. 
Maybe he remembers parts of his previous life. Maybe he remembers just enough. Maybe he isn’t quite sure whether what he remembers are his memories or his dreams. He has a calling. An instinct that tells him that he is supposed to do something. A voice inside his head that tells him that he is meant to save people. What if there was a voice. Why isn’t the voice telling him about himself? The voice only tells him about what he needs to do.
He travels from city to city to perform his duties. If he doesn’t do anything the voice becomes louder and louder. What does the voice tell him? The voice might tell him to do something. It has to be specific. Does it have to be instructive? Prescriptive? The voice tells him to go places. Maybe the voice has directed him for the past 25 years. Taken him from city to city. 
He was first asked to go to Indore. He was directed to a place where a robbery was happening. HE was supposed to stop it. The voice took him there and stopped. He didn’t know what to do. He tried to stop the robbers, but one of them stabbed him. He died. Only for a few minutes. He woke up with pain in his stomach and the robbers gone. The wound pained him, but it vanished in a few hours. He was all okay. This made him believe he was supposed to save people. Help them. Stop evil in whatever form he found it. He began his journey. 
The voice kept him in Indore for a while. It stopped telling him to do things. Then one day he was told to go to a place with a white shutter. He didn’t listen to it. The voice got louder and louder till he thought it would blow his brains to smithereens. He had to listen to it. He went to the white shutter. It was locked. HE didn’t know what to do. Thee voice also stopped. He looked around. There was nothing there to save. He decided to go back. But the voice became louder and unbearable till he turned back.
He stood at the shutter for a while. Thinking what to do. He considered breaking the lock. He applied his full strength, but he couldn’t summon it. It was like his powers were gone. He didn’t know what else to do. His attempts attracted the attention of a couple of people. They came to inquire. Our superhero made up a silly excuse. “Mai chori kar raha tha.” He said. Now knowing what else to say. It made him feel stupid. “Mai kuch bhi bol sakta tha. Koi aur bahana jaise…” He thought for a little while, but no other excuse came to his mind. He decided that the only idea is by default the best idea. He stood waiting for the consequences. He was prepared to fight them. 
The men were surprisingly calm. They scanned him from head to toe. A damdar brand hoodie. Dirty and torn at the shoulder. A plain Tshirt and trousers too short for him. They told him to leave and not come back and they won’t report him to the police. 
The superhero thinks about it. It’s a good deal. He thanked them profusely and turned to leave. But as soon as he took a step, his ear started ringing again. The voice was a shriek now. He turned and it stopped. THe men had seen enough. They warned him again. He pleaded that even if he wants to go, he can’t. The superhero suggests to call the police. That way they can arrest him and take him away from this place. The men suddenly tense up. THey don;t like the idea of calling the police. They tell him that instead of the police they will beat him and teach him a lesson. 
The superhero warns them not to get aggressive. He fears for their well being and tells them to back off. This seems to amuse and anger the men and they beat the superhero up. The superhero hits them with all his force but it barely make a difference. The superhero is lying on the ground at the end. Beaten up. The men pick him up and throw him a few blocks away.
The voice tells him to follow them. After arguing with it for a few seconds, he is forced to do as told. The men go around the warehouse complex and enter the building through the back door. The superhero follows. 
It’s dark inside. Stuff stored and stacked haphazardly everywhere. It looks like people’s discarded stuff. Racks, old television sets, tattered charpais. The men can’t be seen anywhere. He goes forward and sees an old rifle.

---

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis: Scene Drafting
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]
> **Core Focus:** Establishing Akshi's working-class family (clerk father, maid mother), the fake IPS officer lie, and the hospital dementia diagnosis.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

1. Akshi Gupta is a 16-year-old girl, lives in a small house with her mom and dad. Her mother works as a maid in people’s houses and her father is low level clerk in a government job. Although they don’t earn too much, they save money to send Akshi to a private school. Both of them struggle to make ends meet. Akshi is in a school filled with rich kids. SHe feels out of place. She doesn’t have too many friends here, neither does she have enough money. She does side hustles to make some pocket money. She sells cigarettes and alcohol to boys in school, charges money for forging the principal’s signature on other kids permission slips. She also teaches karate to kids in the evening. She has one friend, Naomi Joseph. Akshi has a crush on a boy in her class, Vibhor Bora. He is a handsome jock. Dumb but sweet. She doesn’t have the courage to talk to him. 
She sees one of the boys she signed the permission slip for, harassing a girl in the school. She tells him to stop and walk away, but the boy refuses. They get into a fight. She is brought to the principal’s office where the boy rats her out for forging signatures. She gets suspended for 2 weeks. She is scared that her parents will be disappointed with the suspension. So she doesn’t tell them. 
Next day, she pretends to go to school, but goes to a park near her house. She is thinking of ways to pass the time when she sees this old homeless person across the street, living in a house made of tarpaulin and sticks. She sees him behave strangely. Hugging a tree as if trying to pull it.  is doing it again and again. Failing but now stopping. When not doing this, he is checking out people passing by. Sometimes getting too close to sniff them. She sees that he is tired and exhausted. She shares her tiffin with him and asks what he was doing. He looks at her suspiciously and gives her cagey answers. I was exercising, I live that tree. Akshi can clearly see that the old guy can barely walk straight. This becomes her routine. She goes everyday to the park and meets the old guy, sharing her tiffin. The old guy is also opening up. Akshi asks what he does everyday, hugging the tree and jumping off of a wall. He tells her that he is really exercising. He is a superhero, but because of old age, he is not able to do things that he used to, like flying. His strength is also decreasing. Akshi doesn’t believe him and thinks he is crazy. SHe decides not to engage with the old man anymore. 

2. It’s sunday. Akshi is at home. She has lunch with her mom and dad. She remembers that the old guy wouldn’t have eaten anything. She decides to save some food and wraps it up. She goes to see the old guy. But there are a few boys beating on him. Akshi runs to them and tells them to leave the old guy alone. The boys laugh it off and keep beating the old guy. Akshi karate chops one and kicks the other. The boys turn their attention towards her and grab her. The old guy gets up and shouts at them to stop or he will beat them. He shouts at Akshi to run. Akshi gets free from the boys and runs away. She is hysterical. She could have gotten severely injured. She remembers the old guy. She runs to the road and finds a constable there and takes him to the old guy. 
She reaches there with the constable. The boys are gone. The old guy is on the floor beaten and battered. He is in a fugue state. Not knowing where he is. Alshi takes him to a government hospital, where his wounds are attended to. The doctor tells her that the old guy has dementia. Akshi is worried about him. THe old guy tells her not to worry. He will be fine. He just needs to finish his one last duty as the superhero. Akshi takes it as ramblings of a demented man and takes him to his place. Fixes his tarpaulin house, makes it more comfortable for him. 

3. Akshi’s suspension is over. She goesback to school to find out that there is a school trip in 2 days and she has to get a parent of a guadian to go. Vibhor talks to her for the first time and asks if she is coming to the school trip. SHe says yes. She goes home to ask her parents, but they refuse. They can’t take a leave from their jobs. Akshi has an idea. She decides to take the old guy as her grandfather. She gives him a haircut and a shave and some clothes stolen from a neighbor. She takes him tot he zoo as his daddu. 
At the zoo, she gathers some courage to talk to Vibhor, who has come with his older brother Kuber. AS they strike up a conversation, daddu pops out. Interrupting them. Akshi tries to avoid him but he is everywhere. Finally she takes him to an enclosure and sneaks away from him. She talks to Vibhor and they have a good time. At the end of the day, the zoo has emptied. The students are leaving. Vibhor asks Akshi about daddu. Akshi panics. She runs to find him. Vibhor and his brother come with her. She goes to the moose enclosure where she left him. She sees that he is inside the enclosure in his fugue state. Trying to recognize things. Akshi tells Vibhor that Daddu has dementia. Vibhor is now full on panicking seeing this. Akshi decides to go inside the enclosure and get daddu. The moose is nowhere to be seen. She gets into the enclosure and walks quietly. Kuber starts recording. As Akshi is walking, she makes a little noise. The moose comes. She freezes. The moose sees daddu and goes towards him. To save him, Akshi gets the moose attention and runs away. The moose runs after her. He is almost about to hit AKshi. But in another second she is out of the enclosure. Daddu has saved her. Akshi is in shock. What daddu was saying was true. HE is a superhero. 

4. They take daddu to Vibhor’s house.

---
