DOCUMENT: Daddu_Damdar/Raw_Archive/Scene_Draft_Zoo_Moose_Enclosure_Rescue.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis: Scene Drafting
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]
> **Core Focus:** Developing Akshi as a suspended classroom rebel; the fake grandfather chaperone idea, and the zoo moose enclosure rescue.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Act 1: 1-3 Set up || 4-6 stakes
Act 2: 7-12 success - 12 is midpoint. Disillusionment.
Proactive. Strength
Act 3: 13-18 Downward spiral. - Broken, lost all hope, sabotaged.
Reactive. Weaknesses
18 - Forced to see a secret path that they couldn’t see before. Often something that their character flaw prevented them to do. (Enlightenment). They make a clear, extremely risky plan. (Final strategy)
Act 4: 19-24 Transformation.
Emotional truth. Embracing the truth. 
Stakes should staring be right in the face.
All wants replaced with needs. All fears are faced. All lies revealed. Every choice leads to a single Climax.
22-24 - New world. Character or world has changed. New perspective.  Or adapted in a unexpected way. 
*********************
1. Akshi is like the gunda of the class. She sells cigarettes to boys in class. Netflix subscriptions - one time payment. She has one friend, Naomi Joseph. SHe has a crush on a boy in her class, Vibhor Bora. He is a handsome jock. Dumb but sweet. She can’t seem to be her confident rebellious self around the guy. She sees a boy harrassing a girl in the school. She gets into a fight. Gets punished, suspended. She is happy with the suspension as she thinks it is going to be fun time. 
When she gets home, her parents scold her for being so irresponsible. Then go back to their lives. THey are not very attentive parents. She is usually left on her own. So this time as well, she has to spend time alone at home. Her father has made donations to the school that’s why they haven’t rusticated her. They’ve taken away her credit cards as punishments. She calls a couple of her friends to hang out, but they are disappointed in her for using violence. She is stuck at home. She sees this old homeless person across the street, living in a house made of tarpaulin and sticks. She sees him behave strangely. Trying to uproot a tree with his bare hands. Trying to jump off of a wall and falling flat on his face  is doing it again and again. Failing but now stopping. She talks to him and observes that he zones out from time to time and calls himself a superhero. He tells her that he is building his strength by trying to uprooot the tree and learning to fly. She thinks he is a crazy guy and decides to stay away. . 

2. One day, she is walking around her neighborhood. She sees the old guy practice his jumps off the wall. She stops to observe. A few boys come around and make fun of the old guy. They push him around. He warns them not to bully him, or it will turn ugly for them. They laugh at him and destroy his house. This angers Akshi and she decides to butt in. She tells off the young boys, who now turn their attention to her. She fights a couple of them off, but they over power her. The older guy now puts his torn coat as a cape and tells the boys to get away from the girl, or he will have to do it himself. THe boys laugh at him and decide to finish the job by beating him up. The old guy has an episode of complete memory loss. He gets beaten up pretty badly. The old guy doesn’t remember where he is or who he is. Beaten and battered. Akshi takes him to the hospital to get him checked. 


3. Impetus>> Akshi develops an affection towards the old man. She makes him comfortable. Gives him her tent and a water cooler. She keeps an eye on him to make sure he is fine. Her suspension is also over. She is excited to go back to school. She wants to talk to Vibhor and ask him out on a date. She goes to school, but in the two weeks that she was suspended, the girl she saved has started dating Vibhor. She thinks that there is no benefit from doing good for someone. It always bites you back in the ass. Even helping this old man might come back to haunt her. She decides to not engage with the old man anymore. 
The old guy greets her every time he sees her pass by, but Akshi doesn’t respond to him. He carries on his daily routine of trying to uproot the tree. She feels sorry for him. One day there is a school trip to a zoo. One of their parents of guradians have to chaperon them. Akshi wants a chance to get close to Vibhor, but she can’t take her parents. She doesn’t even want to ask, because she knows they will be too busy. The night before the school trip, it’s all but decided that she will not be able to go. Then an idea pops into her head. She can dress the old guy up and call him her grandfather, her daddu. She cleans him up, gives him a shave and buys him a walker. 
At the zoo, she is looking for any chance to talk to the guy. Get close to him. She introduces her daddu. Daddu is cockblocking her all the way. Whenever she gets a chance to talk to vibhor, who has come with his older brother, Daddu pops out of nowhere and ruin their conversation. Akshi has to give him a slip to get some alone time with Vibhor. She ahs a good time with Vibhor. He is sweet and funny in a dumb way. She finds out that Vibhor is not really dating that girl. He just went on a couple of dates, and she is too smart for him. She makes him feel dumber than he really is. As they are walking around the zoo, most of the students have gone home. The teachers tell the students left to be with their guardians as they are also now leaving. Vibhor calls his brother on his mobile to meet him at the gate. Vibhor asks that he hasn’t seen Daddu anywhere around. Akshi realizes that he is an old demented person and he could be in danger. She and Vibhor look for him around the zoo, but can’t find him. A woman spots an old man in one of the enclosures. Akshi already knows who it’s going to be as she turns to look. Of course, no surprises there, it’s Daddu. She screams at him to get back. But he is in his fugue state, where he is just trying to recognize things around him. It’s a moose enclosure. Akshi and Vibhor scream at daddu but he doesn’t move. When Akshi sees that Daddu isn’t listening, she climbs in to bring him back herself. Her jump crackles a trig and a moose hears it at some distance and decides to check. Akshi runs towards Daddu, but sees the moose and stops. She remembers a tour guide telling them that moose have poor vision, so the key for a lion to kill it to stay still. The moose only sees movement. THe moose comes to check even closer. Vibhor is shouting at her to run back, but she doesn’t move at all. Daddu picks the worst moment to come to his senses, he sees Akshi, waves and calls out to her. The moose runs towards him. Akshi seeing this screams to get the moose’s attention and runs back. The moose sees her run and runs towards her. Daddu sees Akshi telling him to run the other way and save himself. The moose quickly gains up on Akshi and is about to hit her. Akshi stops as she is out of breath. She puts her hand up and shouts at the moose to stop. The moose actually stops. Moose looks at her with curiosity, but as she is catching her breath back, the moose is about to get aggressive again. Akshi knows this is the end. Suddenly something catches her and in a second she is out of the enclosure. She is both scared and dumbfounded to what happened. She catches her breath finally to see that it’s Daddu. Mind blown. His superpowers are real. Vibhor and his brother are gobsmacked. Vibhor’s brother, Kuber, has recorded everything on his phone. A couple of other people have seen it too, so Akshi quickly tells everyone, they should leave. 
 
4. They all come back to Akshi’s house. Kuber is losing his mind seeing the video again and again. Akshi can’t believeit and tries to rationalize, but Kuber shows him the video. In the video, Daddu notices Akshi in trouble, and in a blur runs towards her picks her up, while pushing the moose, jumps the fence and lands in a heroic manner. Vibhor is confused, why does Akshi’s grandfather have superpowers. Akshi tells them the truth. Daddu is a homeless old man who lives across the street. Daddu tells them that he told Akshi he was a superhero. Vihor and Kuber ask him all these questions about his superpowers, and his adventures. Daddu tells them that he doesn’t remember anything. He only remembers bits and parts of his old life. They decide to test his superpowers, but he is really weak, he can’t lift a table. They try to make him run fast, but he can only walk using a walker. They don’t understand how this is happening. Akshi thinks it might be because of his memory loss. If they could find more about his earlier life then maybe he could remember and get his powers back. Vibhor and Kuber take up the challenge of finding out more about Daddu, from old news or something. If he was a superhero, there must be some articles about him. They can not find much on google. They also put it up on social media if someone remembers seeing a superhero, or had an experience with superpowers. 
In the meanwhile, they also decide to start his training. They take him to a gym, where he is useless. Can barely pick up 5 kg dumbbells. His walker doesn’t fit the treadmill and so on. THey aren’t having much success. They are getting a lot of responses from people on social media, but they are mostly useless. 

5. Daddu spends his time either with Akshi and her friends, or at his tent. Vibhor gets a response from one of his social media followers, whose mom had seen something 40 years ago. He gets her number and goes to meet Akshi. Akshi goes to meet Daddu. The boys who had beaten Daddu earlier come to bully him again. Daddu tells Akshi to run and get help. But Akshi stays to fight back. The boys make fun of them and beat up Daddu again. Akshi yells at Daddu to use his superpowers. The boys think that Akshi has also gone mad. They beat her up. Daddu sees this and in anger gets up and beats up the boys. Akshi seeing that allt he boys are down, tries to stop daddu, but he is in rage. He takes out his anger on one particular boy and is about to hit him really hard, which would kill the boy for certain. Akshi finally manages to stop him, but she is shocked by this incident. She goes home, where Vibhor is waiting for her. He tells Akshi about the lead he has. They call the woman together. Who tells them taht she had seen a man. She describes him exactly like a younger Daddu. She tells them that he is not a superhero, but a supervillian. He killed 20 school girls by drowning their school bus. Together with the incident earlier and the woman’s story, Akshi starts doubting Daddu.

6. Dramatic question>> Is daddu a superhero or a supervillian? Good guy or bad guy? What will they do to find out? Vibhor rationalizes the woman’s story, that she might have been mistaken. Vibhor thinks that Daddu should fight crime or something if he has the powers. Akshi is not sure if that is a good idea. Daddu is into the idea of fighting crime. THis sense of purpose is the only thing he properly remembers. He knows that his powers are only to help people. Akshi gets convinced. But they still have to figure out how Daddu’s superpowers work sometimes but not always. 
**********************
7. They think that both times when Daddu showed his powers was to save Akshi. Maybe, his powers come back when he has to help someone. They decide to test this by finding trouble. They go to a shady part of town and try to find trouble, but they see nothing. Finally they find a few gundas harrassing girls. Daddu goes on his walker to confront the gundas. THe gundas laugh at him and push him away. Daddu retaliates and weakly slaps one of the gundas. The gunda gets angry and beats him up. He takes pity on the old man and lets him go. Daddu comes back beaten. Akshi thinks this was a mistake, they should go and get daddu to a hospital. As they are leaving, the gundas grab a girl and are forcing themselves on her. Akshi can’t see this and goes to save her. Vibhor and Daddu try to stop her, but she can’t be stopped. She confronts the gundas by hitting one of them in the balls with a stick. Another gunda comes her way she hits him on his head. The girl runs away. The gundas turn their attention to Akshi. Akshi tries to run, but the gundas grab her and pick her up. Akshi tries to hit the gunda and run away, but the gunda slaps her. She starts bleeding. Daddu is seeing this, his powers come back. He beats the gundas and ties them up and hangs them in their underwear. Vibhor and Akshi are ecstatic. Vibhor figures that the only time Daddu’s power comes is when Akshi is in trouble. 
8
9
10
11
12. Midpoint>>

***********************
13
14

16

18. Lowpoint>>

***********************

DOCUMENT: Daddu_Damdar/Raw_Archive/Scene_Script_Ep01_Market_Explosion_Prologue.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis: Screenplay Scenes
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible|Daddu Damdar]]
> **Core Focus:** Full prose scene: the Gandhi Market prologue where an escaped test subject exhibits sensory overload and detonates before corporate drones.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

1. Ext. Market - Day
A Sunday market crowd. Pop ups and shops filled with customers. We see a family. Akshata and her husband Himanshu, each holding an arm of their 6 year old son, Abeer. They seem to be fighting over something. Akshata tells Himanshu - Mai kisi bhi haal me nahi uthungi. Chai tumhe chahiye hoti hai. You wake up early and make it yourself. Himanshu is pleading - Please yaar. Mai breakfast bana ke to jaata hun na tere liye. Tu ek cup chai nahi pila sakti… Akshata also pleads - Yaar… Wo subah extra aadhe ghante ki neend itni achi hoti hai na… Please yaar Himanshu… Abeer sees something that he likes. He tries to get his parents attention by pulling their arms. He points at the toy. Himanshu refuses - pehle hi aap ke paas bahut toys hain… Akshata - Acha… Kal movie me kya hua? Himanshu - tune kahan tak dekhi? Akshata - Ummmm… Jab Ajay Devgun ki family ko… Aneer is angry at being ignored like this. He wants something, and it’s not even being given a consideration. He pulls his arms away from his parents and runs to the stall. He has eyes for the pink fluffy elephant. He already knows he will put the elephant next to his pillow. He will hug him every night to sleep. His eyes fill with tears in anticipation. His parents follow him to the stall and Akshata yells - Abeer! Abeer turns. His tears now rolling down his  cheeks. It’s Himanshu’s turn next. He is already pissed that he has to get up 30 minutes early to make his tea, because without it, he cannot shit! He takes Abeer’s hand and pulls him away from the stall. Abeer is now bawling his eyes out. His voice fades away as his parents drag him through the market. 
We stay on Pradeep. The 21 year old boy running the toy stall. He is used to seeing kids like these come to his stall and throw all kinds of tantrums. He turns to Waseem and asks - Record kiya? Waseem giggles - Haan kiya na. Pradeep - Mujhko pata hi tha ki ye to rone waala hai. Ganesh ko dikha. Waseem turns his neck to check Ganesh out. Ganesh is talking to a girl. The girl looks like a customer, but Ganesh is flirting with her. Waseem turns back and chuckles - Uska to fantubaaji chal ra hai bro… Pradeep laughs - chal koi ni. Tu isko music laga ke pit pot pe daal de. Waseem nods going back to his phone. Suddenly, they hear a buzzing sound in the air. They both look around, looking for a bee. Waseem spots something - Oh Bhai wo dekh. There is a drone a few feet above their stalls. Waseem tries to remember, what it’s called then says - Chota helicopter. Pradeep is amazed - Chota Helicopter! The drone is systematically turning in all direction. Ganesh joins them following the noise - Abey ganwaron. Ise drone bolte hain. Ganesh looks at the drone suspiciously and whispers to Pradeep and Waseem - Isi se sarkar hampar nazar rakhti hai. A realization comes upon both boys - Oh! Aisa kya! The drone leaves. 
We follow the drone as it flies a little over the crowd. It zeroes in on a man lying down on the ground. On the first glance he looks like a homeless person. Maybe a drunk. The person gets up. He is wearing formal clothes. Shirt, pant, leather shoes. He looks like a middle class man on a working day. The man opens his eyes. The sun is too bright for him. He closes his eyes forcefully. His ears twitch. He can hear everyone clearly all at once. He puts his hands on his ears to shut out all the noise. But it’s too much. His every sense exaggerated. He tries to move through the crowd. The drone follows him from a distance. HE can hear the buzzing sound clearly. It is piercing his ears. He is confused and disconcerted. Staggering and stumbling, he bumps into someone. It’s a huge dude. It was a light bump, but the dude went through a fruit stall. He gets up threatening to beat the man. The man gets scared. He takes a step back and runs. In an instant he is a the other end of the market. Near Pradeep’s stall. He doesn’t know what happened. A second ago he was there. Now he is here. He is even more confused. Pradeep sees this strange man in front of his stall and asks - Bhai pura daaru ka adda pi gaye kya? Waseem laughs. The man comes closer to Pradeep’s stall trying to zero in on his voice. Pradeep thinks he has come to have a fight. He is ready for it. He taps Waseem - Ganesh ko bula. Waseem turns and shouts - GANEEEESSHH! Lafda ho raha hai! Ganesh comes back running. 
Himanshu is annoyed as he and Akshata come back with their jubilant son. Himanshu stands next to the man and takes out his wallet, irritated, he asks - Kitne ka hai! Takes out some money and tells Pradeep - Wo pink haathi. Pradeep is engaged with the man and almost ignoring himanshu tells him - 500 rupaye. Then to the man - Kya chahiye bhai. Hum teen hain tu ek. Lafda karna hai to koi aur pakad. The man is in pain something is happening to him. He staggers back and bumps into Himanshu. Himanshu is already irritated and tries to shove the man. The man swings his hand to push him away, but Himanshu goes flying into a stall 20 feet away. The man is surprised and looks at his hands. Askshata is horrified and screams at the man. She takes stuffed toys from pradeep’s stall and throws them at the man. Pradeep looks at this and tries to stop Akshata - Madam. Please! Rukiye madam. Waseem hands him a stick. He hands it to Akshata. Akshata takes the stick and hits the man repeatedly. But nothing is having an effect. It’s a minor irritation. He holds the stick and pushes it back. The stick hits Akshata on the head. Blood drips down. Seeing this, Pradeep, Waseem and GAnesh rain down on the man. Abeer starts crying again. The man pushes them back. They fly off and fall far away as well. The man flails his hands and legs around in frustration. Maybe, he is going mad. Everything he hits either breaks explodes or flies off. He has super strength. Everyone is stunned. What’s going on? A few people approach the man to hold him down. The man himself starts crying. He doesn’t know what’s happening to him. He goes on the ground. Sobbing like a child. A completely free from shame, innocent child like bawl. 7-8 men come forward and hold him down. This time, he doesn’t retaliate. Akshata has now regained consciousness. Somebody calls the police - Hello! Police! Haan ye Gandhi market aa jaiye… PAta nahi.. Ek aadmi sabko maar raha hai… aur… Her eyes go wide as she sees the men get up suddenly. The man is burning hot. His body is glowing from the inside. It looks  like he has a sun inside him. Himanshu has now gotten up. He curiously and cautiously takes a few steps towards the man to see what’s happening. Silence for a long beat. Then… BOOOOM! The man explodes like a bomb. Body parts flying everywhere. Screams and mayhem. The drone has recorded everything. It goes up to get a birds eye view of the explosion. Recording a few seconds of the aftermath. The drone turns around and zooms away. The screen is left with a beautiful sunset and screams in the background.

---

DOCUMENT: Hotel_Bhoot/00_Story_Bible.md
================================================================================

# Hotel Bhoot: Master Concept Bible
- **Protagonists:** Seemit Sharma (ex-banker), Shohini (ex-air hostess), children Asmita (15) and Vibhor (10).
- **The Premise:** Buying the *Hotel Mountain Queen* in Mussoorie to save their marriage, only to find the staff has fled due to hauntings.
- **The Turn:** Seemit negotiates with head ghost Devendra Joshi (British-era soldier) to employ the ghosts in room service and kitchen operations, turning the property into an adventure hotel.


================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Hotel_Bhoot/Raw_Archive/Concept_Note_Hotel_Mountain_Queen_Staff.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible]]
> **Core Focus:** Seemit Sharma shifting his family from Delhi to Mussoorie to run the haunted Mountain Queen hotel, employing the ghosts as staff to defeat builder Tyagi.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Hotel Bhoot: Concept Note
   
   Logline: A dysfunctional family buys a hotel only to discover that it’s haunted, and decide to employ the ghosts as hotel staff. 
   Genre: Drama, Comedy
   
   Synopsis: Seemit Sharma (40) has bought the hotel Mountain Queen in mussoorie and has shifted with his family. His relationship with his wife Shohini Sharma (38) is not as it used to be. They fight a lot as Shohini thinks Seemit doesn’t care about the family and doesn’t give time to them. They have two kids. 15 year old Asmita and a 10 year old Vibhor. 
   As they shift to Mussoorie to manage and run the Mountain Queen, it’s in a bad state. There has been a constant decline in the guests that have come in and most of the staff has run away. Right now they only have two employees working at the mountain queen. But they are left with one old cleaner Ravindra (65) as the other employee also runs away. 
   Seemit finds out that the reason everyone is keeping away from the hotel is that it is haunted. THe ghosts chase away any guests that come. This creates a problem for Seemit as he has taken a huge loan from the bank and if he is unable to make the hotel profitable, the bank will take it and sell it to a local builder Mr Tyagi. Mr Tyagi has plans to raze the building to the ground and build a new shopping center. 
   At first Seemit is frustrated by this. He brings tantriks and pandits to get rid of the ghosts. But to no avail. Soon, his family makes him realizes that he can use the situation to his advantage. If the hotel is destroyed, so will the ghosts. He convinces the ghosts to help him run the hotel and in turn he will make sure that they always have a place to stay. The ghosts agree to help him out. 
   Seemit rebrands the hotel as a haunted hotel for adventurous people. It picks up. The ghosts act as the hotel staff. Working in the kitchen, room service and all. The hotel is doing well, much to the disappointment of Mr Tyagi. Mr Tyagi tries to ruin the hotel in different ways. But now Seemit has the help of the ghosts to thwart those plans. 
   
   Characters:
   Seemit Sharma: He used to work as an investment banker in Delhi. But the pressure of work took a toll on his family life and mental health. He spent less and less time with his wife and kids. Started fighting with his wife. Even couples counselling didn’t help. His relationship with his kids deteriorated as well. HIs daughter Asmita did not respect him. She uses to be close to him when she was little, but as she has grown up she has gotten further away from Seemit. His work was taking a toll on his mental health as well. He had an violent episode in the office. After which his boss gave him a few weeks off. Seemit decided that all of this needed to stop. He will not have a family if this goes on. So he decided to come back to his home town and run a hotel that he loved as a kid. He hopes that his family will be together, will spend time with each other and they will get back to a normal close knit family. 
   
   Shohini Sharma: Shohini used to be an Air hostess as in her youth. At 22 she met Seemit on one of the flights. They hit it off and started dating. Soon they fell in love and got married. Shohini was still working, but that stopped once she had Asmita. She became a full time mom. This made her sad and resentful. Seemit also became obsessed with his work and barely spent time with her or the baby. When Asmita was 4 Shohini decided to start working again. But she got pregnant again with Vibhor. This put a dent in her plans of having a career at all. Her relationship with Seemit suffered. SHe was raising these two kids alone with no help from Seemit. She wanted to divorce him. But Seemit had a mental breakdown at work. HE had no one but Shohini to help him out. She still loved him and gave him another chance. When Seemit decided to buy a hotel, she was excited about it. But when  she founf out that they will be shifting to a small town, she was annoyed and fought with Seemit. Seemit told her to give him one last chance. They will try to run the hotel for a year. If it didn’t work. They will shift back to dehli. Shohini reluctantly agreed. 
   
   Asmita Sharma: Asmita is a typical 15 year old going to school. She has her friends in Delhi. She likes to read and draw. But she has gotten into partying in peer pressure. Her friends are the cool kids of the school. She thinks herself to be lucky to be their friends and does whatever they do. Her father doesn’t like this and scolds her for it. But she doesn’t care. She thinks her father is barely around, so she lies to him about what she is doing or where she is going. She hears her mom and dad fight all the time. THat’s when she puts on her headphones and draws. 
   She is angry that her dad has decided to move cities. She is angry that he didn’t even ask her for an opinion. But she has no choice. She will have to start over again. Make new friends. She is extremely doubtful that there will be anyone as cool as her friends in Delhi. SHe heard her father tell her mom that they will give it a year’s time, if it doesn’t work, they will go back to delhi. So she decided to sabotage the hotel so taht they can move back to delhi. 
   
   Vibhor Sharma: Vibhor is a naughty child. Always getting in trouble at the school. He has gotten suspended a few times for bullying children from his class. He used to be a good student, but recently his grades have gone down as well. His parents have tried scolding him, punishing him, but it hasn’t worked. He has no friends to play with, so he plays videogames all day. The only friend he has is his sister. He admires his sister and wants to be close to her all the time. But she thinks of him as s nuisance and avoids him. When he finds out that his family is moving, he is happy at first. But seeing his sister unhappy he also decides that he doesn’t want to move. He decides that he will help his sister in sabotaging the hotel and moving back to delhi. But during his stay he will get closer to her. This will create a doubt in him about moving back to delhi, because that would mean that his sister will go back to her friends and will not have time for him anymore. 
   
   Ravindra Joshi: Ravinder Joshi has worked in the hotel for 35 years. His father Devendra Joshi(Who used to be in the british army and ran away) bought this land and the house that was built on it. Devendra made the hotel what it was, but he died young when he was 35. Ravindra’s family ran into financial troubles after that and had to sell the hotel. RAvindra started working at the hotel when he was 29, as he had loving feelings for the place and has worked there since then. For some reason, the ghosts haven’t bothered him that much and he stayed on till now. 
   
   Mr SP Tyagi: He is a real estate businessman. He has amassed a lot of wealth and wants more. He has his eyes set on the Hotel Mountain Queen. It is in prime location and he has a deal in place to construct a shopping center and sell it for 50 crores to a buyer from Bombay. He will try everything in his power to get the hotel.
   
   The Ghosts:
   
   Devendra Joshi: He is resentful of the fact that he died early and didn’t get to live the life that he wanted. He doesn’t come out that often, but he is the leader of the otherwise unruly pack of ghosts. 
   
   Sheetal Chaddha: Her husbnd killed her in the hotel and got 14 years of prison. She hates to see any happy couples in the hotel. She scares any couples that come to the hotel and scares them off. 
   
   Sonal Tickoo: Sonal died when she was 12 in a drowning accident on the hotel premises. She now only wants to people to play with and tries to make friends with any kids that come along. But this scares away the kids and the parents. 
   
   Varun Bisht: He was a local farmer who supplied veggies and fruits from his farm. He died at the hotel in an accident. He is now trapped in the hotel and can’t go out. The only time he is happy is when he sees his daughter and wife, who are still alive. 
   
   There will be other ghosts (minor characters) that will have similar story. The ghosts don’t really get along that much. THey only listen to Devendra’s ghost. But Devendra is lonely and keeps to himself most of the time. He only comes out when he sees guests not respecting his hotel. 
   
   Storylines: 
   
   1. The family dynamics of Seemit and his wife and kids. How they fight with each other and then come together to run this hotel. How the relationship between husband and wife, father and daughter, and sister and brother gets resolved.
   
   2. The ghosts and the Sharma family getting together to save the hotel. Working together with ghosts, while Seemit tries to mend his relationship with his family. 
   
   3. The builder Mr Tyagi and his antics to get the hotel. The conflict between Seemit and his family and Mr Tyagi.

---

DOCUMENT: One_Two_Three_Four/00_Story_Bible.md
================================================================================

# 1… 2… 1 2 3 4: Master Story Bible

## Narrative Structure: Four Eras
1. **Age 21 (The Breakup & Five Stages of Grief):** Govind breaks off his engagement with Shweta so she won't grow to resent his artistic poverty.
2. **Age 23 (The Wedding Eve):** Govind returns to stop her arranged marriage, but Shweta stands firm, urging him not to compromise his music.
3. **Age 26 (Mumbai Cafe Encounter):** Shweta is pregnant and married into a wealthy family; Govind is surviving as an indie cafe singer.
4. **Age 30 (The Ad Agency Jingle Reconnection):** Govind refuses to sell out to a soap commercial until junior copywriter Shweta spontaneously bursts into classic Indian TV jingles (Nirma, Lifebuoy). They leave together as equals.

================================================================================

> [!abstract] Archive Log Diagnosis
> **Project:** [[00_Story_Bible]]
> **Core Focus:** Govind and Shweta: the 5 stages of breakup at age 21, wedding-eve confrontation at 23, pregnant cafe reunion at 26, and the ad agency jingle reconciliation at 30.
> **Status:** Preserved working draft synthesized into the primary project workspace.

---

Title: 1… 2… 1 2 3 4
   
   Part 1: Govind and Shweta are both 21 year olds. They have been friends for 15 years and have dated for 5. They live in a small town. As our story starts, they are sitting near a fountain, eating kachori from a road side stall. Govind proposes to Shweta very casually. Shweta thinks he might be joking. Govind assures her that he is serious. Shweta tells him why they can’t get married, no. 1: They are young. Govind says it doesn’t matter they both know they love each other. No. 2: THey both don’t have a job. Govind tells her that she can get a job, he wants to be a musician. They have an argument about this. Shweta tells him that to have a stable family and a marriage he needs to get a job. Govind thinks he has to follow his dreams. This is the right time. He is young and has enough time to struggle and make it. Shweta is frustrated, but relents to his arguments. No. 3. She doens’t know if her father would agree. Govind takes out his cell phone. THere is a group call to his mom and Shweta’s dad. They’ve been listening. They both approve. Finally no 4: He hasn’t proposed to her properly. A friend gets up from the opposite side of the fountain and hands Govind a guitar. Govind sings a song confessing his love and asking Shweta to marry him. As we fade away. 
   Part 2: 1 year later. 
   Govind declares that he is not going to get married to Shweta. His mother tries to put some sense into him. She is not doing a good job. She threatens, emotionally blackmails, even hits him. But Govind is not relenting. He is not telling his mother either. 
   Shweta enters. Hits him with something. THey fight while the daal is being made. The pressure cooker is whistling. Shweta is hitting him and shouting at him. While his mother tells Shweta to knock some sense into him. It’s all very chaotic with the cooker and the fighting and the arguments. (Anger) Shweta then thinks that Govind is kidding. They’ve been together since childhood now they’ve been engaged for a year. And now he wants to break up. He is surely joking. Playing a prank. SHe tells his mom that he is playing one his practical jokes on them. And they are so silly they’ve fallen for it. (Denial)
   Finally Govind takes control. He takes Shweta outside to talk to her. He explains that they want different things from life. And if they stay together they will end up hating each other. Shweta doens’t understand.
   Govind wants to be a musician. SHweta wants him to get a job. THey will get married soon, and she wants a stable home for both of them. Govind refuses. He tells her that music is her passion. 
   A sequence of scenes we go though Bargaining. Shweta gives him various options like. He can work and purue music on the side. Maybe he can get a job in some music shop or something. Finally she accedes that Govind can focus on music. SHe will get a job and make money for the both of them. Govind refuses. He knows that Shweta’s father will never accept this. Shweta knows this is true. But she says that she will fight her dad. If he disagrees. THey will leave home and run away. SHe will get a job and earn for the both of them. He tells her that she will get a job now, but in a couple of years his not making money will start to bother her. SHe will get angry, pick fights with him. Then eventually she will start resenting him and hate him. He doesn’t want that to happen. 
   Shweta begins to cry. (Depression) She is sad and inconsolable. Govind throws away his stern behavior and tells Shweta that this is for their own good. He calmly explains, while she cries remembering that she will miss him. SHe doesn’t know life without him etc. 
   Finally Shweta accepts the decision and leaves. 
   
   Part 3: 2 years later
   It’s a wedding house. Decorations are everywhere. Govind comes in his scooty towards the back gate. Some people are carrying tables and chairs inside. He sneaks in with them. He sneaks through the corridor hiding his face. He finally encounters Shweta. Shweta is shocked at seeing him and shouts his name. HEr father comes out from another room hearing the name and hits him with a shoe. Shweta stops him from beating up govind and takes him inside her room. 
   Govind has had a change of heart. He has contacted Shweta a few times before this. He agrees to take up a job. He tells Shweta that he is going to marry her take up a job. Wherever she wants. He is having a tough time, doing music. THere is just so much rejection. He doesn’t know what to do.
   Shweta tells him that this is the wrong time. She is getting married today. Govind tells her that it doesn’t matter. He knows that she loves him. Shweta tells him that she does love him. But she has realized that what Govind said was true. He is not going to be happy taking up a job. And she is not going to be happy if he is not happy. Govind tries really hard to convince her. He thinks that she is getting married to take revenge on him. Shweta tells him that this is not the case. She knows the guy she is marrying. He is a good man. SHe likes him also. Govind tells her that he can not live without her. 
   Finally Shweta tells Govind that they’ve made up their minds about something. She tells him that she knows that becomeing a musician was Govind’s dream. He wouldn’t be happy giving it up. There is struggle everywhere. He has to fight it and make it somehow. On his own terms. He is just having a momentary doubt. He should go and try make it as a musician. Govind is angry and sad and leaves. 
   
   Part 3: 3 years later: Mumbai.
   
   We’re in a cafe. Some music playing in the background. Shweta enters and takes a seat. She is pregnant. She smiles looking at a something. WE see her POV, it’s Govind. He is singing one of his tunes. People are not paying attention. THey are talking amongst themselves. Only an old lady is cheering him on at the front of the cafe. Govind sees Shweta and smiles. He finishes his set and goes to talk to her. 
   
   They talk about their lives. Govind tells her that he couldn’t do much in their home town. So after she got married, he came to Bombay. Got rejected a bunch of times. Now he is singing in bars and cafes. He makes decent money. He has a youtube channel. That has a few subscribers. 
   Shweta tells him that she has a baby girl now. And she loves Govind’s music. Shweta puts it on her phone everynight to calm her down and put her to sleep. 
   Govind and Shweta talk about their lives. Govind is happy with what he is doing. Although Bombay is expensive but he likes it here and he is making enough money with these cafe and bar gigs. 
   Shweta has a husband and a 3 year old son. She seems reluctant in talking about her husband. She wants to work. Her husband makes a lot of money from family businesses. So she doesn’t really have to work anywhere. But she gets bored at home. And was thinking of taking up a job in advertising as she was really interested in TV ads and all. But she can’t. With her baby on the way, and her husband. She can’t think of doing both. 
   Govind encourages her to do whatever she wants. He tells her that sometimes sacrifies have to be made. You have to lose the thing you love the most to follow your dreams. Shweta understands that he is talking about her. 
   They continue talking as we fade away. We see them recounting old days. Laughing. 
   
   Part 4: 4 years later: Mumbai
   
   Govind is in a conference room. He is sitting with his manager. THere are a bunch of executives sitting in front of him. They are trying to convince him to do a song for their new soap brand. Govind doesn’t want to do it. His manager keeps whispering to him to hear the agency out. It’s a lot of money. GOvind tells him that he doesn’t work for money. While they are arguing we see Shweta tiptoeing to enter the conference room. THe executive looks at her angrily and gestures her to sit down in the corner. Govind turns to the agency executives and tells them that what he does is art and he doesn’t believe advertising has good music or it can be called art. THe executive has no answer. WE see Shweta hesitating, then suddenly she bursts into a song, “Hema, Rekha, Jaya aur Sushma, sabki pasand nirma. WAshing powder nirma. Nirma.” The executive is flabbergasted. He apologizes to Govind and tells him that she is just a junior copywriter. Govind is staring at Shweta and sings, “Tandurusti ki raksha karta hai lifebuoy, lifebuoy hai jahan tandurusti hai wahan. Lifebuoy”. The executive is stunned. Shweta and Govind sing at the same time, “Doodh doodh doodh doodh, Wonderful doodh. Piyo glass full” THey both start laughing. Govind turns to the executive and tells him that he will do it. Cut to:
   Shweta and Govind are in a car. Shweta is driving. GOvind tells her that he si proud of her for following what she wanted to do. Shweta plays it down, saying that she is only a junior copywriter. It’s not like she is the creative director. Govind smiles and says, “Yet!” Shweta smiles and nods, understanding what he wants to say. Silence for a few seconds. Govind asks her how her husband is. Shweta tells him that they got divorced. 
   They stop at a school Kids are coming out. One small boy of 4 years is waiting near the gate. He sees Shweta and runs towards her. He sees Govind. Govind doesn’t know how to behave near the kid. Shweta introduces them. Govind looks in his pocket to give him something. He finds something. He gives it to the kid. THe kid starts eating it. But Govind stops him. He tells him that it’s a harmonica. You have to play it. Shweta smiles as she sees both of them interacting. GOvind shows the kid how to play harmonica as we fade away.

# Pradeep Kukkad: Master Project Bible

## 1. Character & Transmedia Premise
- **The Concept:** Pradeep Kukkad is a half-human, half-rooster singer-songwriter attempting to make it as a legitimate indie pop star and viral digital creator.
- **The Tone:** Utterly deadpan sincerity meeting existential livestock horror. He treats routine poultry processing (marination, tandoors, egg collecting) as profound human tragedy and civil rights violations.
- **Medium Pipeline:** Acoustic guitar tracks, satirical YouTube mockumentaries, Instagram reels, and short music videos.

## 2. Song Catalog & Thematic Themes
1. **Ek Khwab (Tandoori Martyrdom):** Capo 6 acoustic ballad lamenting a lover who was marinated and cooked in a clay oven.
2. **I Fell in Love with a Goat:** Capo 4 country-folk ballad celebrating inter-species love facing the shared threat of the butcher's knife.
3. **Mere Ande:** Raw blues/folk grievance mourning children transformed into breakfast omelettes.
4. **Trust Me Bro:** Club/party satire critiquing toxic party peer pressure and pharmaceutical escapism.

Nahi chahiye to bhai, saada ganja kaunsa kam hai.

TRUST ME BRO!! For you my love is Shalimar.  
TRUST ME BRO!! Tera bhai bol raha hai yaar.

DOCUMENT: Series_Spines_and_Treatments/Sumitra_The_Feudal_Syndicate.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine
> **Core Spark:** A female rural protector whose rise against landlord exploitation leads to tragic moral compromise
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [ ] Map the 14-year gap between father's imprisonment and release
> - [ ] Flesh out the Pradhan's commercial land-grab subplot (factories/waste dumping)
> - [ ] Structure the Ambuj vs. Sumitra emotional tension (unaware cop vs outlaw feudal lord)

Sumitra was a girl of 12, when her mom was raped and murdered by a powerful man. She and her dad went to the police. They took the report, but arrested the wrong person. When they went to the station to tell them who it really was, they saw the man, who was a big jameendar in their village laughing with the police officers. 
Angry, Sumitra confronts them, they beat her father up to the point of death. The Jamindar threatens to rape Sumitra as well, and forces himself on her. IN her struggle with the Jamindar, Sumitra picks up a gun lying around and shoots the Jamindar. Then she turns around to shoot the two officers. (This could also be her father. But I feel if we make Sumitra do it, it will be like a dark past that she hasn’t gotten over yet. A wound if you will) Her father is horrified at this. Afraid of what will happen to her daughter if she gets caught, he takes the blame. They argue their case in court, and tell the judge the circumstances (self defense. Beating and the rape attempt) under which the killings happened. But the other officers along with other Jamindars of the village, plot against them and the father, Bheeshma, goes to Jail for 14 years.
Angry at this injustice Sumitra distrusts cops and the law. She vows to become so powerful that no one is able to take advantage of her family and anyone like her ever again.
Her grandfather and grandmother come to the village to raise her. Her grandfather used to be in military and trains her to fight and use weapons. Her grandmother used to be in politics. She teaches her at home. 
With the help of her grandfather and grandmother. Through clever manipulation and political connections they acquire more and more land in the village and become one of the biggest land holders in the village. 
Sumitra sees the other landholders (Jamindars) exploiting farmers and labor. She recruits men to fight these Jamindars off, and saves farmers from exploitation. This happens several times till most of the Jamindars are afraid of her. All villagers become loyal to her and go to her in case of any issue. Even the Village Pradhan starts taking her permission to carry on his day to day activities in the village.
Her father is about to get released from the prison. In the time between his imprisonment and release, Sumitra gained power in the village and drove off the Jamindar’s family out and took over their lands. The eldest son of the Jamaindar, Ambuj, vowed to become a police officer after his father’s death. He has been transferred to this village. He decides to bring his whole family and reclaim the land. He knows of Sumitra and her family is like a guda family, but doesn’t know that her father Bheeshma was responsible for his father’s death. He only knows that she has terrorized the entire village Jamindari and holds a lot of power in the entire district. The MLAs and MPs are afraid of her and go to her to win elections. He wants to go back, get back his family’s land and stand up to Sumitra to end her reign of terror. 
The Village pradhan is an evil fellow. He dislikes Sumitra and her family, but bows down to her because of the power she yields. He instigates Ambuj,to take revenge on her. He wants the power Sumitra holds. His plan is to acquire the farming lands and build factories and shopping malls. He has talked about this with Sumitra. But Sumitra and refused him in strong words. She has seen the damage these factories do to the neighboring lands, lakes and rivers by dumping their waste in it. 
The pradhan has tried to get the smaller farmers to sell off their lands, which they have agreed to, but Sumitra has ordered all of them not to do it. And being loyal to Sumitra they have listened to her. They believe that Sumitra would never do anything that is not in their interest.
When Ambuj arrives in the village, Sumitra doesn’t know his identity. But because she distrusts Police she doesn’t like him either. She orders him to bow down to her several times, but he refuses. This makes her dislike him even more. He doesn’t know that Sumitra’s father was the one who killed his father. He finds out when her father is released from the prison. His determination to end Sunitra’s stronghold gets even stronger. The pradhan finds out about the connection between them and instigates Ambuj.
Sumitra sees how honest and good Ambuj is and starts developing a soft corner for him. She might be falling in love with him. But then eventually finds out about his family and his father. She now wants to destroy his family just like his father destroyed hers. 
This will be her journey to realizing that she is becoming the person she hated.


================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Series_Spines_and_Treatments/The_Blue_Tick_Illusion_and_the_Bhutan_Poster.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (short_story | 2018_Crucible)
> **Core Spark:** Sameer staging Mauritius beach shots on a windy Bangalore balcony while yelling at a boy holding a poster; falling in love with agency influencer Disha who eats chicken parathas off-camera while preaching veganism; losing authentic love to obtain a verified blue tick
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - Combine the Characters.md dossiers into the head of this script file
> - Polish the sharp dialogue beats between Sameer, Prerna, and Disha

---

First scene: A good looking boy standing at the beach looking at the sunset. We hear voices of clicks. And see the beautiful pictures. The boy turns around, looks straight at the camera and raises his bottle to say cheers. More pictures clicked. He walks towards the camera as more pictures are clicked. He blows a kiss towards the camera. And walks past it as the background flies up. 
The camera pans out. The boy comes back angry. This is SAmeer. He shouts at another boy holding the poster of a sunset. “Abey dhang se pakad na. Ud kyun rha hai.” 
The other boy replies, “Mai kya karun agar hawa chal rahi hai to. Balcony ka darwaaza band karne ko bola tha.” THe photographer, Prerna calms Sameer down and tells him it doesn’t matter. The pictures have come out great. Sameer looks at the camera screen. The pictures have actually come out great. SAmeer is happy, “Ispe hashtag mauritius daalna hai. Yaad rakhna.” PRerna nods.

Sameer changes his clothes to swimming trunks and hands another poster to the boy. “Chalo ab wo sunscreen brand ke liye bhi kar lete hain” to the boy “Is baar dhang se pakadna”. Zoheb is irritated and says “100 rupaye me jaan le lo”. Sameer snaps back, “ TO kya ghar tere naam kar dun.” The boy replies back, “Mujhe pata hai ye tum influencer log bahut paisa kamate ho. Lekin jeb se dhela nahi nikalta”
Sameer charges towards him, but Prerna holds his hand to calm him down. SAmeer takes a deep breath and tells him ki bhai abhi mai ye sab free me kar raha hun, Isse followe badhenge. To paise mujhe bhi milenge, aur tujhe bhi. Prerna looks at Sameer lovingly. Sameer tells the boy, “Chal ab pakad”

We get a sense that Prerna likes him by the way she is looking at him. She is fixing his hair. 

Sameer holds a cocunut with a straw drinking out of it. The boy opens the poster of another Beach during the day. 
We see Prerna clicking pictures. This time we see it from above as Sameer gives different poses. 


Cut:

 Disha is a social media and lifestyle infuencer. We see her talking into the camera talking about some vegan diet that she follows and how it has helped her with her skin and weight etc. She talks about Vegan diet ke kayi fayede hain. Mai to kabse follow kar rahi hun. No animal products for me. 

Her maid comes from behind and tells her didi paranthe bana ke rakh diye hain, chicken aap microwave me garam kar lena.” 
Disha is pissed, aapne video ke beech me parathe aur chicken kyun bola. Ab video dobara karna padega. Acha hua live nahi tha. THe maid doesn’t understand any of it and leaves making a face, “Parathe abhi kha lo, thande ho jayenge. Disha goes to her dining table to devour the parathas and the chicken. 
Disha recieves a phone call. It’s from an organizer. She is invited as an influencer to an event in a couple of days, to review a restaurant. She accepts and tells the organizer that she will be there. 

Cut:

Sameer, Prerna drive inside a fancy hotel in a cab. They are all wearing fancy clothes. Prerna asks Sameer Sameer plops himself down at the waiting room couch as if he belongs there. Prerna clicks a few pictures. THe hotel manager comes to tell them that they can’t click pictures. Sameer asks the manager what’s the price for one room for a night. The manager tells him 20 thousand bucks. Sameer thinks for a second and asks, if this is a cheap hotel. The manager is somewhat offended and tells him that no. This is one of the best hotels in bangalore, he lists off the rating and reviews of the hotel.

SAmeer tells the manager that he is looking for a hotel for his client and his team of 20 people for a week. He is just scouting the best hotels here and needs to send pictures to his client for him to approve the hotel. The manager smiles and tells them that in that case feel free to take pictures. SAmeer tells him that he want to see the rooms as well. He goes and clicks pictures lying on the bed. IN the washroom, in the lobby. The manager finds it weird but Sameer keeps on giving him hope by saying thinkgs like, we would need to book your banquet hall for a crowd of 200 people, we will need to book the meeting rooms for the whole week etc.
After everything, he gives the manager his card. And tells him that he will get in touch soon. 

Prerna and Sameer run out, laughing at what they’ve just done. Sameer gets a phone call. An organizer asks if he is free in a couple of days. Sameer says, “Ji mai abhi mauritius me hun…”. THe organizer tells him “YE to bada bura hua. Hamare hotel ka openeing event tha. And we were looking for influencers who could promote our brand and…”
Sameer interrupts him and tells him, “… but my flight to india is tomorrow.” He hesitates,”Umm.. What might you be… What are you paying for the event.”
THe organizer chuckles and tells him that, “App galat samajh rahe hain… Hum aapko paise nahi de rahe hain... Hum aapse paise lene ki jagah aapko ye opportunity de rahe hain… to you know… Build your profile… And in turn, you tell everyone how GREAT we are.” 
Sameer immediately tells him that he understands and will be there for the event. Sameer is excited and jumps with Prerna, “Finally mujhe events par bhi bulane lage.” PRerna is also excited for him and asks if they are paying him.. 
SAmeer tells her, “nahi.. Abhi to nahi… But at least events par to bulane lage.”

*****

At the event, the organizer, Pathak, comes to Sameer and asks, “Mauritius ki trip kaisi rahi aapki? Etc etc. Don’t forget to get the foot massage, there is an accupuncture healing, a meditation center…”
SAmeer says” Bas bhi kijiye Pathak Sir, ye sab karne ka time kahan hai….”
Pathak tells him, “Sab karne ki zarurat nahi nahi… Kuch kuch try kijiye ji kar sakte hain… Baaki aapke followers ko kaunsa pata chal raha hai.” He winks. Before leaving he tells him, “Bahar buffet laga hai, don’t forget that…”

Disha is at the event. She is tasting the food with a drink in hand. Sameer is standing with the jacket that matches the waiter’s jacket. Disha mistakes him for he waiter and hands him her glass, “Bhaiya ye repeat kar do.” Sameer looks at her. She is pretty. He goes away and brings the drink for her, but doesn’t go away. Disha tells him ki bade badtameez ho, yahin khade ho abhi tak. Sameer smiles and doesn’t say anything. DIsha scolds him again for staring at her. 

Another influencer recognizes Sameer and comes to him to greet him. His plate is full of food. Pakodas, fried chicken.  “Abey tujhe bhi bulaya hai is event pe”, SAmeer nods.
“Bahut Paisa kharch kar rahe hain bhai. Mujhe bhi 10 hazaar diye…” He sees DIsha and introduces himself, “Myself, Pravin, health and fitness influencer.” He immediately sees something, “Oye Jalebi… Excuse me.”
 
Disha realizes that SAmeer is also an influencer and apologizes. She tells him ki aapki jacket dekh ke. Sameer laughs and takes his jacket off. 
THey spend some time at the event. Sameer tells her countries he has visited and his experiences, “Abhi last week hi mauritius gaya tha mai.”
Disha tells him, “POrt Louis is beautiful. 
SAmeer: porta lou…”
DIsha:” POrt louis, Mauritius ka capital…
SAmeer: Oh acha wo… POrt louis… Mujhe laga… Acha wo waala purtgal ya aisa kuch bol rahi hain. Wo wale port louis to gaya hun mai. Bahut acha sheher hai.

They get to talking and hit it off. They go to Foot massage and meditation center. Click pictures for each other. 

***************

A few days pass after the event. 

Sameer is at a fake cleanliness drive to post on social media. He is not really cleaning anything. Just holding a broom and pretending to clean. 
Prerna tells him that she has recieved a letter for a wildlife photographers residency in South Africa. She is thinking of going. THey want her there in a couple of months. Sameer is scared and tells her ki Mujhe teri zarurat hai.  Tu aise mujhe chod ke nahi ja sakti. Aur waise bhi photography to tu kar hi rahi hai na. Wahan ke janwaron se acha is jaanwar ki photo kheech le.” Prerna looks unconvinced. Sameer keeps a hand on her shoulder and squeezes her towards him. He tells her, “Arey wildlife photography is karni hai na. Hum Badigarh chalenge. Wahan bhi to jaanwar hain. Plus tujhe pata hai tere bina mai kuch bhi nahi kar sakta?”. Prerna smiles and tells him okay. She won’t go. 

****

Disha is in a  cab going somewhere. Right then she sees Sameer with a broom standing on the side of the road. SHe stops the cab and gets out. She is walking towards Sameer when he notices her, “Hi. Tum yahan kaise” Disha replies, “ Mai Ghar ja rahi thi rahi thi. To tumhe dekha.” Disha points at the broom and asks, “tum kya kar rahe ho.” Sameer stutters, “Cleaniness drive.” Disha seems impressed. Sameer tells her ki, “Sheher kitna ganda kar diya hai ham logon ne to saaf karne ki zimmedaari bhi hamari hi hai.”
She looks around, there is a lot of garbage still lying around, Sameer tells her ki abhi start hi kiya hai. Disha tells her that it’s a nice thing he is doing and she will help. 
Prerna is looking at all this, and we see a hint of jealousy. She tells her ki ham kar lenge, tumhari zarurat nahi hai. Sameer eyes Prerna and tells her, are madad hi to karna chahti hai na karne do. Disha starts picking up the garbage. Sameer tells prerna to click more pictures, much to her chagrin. 


At home Disha gets a notification, Sameer has tagged you in a post. She looks at Sameer’s profile, smiles at some of the posts. FInally sends him a message, thanks for the tag. She tells him ki uske followers to already poochne lag gaye hain ki kya ye mera boyfriend hai. Sameer tells her ki unko haan ya na kuch mat bolna. Bas kuch bhi vague sa answer de dena. Isse followers me intrigue bana rehta hai.
DIsha laughs at this, :LMAO…
Sameer’s expressions are like, isme LMAO kya tha, ye to common sense hai. 
THey flirt a little more. Disha finally asks him out on a date. Sameer is happy and surprised, “Abey ye kya ho raha hai mere saath. Din aa gaye mere bhi. 
He agrees for the date. 

*****
Disha and Sameer meet at a fancy cafe. Disha tells him about her job. She is a social media planner for an agency, she was making money for brands and making other people famous. She decided to do it for herself. 
Sameer ko lagta hai ki ye to badi hifi hai. Kuch to bada bolna padega. He tells her that he likes to travel and enjoy life. He decided to share it with the world. He is obviously lying. 
Disha tells him that she has seen his posts and it seems he has lived an interesting life. Sameer shrugs very matter of factly. 
We see them having a good time. Sameer make Disha laugh. At the end of the date. Sameer takes out his phone and clicks a selfie, “Apne followers ko bhi kuch dikhayen.” Disha smiles as they click a picture. 

Sameer goes home and sees ki uski posts pe bahut like aa rahe hain. Uske followers bhi badh gaye hain. 

He texts Prerna, Dekh yaar, aj dhai sau followers badh gaye. Prerna is a little jealous and tells him that photo dhang se nahi khichi. Light galat thi.” 
Sameer: Are you kidding. Dekh kitne likes mile hain. Tu bas jealous hai ki tune nahi khichi photo. … Chal agli date pe tu bhi chal lena. SAmeer gets a text from Disha telling him she had a great time. Sameer cuts the call with PRerna and replies to Disha.


They keep talking on the phone and over texts. We see Disha smiling at Sameer’s text. Sameer is bumping into chairs and tables at his house, writing to Disha. 

They are on a video call. Sameer making Prerna wait as they are out on the street for a shoot. Prerna is getting irritated. 

Disha asks him to take her to that expensive restaurant that he goes to. “Tumhari post me tha, last month. 

 Sameer is a little taken aback, and says that uski to quality bahut kharab ho gayi hai.  Disha is disappointed, maine to bahut ache reviews sune hain. Tumne bhi bahut tareef ki thi. 

Sameer: Wo to karni padti hai na… But quality bahut kharab ho gayi hai… TAndoori chicken to ekdun kharab hai.
Disha: Tandoori chicken??? Wo to Korean restaurant hai na.

Sameer: OH acha, tum us waale restaurant ki baat kar rahi ho. Haan haan. WO to thik hai. Bahut acha to nahi keh sakte… But…
DIsha: Pleeeaasee will you take me there. Mujhe korean food ke baare me kuch bhi nahi pata… Sameer has to say yes. 

He is in a bind. He asks PRerna for help. Prerna loans him some money. She preps him with basic korean food. 

The date goes well. Sameer is awkward with his knowledge about korean food and fumbles, but he is charming and salvages the situation. Disha likes how nice and polite he is. They take several selfies.

Sameer walks Disha back home. 
 

SAmeer goes to meet Prerna after the date. He tells her that he likes Disha. He talks her up and tells her how pretty and sophisticated she is and he thinks that she likes him too. Prerna is annoyed and tells him ki wo tumhe nahi is dusre SAmeer ko pasand karti hai. SAmeer says he doesn’t care. Prerna tells him to be honest. She tells him that he is a great guy and Disha will be lucky to have him. But Sameer doesn’t agree. USko lagta hai ki Disha fakkad se ladke ko kyun pasand karegi. Koi bhi ek fakkad se ladke ko kyun pasand karega. 

 
Saneer is completely taken by Disha. Prerna tells him to let Disha see the real Sameer. Sameer tells Prerna that she doesn’t know that if he told Disha all this, he will definitely lose her. 

Sameer and Prerna have a fight, where he tells her that she doesn’t know what it is to love someone and lose them. This hits Prerna hard and she goes away, finally telling him that she does know what it is to love someone and lose them. She has been doing it everyday for him. Loving him and losing him to Disha. Saying this she leaves. Sameer is shocked but tells himself that he can do it without Prerna’s help.

****************

Disha is enamoured by Sameer’s persona and charm.  SAmeer’s new post. 
Followers messages. Sameer wants to take DIsha to another fancy place, but he doesn’t have money. He can’t ask Prerna anymore. 

He takes her to a small cramped dosa place. Saying mengi jagaho par aisi baat kahan. SAmeer knows people here. He is freiendly with them. Log bhi usko jaante hain. The old waiter talks as if he has been friends with Sameer for a long time. 
Sameer then remembers something and apologizes to DIsha. Ki ye to vegan nahi hai. PLus yahan kaafi oily khana hota hai. DIsha has to pretend that it’s okay. Kabhi kabhi cheat days kiye ja sate hain. 
She eats the dosa. It’s amazing. 

Disha and Sameer have fallen in love with each other. But SAmeer is hesitant in taking her outside. He can’t afford to take her anywhere. He makes excuses like, aj kisi laser tag arena me invited hun etc. Disha: Wah. Tum kaafi interesting ho yaar. 
Sameer is a little guilty but says thanks. 

Somewthing weighs on DIsha’s mind as well. SAmeer asks her about how to keep healthy and eat well. Mai bhi vegan ho jaun to. Disha has to fake teh answers. She looks it up online and gives him a bookish answer. Sameer tells her how sophisticated she is. I have never met anyonw like you. DIsha is weirded out by this. She feels guilty. 

After the call, Sameer doesn’t know what to do. He wants to meet Disha but can’t afford to take her out. He calls Prerna, prerna refuses to help him out.  


Finally, Disha calls him and confesses that what Sameer knows about her is not entirely true. She is not the bubbly fashion and lifestyle guru she pretends to be. Most of her clothes are borrowed from friends. Wo to vegan bhi nahi hai. USko chicken bahut  pasand hai. She hasn’t visited all the places she says she has and she can’t pretend to be this fake person anymore in front of Sameer as she has fallen in love with him. 

This comes as a relief to Sameer. Who decides to tell Disha about him as well. He meets Disha for a date and takes her to his real house. He shows her his real life, living in a middle class colony and hanging out with his average school friends. Then he confesses his love as well. 

This comes as a shock to Disha. Who is she to judge. And anyway, she had fallen in love with the sweet and caring Sameer. 

They continue dating, and Disha is having a good time with the real SAmeer. He is the same funny, sweet and caring SAmeer. ONe day she comes to his house, and sees him shooting a picture with a poster of eifel tower behind him. Sameer is relieved that she is here. He asks her to clck the pictures. Ye tripod pe angle thik se aa hi nahi raha. Disha is deep in thought as she is clicking pictures. 

Sameer and Disha go out and order non veg food. Sameer also orders one vegan dish. Disha asks kiske liye. Sameer tells her ki ye apne social media par post nahi karogi. Disha nods, but she is not entirely happy with something. 

Disha asks Sameer, what if we stopped doing this. Sameer laughs and says why? Disha tells him that she is tired of living a fake life. CAn we be real? Sameer tells her that we are real. We are trying to live the life we want. Not the one we have. DIsha tells him that she is not happy like this. LEt’s both just stop and be ourselves. 


Sameer is annoyed and says, “Tumhe kya ho gaya?” Abhi tak to sab thik tha. You were fine till you didn’t know. What now?
DIsha says, but now I know na. I like you. And you’ve made me realize what I’ve been missing. 
SAmeer gets worked up and says,” I Don’t want to talk about it right now”
THey wat in silence. At the end of the meal, Sameer tells her, let’s take a selfie.DIsha is not in the mood. SHe is angry. Sameer takes her hand and tells her to smile. He himself has a big smile on his face, and looks really happy. 
DIsha can’t fake it anymore and gets up and leaves. Sameer follows her.

Disha gives Sameer an ultimatum, she wants both of them to stop pretending and live like who they really are. Sameer has built all this with so much work over the years. He is not ready to give it up. They both part ways as they can’t agree on this.

At first Sameer is fine as he tells himself that Disha didn’t really love the real Sameer. She was in love with the loser who wasn’t worth anything. Sameer goes on with his life, but eventually feels lonely without Disha and his friends. He tries to get in touch with PRerna and Zoheb but they are not responding to his texts and calls.

Sameer is sad, lonely and heartbroken. Prerna’s words ring in his ears, “YOu have to love yourself before you can expect anyone else to love you.” 

He comes home sits at his laptiop, looking at people’s comments on his pictures. Pictures of him having fun with friends at a pool, but his own apartment is dark and empty. His followers have increased a lot. He sees a message that he has verified profile and will recieve a blue tick. We see a blue tick on his profile. He gets excited and calls Prerna. SHe is not picking up. He goes to Prerna’s house and tells her that he has finally got a blue tick. Ab offers ki line laf jayegi…

Prerna sarcatically congratulates him. SAmeer tells her let’s celebrate. Prerna doesn’t want to go. SAmeer pleads, but Prerna refuses. 

Sameer asks her to be his friend again. He tells her that he wants things to go back to the way they were. 

But Prerna doesn’t want things to be the same. She tells him that she is fed up of doing Sameer’s dirty work. She was in love with Sameer and not this blue tick guy. 

She tells Sameer that she is going to south africa. This shocks Sameer. He asks Prerna tu mujhe chod ke aise kaise ja sakti hai tu to mujhse pyar karti hai. Prerna  sneers at this and `tells Sameer that even he doesn’t love the real Sameer. YOu have to love yourself before you can expect anyone else to love you. 



He receives a call from the organizer, congratulating him on his new verified status and blue tick. He tells him that he has a great offer for him and calls him for a meeting. 
Sameer goes for the meeting. THe organizer tells him that he has a couple of brands that want you to promote their products when you go to maldives and switzerland. Sameer is surprised. He tells the Pathak sir ki abhi to mera koi plan nahi hai na maldives jaane ka na switzerland. THe organizer smiles and says. Sir hame pata hai ki aap chahen to aj hi dono jagah ja sakte hain. Right? 

Sameer is shocked. Organizer sees his expressions and explains. Sir itna to hame pata chal jaata hai. Aap apne followers ko bewakoof bana sakte hain. Hame thodi na. Sameer is speechless. The organizer tells him. Ki tension na lo. Hame koi pareshaani nahi hai ki aap asli me jaate hain ki nahi. Hame to bas aapke followers se matlab hai. He pushes the packets towards him and tells him ki is hafte dono jagah chale jayiyega. HE laughs and leaves. 

Sameer is embarrased and in shock. He goes home and looks at all his pictures. 

 Sameer finally decides to come clean. He starts telling people about the lies he has built over the years. He posts about his real life and the day to day struggle he goes through. He starts losing followers. But Sameer doesn’t care. He recieves abuse online from people who used to look upto him, take his advice and aspire to be like him. 
After receiving online abuse one particular day, Sameer is fed up. He posts a video telling his story, about the life he aspired to have and the image he wanted people to percieve. But in the process he lost the girl he loved. He doesn’t say which girl, but this post is emotional and brutally honest. 

Prerna sees the change in Sameer and comes to 
.see him. She tells Sameer that his confession was beautiful and now he can start the rest of his real life. As Sameer and Prerna hug, Disha also comes back and is willing to take him back. Sameer tells Disha that the boy she loved doesn’t exist anymore. He was someone he created. 
Disha tells Sameer that she was in love with the real Sameer and not the fake one. She has also seen the post and knows that the Sameer she loved is back. Sameer is really happy to hear this as Prerna pushes him towards Disha. THey hug, living happily ever after.

================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (short_story | 2018_Crucible)
> **Core Spark:** A pandemic where news anchors parrot propaganda while infected humans eat their own organs; a protagonist surviving on Maggi who flees across India accompanied by a stoner girl, two armed survivors, and a movie star; featuring a tragic zombie who doesn't realize she has turned
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - Flesh out the Mumbai to Dehradun road trek checkpoints
> - Polish the poignant monologue of the un-converted zombie looking in the mirror

---

1. Viral Zombie infection. If the zombie does not eat human flesh, it will eat it’s own body from the inside. 

2. Stuck at home in Bombay.Does not want to go out. Watching TV all day. Masturbating. WAtching news coverage.

3. News coverage praising the government for the great job done in curbing the pandemic. No on ground coverage from abroad. Infection numbers fudged. 

4. HAs to go out to get maggi. Sees the real situation. Has to save a little girl. 

5. News anchor wants to meet the editor. Has done whatever has been said. Doesn’t believe it anymore. Finally wakes up to the reality. 

6. A celebrity. Farookh Khan (FRK). Two people go into his house to keep themselves safe. THey think that FRK has either become a zombie or is in hiding. Meet FRK.

7. Politicians. 

8. The end is realizing the enormity and making communities. 

9. Poor people. Migrant workers. Rich people. 

10. A zombie’s POV. This Zombie hasn’t fully converted. She thinks she is still human. She hides from other zombies. At the end she is trapped in between other zombies. THe drama builds but they ignore her. She is confused as to why. She finds out the she has turned into a zombie as well. She goes to kill herself. 
***************
***************

Character A: Stays at home in Mumbai. Quarantine guidelines (not to go out and interact with people physically). Talks to his mom and dad regularly on phone. He watches TV, News, social media at home. He gets contradicting information about the pandemic. People on twitter keep saying that situation is worse, but the government is saying - everything is under control. Even the news anchors say that the government is doing a good job and everything is under control. 
He gets his groceries and stuff ordered. But this time the delivery didn’t happen. He has been eating maggi for days now. 

Something happens to his mom: He is irritated with his mom answering the door everytime. He has been telling his mom not to open her door for anyone. One day, during a call, the bell to his mom’s house rings. Then something happens. He is unable to get in touch with her. He gets worried. And after a lot of deliberation decides to go all the way to Dehradun. 

He tries to book a flight but can’t. Maybe he tries to book a train. Goes to Airport/railway station. 

Now he has to find a car and supplies. First he leaves his house and confronts the real problem for the first time. 

He meets character B (a girl): SHe is looking for Ganja. A has to help find ganja for B. 

THey decide to go to dehradun together. 

On the way, they find character C and D. C and D (both girls) are resourceful people. A and D see the supplies that A has. (Maggi and Weed). They decide to raid the police station to get guns and weapons. Go to SRK’s house to get cars. They contemplate staying at his house.
SRK is alive and requests to come along.

On their journey, they see the real condition of the world. Most of the people have died or become zombies. 

B, C, D talk about repopulating earth. THey don’t know any other guy. THey fight about taking A for themselves. 

*************
Character E: A news anchor. (B storyline) She is spreading fake news. She herself doesn’t know the real facts. On ground reporting is not allowed. HOw will she find out the reality. SHe has been a part of the government’s propaganda machine for a long time.
***********
