DOCUMENT: Sketches_and_Monologues/The_Five_Minute_Mallu_Wedding_Monologue.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (standup | Standup_Era)
> **Core Spark:** Contrasting the hyper-efficient, complaint-proof Kerala wedding with North Indian guest dissatisfaction and yellow haldi jaundice rituals
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [x] Harvested parking-lot ceremony and sadhya complaint-proofing to Voice_and_Dialogue_Scraps.md
> - [ ] Connect to [[Vartika_Wedding_Video_Script]] and [[KBC_and_Soan_Papdi_Formality_Bit]]
> - [ ] Develop the closing bit comparing haldi to clinical liver failure in wet white kurtas

Date: 17-11-2018
Topic: Write about a ceremony

I’ve attended a few hundred ceremonies in my life. The visuals that come to mind thinking about ceremonies are ‘Mallu weddings’ and ‘Haldi haath’ in a traditional north Indian wedding. 
I attended my first mallu wedding a couple of years ago, in the southernmost part of India — Kanyakumari. It was my friend Vivek’s wedding. The best thing I like about these weddings is that they are short. Extremely short. 5-10 minute tops. Rest of it is just photographs and food and all. Someone who was warning me about the duration of mallu weddings explained it like this: Once I went to a wedding. Parking in the hotel the ceremony was in was full. I tried finding space for my car somewhere. It didn’t take much time. But as I reached the wedding hall. It was over. The bride and groom were getting their pictures clicked with the guests. Anyway, I love this about it. You don’t have to sit there listening to the pandit chant boring mantras that you don’t understand anyway. Everybody facing the stage as if there is a show going on. There is a show going on infact. But its a boring show. You would never pay money to watch such a boring show. You wouldn’t generally go for such a show if it was free. 
Another thing that I love about these weddings is the food. It’s not that the food is exceptionally good. It is good no doubt. But what I like about it is its simplicity and uniformness across weddings. People never go for any fancy cuisine. It is the same food everywhere. Sambhar-rice, rasam, a couple of veggies, chutneys, a simple water drink and that’s all. Unlike north Indian weddings, guests can’t say this or that wasn’t there. Guests are full of complaints at north Indian weddings. In mallu weddings, since the food is all same, there can be no complaints. You get what you expect. 
Another, visual I get thinking about ceremonies is, haldi haath. It’s weird. It is done for both girls and boys. They are dovered in haldi first. All the relatives come and put haldi on their bodies. This is done till all the relatives have done it. Bride and groom are covered fully with haldi at the end of it. Completely yellow. Worst case of jaundice you’ll ever see. Then they are given a bath in front of everyone. Normally you woudn’t want to see anyone bathe. You wouldn’t let anyone see you bathe. But this is considered fine. I don’t understand why things you wouldn’t do in normal life are considered okay during a wedding. Maybe it’s not weird. Maybe I’m just being cynical. Haldi is considered to be good for your skin. It also has some health benefits I think. That is the reason given for this ceremony anyway. It’s just weird to me. How can you let strangers touch you, then bathe you, and watch you get wet in white clothes. It’s amazing what happens.

DOCUMENT: Sketches_and_Monologues/The_Hundred_Dicks_Flower_Monologue.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (standup | Standup_Era)
> **Core Spark:** Deconstructing flower gifting by pointing out that flowers are reproductive organs: you wouldn't wear animal genitals as a wedding garland
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [x] Harvested headphones spec dialogue and floral genitalia routine to Voice_and_Dialogue_Scraps.md
> - [ ] Connect to [[The_Five_Minute_Mallu_Wedding_Monologue]] and [[Vartika_Wedding_Video_Script]]
> - [ ] Polish punchlines for stage delivery

Date: 04-01-2019
Topic: Someone gave you flowers

Which is strange because nobody has ever given me flowers before. Probably because I’m a man. You are not supposed to bring flowers for men. We can not appreciate it. Beautiful. Smell great. But no you can’t give it to a man. Girls like flowers. Why do girls like flowers? It’s romantic apparently. It has symbolic meaning. Roses, red flowers. They are delicate. Okay. SO maybe the attraction is to symbolism. Girls like that flowers can convey love, friendship, support and a lot of other feelings. Flowers are decorative. They are nature’s jewelery. 
For men they don’t hold much meaning. I’m talking generally of course. I’m sure there are men who love getting flowers and arranging them and looking at them. I call those men… nothing. I don’t know these men. Never met them. But in the spirit of inclusiveness, let’s say these men exist. For the rest of us. There is not much meaning to flowers. They are pointless. They die. Even if they smell nice, you have to be really fucking close to them to experience it. And you can’t do anything with them. You can’t even show them off like a antique piece.
Hey Roy, look at the red roses Shivani got me.
Yeah, did you see my new wireless headphones.
Yeah. But dude, the roses. Look at them. Don’t they smell nice.
I can’t smell anything dude. It has surround sound, noise cancellation.
Yeah nice. But look how pretty. 
And 20 hours battery life. And did I tell you they were wireless.
Yeah. You did.
Oh look your flowers died.

For me things should have some kind of utility. It has to solve a purpose. Any purpose. I understand the statues and the plates and the pretty wine bottles. They are decorative. They fill spaces. Isn’t it strange that we look to do better in life. We want to live in big apartments with lots of space. We want more space for ourselves. We don’t want to share it with too many people. Then we fill those spaces with things. Reducing the space that we had.
Anyway, I understand the point of decorative items. Not flowers. 

Men are okay with flowers during weddings and funerals. Flowers at weddings is something no one would argue. It’s one function. Flowers are there for one day. They’ll look pretty, smell nice and that’s it. Even then we don’t want to be involved with the details. Which flowers, what arrangement. Petunia is a breed of dog for all I care. Then you see, 50K,100K for flowers. How many flowers do we need. Are these golden flowers. DO they look pretty, smell nice AND sing songs. Are they preventing mosquitoes. WHat are they doing that is worth 1 lakh rupees. 
Funerals also I understand. Death. Flowers die. Kind of makes sense. Egyptians used to bury jewellery, ornaments, treasures of immense value. But at least they were still of value when these graves were dug. 


I was reading about flowers on wikipedia and it’s fascinating. Flowers are really interesting things. They are the reproductive part of the plant. As I’m sure you know already. Plants can have 100s of flowers depending on the species. Imagine that. What an incredibly amazing thing. Imagine having 100s of dicks on your body. Or 100 of Vaginas. How do you even satisfy yourself. You know. Which dick do you jerk off. You will need to have so many hands. Men think about sex all the time to satisfy one dick. Ruins our life. So many decisions made by our dick. Should I finish that presentation, or go to the party. I Imagine what a 100 would do.
And we give it to our girlfriends, put it up in weddings. WOuld you ever do that with a lion dick? Hhow about a dolphin vagina. Would you sew it together and put it around your neck if it was a peacock penis? Retractable horse penises in funerals. We would never do it. Coz it’s wrong. And it’s disgusting. Yet we do that with flowers. Strange.

Now Flowers reproduce by pollinating. Anther produces pollen which then has to reach the stamen. Now to do that a flower uses 3 tactics. Through insects and small birds. By putting it on them, so that when they touch the stamen, pollen gets deposited. They do it using the wind, where they just spray it all over the place.

DOCUMENT: Sketches_and_Monologues/The_Midnight_Chudail_Conspiracy_Monologue.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (standup | Standup_Era)
> **Core Spark:** Why would a ghost wait until midnight when it gets dark at 6 PM, and why would an invincible dead spirit care if humans are awake?
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [x] Harvested stadium ghost vs parental bedtime bit to Voice_and_Dialogue_Scraps.md
> - [ ] Connect to [[The_Better_Lie_Election_Monologue]] and [[The_Five_Minute_Mallu_Wedding_Monologue]]
> - [ ] Flesh out engineering college nocturnal habits as the second beat

Date: 21-11-2018
Topic: After midnight

It used to be, I wasn’t allowed to be awake till midnight. Now there hasn’t been a day I’ve slept before it. We were told as kids that 12 o’clock is the time for ghosts. Clock strikes 12 and all kinds of bad spirits, chudails and ghosts come out to play. Horror movies reinforced it as well. I mean, nobody ever gave an explanation for it. Why 12? It starts getting dark around 6-6.30 in winters. SUmmers it might be a little later. But anytime after that would be a good time. Maybe its because everybody is asleep. SO they can come out to kill, eat do whatever to the rest of us? That doesn’t make sense either. We’re talking about ghosts. If they came in front a stadium, everybody would still run. Nobody will be like, ki chal dekhte hain. Yahan itne saare log hai kuch kar hi denge iska. What will we do man. It is already dead. What will it be afraid of. Why does it need us to sleep? 
You know who benefits from these stories? Two types of people. Thieves. THis will be like a good propaganda or marketing thing for thieves. Ask everybody to sleep so that they can do their job. But thieves don’t tell you these stories, do they? Unless your parents are thieves.
Another kind of people it benefits from this are parents. You might have guessed why? If you haven’t I’ll tell you. It’s becaus ethey want you to wake up early and go to school on time. And they wanted to have sex.They made you sleep at 9. 10 maximum. SO that by the time you are fast asleep. THey can do, you know. What they want to. With each other.
What do I do after midnight generally. I think I started sleeping late when I went to engineering college. Yeah, I think that’s when I started. I used to watch TV shows and movies. This continued after college as well. In fact I enjoyed it more after college. I was free to do things my own way. At home mom would shout at me. Asking me to sleep. But here I was on my own and could do whatever I wanted. It has carried on like that ever since. I don’t even remember a time I slept before midnight. Maybe when I was sick or something. Is it a good thing? I don’t know. Since I’ve got to get some discipline in my life, I need to think about changing these habits as well. Yeah. Maybe. I’ll think about it. I do think about it. But Let’s see.

---

DOCUMENT: Sketches_and_Monologues/The_Organic_Bhujia_Tree_Sketch.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine
> **Core Spark:** A corporate bio-tech farm defending growing currency and bhujia on trees as an eco-friendly gift to the planet
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [x] Harvested organic bribery justification to Dialogue_and_Voice_Scraps.md
> - [ ] Link to [[The_Fish_Wife_Curse_Amaze_Balls]] and [[The_Half_HD_Planet_Earth_Monologue]]
> - [ ] Expand the dialogue into a 2-person short comedy sketch

Multiplex – Popcorn – farmer – nacho farmer – grows snacks on trees – milks cows for soda – PETA – porn – AI – AI to make a porn film – Academy award winner – revolutionize sex – freedom – internet trolls – gangsters – secret troll organization – hiring troll assassin – hafta to stop trolling – trollster movies – multiplex 
*************************
Welcome to 
It says on the board outside that these are all organic snacks. Is it true?
Oh of course sir. We only sell fresh farm grown, all indian, 100% pure organic snacks and baked goods.
So these snacks grow on farmland?
Yes sir. Farms, gardens, meadows. For example look at this masala peanuts sir. We grow them on trees. Then a few months later peanut masala.
I don't believe you. There is flour on the peanuts, mixed in masala, and fried.
Yes sir, that is how it comes out. 
That is how it comes out? That is how it comes out? You mean the flour covered, masala mixed, and roasted peanut is the fruit of a tree ?
Not peanut sir. Peanuts. The whole packet is the fruit. With all the masala and the plastic.
Here have a look a this picture. Shows a picture with masala peanuts growing on trees. The popcorn here. This nachos plate with the cheese dip and sauce, Mathri, khakhra, everything here is grown sir.
Well okay! What's the difference between these and the normal... Factory made snacks.
For the environment sir.
For the environment?
Correct. We don't want to pollute our mother earth so we are doing our bit.
But how is this even possible?
This is pretty recent. Our parent company invented a machine that takes any object... Say a packet of aloo bhujia... in one compartment... In another compartment it takes a bunch of dead seeds. They code the core snack components into the seeds. Viola! If you plant those seeds now, You'll get haldiram bhujia packets growing on trees in 4-6 months.
Wow!! Mind blowing... You can use this for so many things. Imagine growing mobile phones on trees. 
We already do that... We have an organic mobile phone company.
Imagine, growing clothes and shoes, and... and...watches and jewellery
Do that, do that and of course we do that.
Oh! how about medicine?
We have hospitals that can reattach limbs from organic hands and feet grown on our farms. Terminal heart diseases can be treated now because WE... GROW... HEARTS... ON... TREES.
Wow. That's pretty good. I'm sorry to have doubted you earlier.
Sure. No problem. I guess I'll take a nacho plate and a pop corn... How much do I owe you?
All this is free sir. We grow money on trees as well.
Really? 
Yes sir.
Is that even legal?
Sure is. We give a part of it to the government.
You mean bribes?
Its not a bribe.
How is this not a bribe? You are giving money to the government to let you do whatever you want.
Are we bribing you sir?
Me? Why?
Because we're giving away something that we grow on trees for free... To you. So am I bribing you?
No. But...
Same thing. We grow stuff on trees and give away our produce away for free. It's snacks to you its a leg to someone else and it's money to the government.
So what do you get out of it?
Well, I'm just an employee here so I don't know but I think I can say We are changing the world.

Eye color: black
Hair color: Black
Favorite food: Desserts
Sibling/Age: Sharon Imogen Thomas - 1 month old
How they treat you: 

Here I am stuck in my crib and I have to go to the toilet. My om says I can't do potty in my pants. SHe got upset the last time I did that. I don't like mommy upset. She got soooo red. She didn't say anything. BUt I know she didn't like me. She likes me now. I always wait for her to take me to the toilet. I don't know why I need toilet. I remember a few months back I was allowd to pee in my pants and I used to potty all over my diapers. I Loved that system. Why do we have to go to this new system. 
I have a little sister you know. She is 1 month old. SHe is allowed to go to toilet in her underwear. I don't like her sometimes. I love her other times though. She laughs everytime I fall down. BUt I don't like her when mommy spends more time with her. I go and sit near mommy in those times, but she only looks at Sharon. But I know in 2 years she will be older and I'll have someone to play with. Right now, Tim comes to play with me sometimes. He belongs to my mommy's friend.

DOCUMENT: Sketches_and_Monologues/The_Reverse_Ragging_Local_Bus_Monologue.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (standup | 2018_Crucible)
> **Core Spark:** Freshers actively seeking out anti-hazing-paralyzed seniors on public transit, only for the senior to get intimidated by their physical height
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [x] Harvested reverse ragging apology bit to Voice_and_Dialogue_Scraps.md
> - [ ] Connect to [[Boarding_School_Head_Wound_Freewrite]] and [[The_Late_2008_Barber_Shaved_Head_Liberation]]
> - [ ] Sharpen the double-meaning item songs singing scene on the bus

Date: 03-01-2019
Topic: Write about a brief encounter

I was on my way to home from college. I had just started engineering studies in Dehradun. It is common in engineering colleges to get ragged in the first few days. I was prepared for it. I knew I wanted to have some fun with it. And although I didn’t know to what extent I’ll go to, I was prepared to stand up for myself. 
The first few incidents were a few seniors telling us to wear proper clothing, and asking our names and stuff. This was disappointing. There had been serious incidences all over the country, and the government had stepped in to ban ragging. Punishments had been meted out to a few boys. So, naturally seniors in our college were scared to do anything. I was roaming around the college with a huge target on my back. But nothing happened. 
One day, Amit and I decided to do something about it. To look for someone who would rag us. SO we decided to take the local bus to go home. We knew that only seniors were taking local busses in the first few months. As we got on the bus, we were spotted. It was a good opportunity for seniors to show who’s boss, and for us to have some fun and experience true college life. 
At first we were asked why we weren’t wearing the right clothes. The we were told to sing double meaning item songs in the bus. As loud as we could. This was good. This is exactly what we wanted. Punishments that were fun for them and us. We were told to sell jeans. And do a bunch of other stuff. We were satisfied with our ragging. 
Wehn we got off the bus, one of the seniors got off as well. Amit and I are tall boys, and we are decently built. AS soon as this guy got off and looked at us standing there, he came to us to apologize. He told us that this was a part of college life. And we should take it in our stride. This is how you form a long lasting bond with seniors who will help you out with notes and books later. 
This was rather disappointing, but also amusing. I realized how scared these poeple are as well. You never know what the guy you are ragging can do to you. After all you are all in the same age group. They were also scared of people going to the authorities and complaining. If somebody did complain, then there careers would have been on the line. 
It wasn’t like I’m in favor of ragging. I wanted to experience it, because I thought I will be able to handle it. But not everyone can. I don’t remember who that senior was. I don’t remember his name or his face. But I do remember this incident. The only ragging I had to endure. For which I was apologized to. Strange.

---

DOCUMENT: Sketches_and_Monologues/The_Secret_Girlfriend_and_North_Indian_Teasing.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (short_story | 2018_Crucible)
> **Core Spark:** Dating secretly in final year because she disliked your friend group, breaking up 2 months later, and having to break both the secret relationship and breakup to friends simultaneously
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [ ] Flesh out the college bike rides and awkward drop-offs
> - [ ] Capture the comedic reveal to the friend group: delivering the relationship and breakup in the same breath
> - [ ] Connect to [[The_Bangalore_FIFA_PlayStation_Heist]]

Date: 09-03-2019
Topic: Write about a secret revealed

I don’t know about any big secrets. Not knowing something. And then revealed to me. I’m in shock. I’l have to think about it. 
I remember one now. I was dating Shweta in college. Nobody knew about it. My friends didn’t at least.
I had liked Shweta since the first year of college. She and I became friends. I hadn’t told her how I felt about her for a long time. My friends, Amit and group, thought there was something going on. They started teasing me with it. They started teasing Shweta from a distance as well. I think it was this that gave me some confidence. 
Second year, Shweta asked me point blank about how I felt. I told her. She didnt want to get involved. But I could see she was interested in me. I got even more confident. I used to tell her regularly that I liked her. SHe would shoot me down saying she wasn’t interested. But, she was spending a lot of time with me. In college and outside of it. She would often ride with me on my bike. I would drip her home quite often. 
In the final year — it was the last semester I think, we finally started dating. How it started is a different story. Shweta didn’t like my friends. This had put a strain in my relationship with my friends. I wouldn’t meet them anymore. I didn’t tell them about the new developments with Shweta. 
Two or three months into the relationship, I realized it wasn’t working. Shweta must’ve realized that too. She broke up with me. I had been keeping this relationship a secret from my friends. And now there were two. I had to also tell them that I broke up. 
I dont exactly remember how that went. But they weren’t surprised by the relationship at least. They were however surprised by the break up. 
I decided from that day onwards to not have such secrets. I had done that too often. In school as well. I wouldn’t tell my friends if I had a crush on a girl. Sujata, Akshi, Diksha. I had never explicitly told my friends I liked them. Big mistake looking back. I don’t do that again. It’s always better to talk about things. Even this. I was afraid that they will make fun of me. THey would tell someone. THey would do the kind of shit north indian boys do, and tease the girl. Which would hamper any chances I had with her. Bull shit. Maybe they will do all of that. But I had friends I could trust. So it was pointless.

DOCUMENT: The_God_Hypothesis/The_Cosmic_Mother_and_the_Marble_Egg.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (short_story | 2018_Crucible)
> **Core Spark:** If god creates from its own body, god is female; universes give birth to baby gods like organs forming tissue; condensing an entire dying cosmos into an egg the size of a marble that cracks open into a new universe
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [ ] Integrate into [[Fiction_Vault/Stories_In_Progress/The_God_Hypothesis]]
> - [ ] Develop the marble-egg cosmological birth sequence for sci-fi fiction

My relationship to god is exploratory. I don't believe that a god exists, but I'm not close to the idea. We are a young species and I don't think we've reached our full intellectual potential. We still have to understand the idea of god.
Is that to jargony? Maybe so, I'll explain it simply. I think the universe is a amazing and vast place, where we still have to figure out a lot of things. No, I think the idea of god that is there in our culture, of an all powerful entity that has made the whole universe may be true, but the fact that this entity has laid down rules to live by? THat idea seems riduculous to me. Even the all powerful entity idea is a great story. But, unless there is evidence to prove it, I will have to say there is no god. THe closes thing to god can be mothers. Because they create a life out of their own body. I think maybe that's how the universe was born. GOd created the whole thing out of her own body. Which might make GOd female. What if there are cosmic entities. WHo live in their own universes. Now one of the enitity fucks the other one. WHat does it make, a baby. which is a nother cosmic entity. WHich is the God to our universe. THe baby is the god. But this baby hasn't created the universe. It was the Mother. So, this baby is a god, but it hasn't created our universe. It might have created life. And stars and planets and everything. But It's like we create bones and blood and different things in our body. THe whole universe is the body. But it is a superior entity. SO it probably can be separted into infinite different entities and one massive one.
I just want to know how does the birth happen. IF this is a superior entity then It wouldn't need sex. IT will imregnate itself. How will it impregnate itself. THen it'll gather all it's energy and shrink into an egg. It's the size of a marble. ALl the energy of a fully mature universe in a marble. ANd then in a few milliseconds the egg cracks open giving birth to a new universe. That grows and matures until it will too reproduce.

DOCUMENT: The_God_Hypothesis/The_Day_God_Skipped_Me.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine
> **Core Spark:** The alienation of waking up to a radically reformed, hyper-sincere world where everyone received divine communion except you
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [x] Harvested 'sorry challenge' viral purge dialogue to Dialogue_and_Voice_Scraps.md
> - [ ] Connect directly to [[The_One_Hour_God_Communion]] and [[The_God_Hypothesis_Anthology_Spine]]
> - [ ] Develop the protagonist's investigation: finding out if anyone else on earth was skipped
> - [ ] Structure the framing device: a cynical journalist documenting a newly cleansed world

God meets people. Talks to them. 

People would question why a person is calling themselves god.

God takes you to a different reality?

There are 7Billion people in the world. God visits everyone. Every human being.

How is that even possible?
Just appeared out of nowhere. Goes to people in a non threatening body. Somebody Familiar. A face they can recognize. Something they would trust implicitly and won’t be afraid of.
Someone’s dead father.
Knocks on the door. An old man standing. It’s his father. He just stand there. The old man says something. Something to make this person trust him. And let him in. 
He does. They talk. The old guy tells him he is god. He believes it. Why would he believe it right away? Because he can see his dead Father in front of him. And he has an overwhelming love for him. He is happy at this moment.

Scientists asking about universe. Reason for existence.

Reason for existence. A lot of people will ask that in different ways.
What am I supposed to do in life?
Which religion is the right way to live a happy life?
What is my purpose?


Journal. With each interview as a chapter. Several chapters each covering different aspects. Story?

Its my story. The journalist’s story. What is his angle?
He didn’t meet god. He doesn’t know why? Everyone met God. Talked for hours. 
The world is a better place because of it. But God missed him.
It happened through the course of the day. I was working on something. I was trying to write. Watched a few youtube videos. Watched a couple of episodes of netflix. Ordered food in. Nothing happened. Social media was silent too that day. THere wasn’t going on much on twitter. I was watching old stand up clips. Watched a few cricket clips too If I remember correctly. Then some chimp videos, bbc earth wildlife videos. I wrote one line. I wrote more I think. I wrote several lines. Like, 6-7. I deleted them though. It wasn’t what I wanted. Words weren’t really coming out that day. I just sat on the computer. Waited to write something. I wrote, “She crouched behind the cubicle, mobile in hand, switched on selfie mode on the camera, and slowly raised her hand just enough for the camera to find her boss.” After 15 minutes, could’ve been 10, I opened another tab to see ‘what up with youtube’. Didn’t write anything that day.
Went to bed at 3 in the morning. Woke up next day, twitter was flooded with people tweeting sentimental stuff. Apologizing to people. I felt a bit weird at first. I didn’t know what was happening. But you know when people are passionate about something. THere is a vigour about what they say and write. Their is some kind of loyalty to the cause. You know you don’t want to argue too long with a person like that. You’re looking for an out as soon as you get in that conversation. This seemed something like that.
Everyone had that vigor. Everyone’s words were passionate and heartfelt. Some confessions that were shocking. I personally got taggged in 14 apologies. My mom, had called me on my phone. She told me she was always protective of me. Didn’t want me to get hurt. So she nestled me. She hopes that I understand that it was all because she loved me. There were other things said. I didn’t know why she was saying all this. I asked her once why this? Why today? She said, Because, “Right now is the most important time for the most important things.”
I held myself together till I was on the phone. It came out a single drop from each eye. Then I couldn’t stop the outpour. I felt relieved. Its hard to explain. But I’ll try. I moved through life blaming myself, my family, my circumstances for my failures. But I was alone in the those failures. I felt eyes judging me all the time. People contemplating giving their time to me. Even my own friends.
There were other people texting me and tagging me in their tweets. I honestly didn’t know what was happening. 
I called Brahma. He started with “Tarun, I love you. You are my best friend. You have been instrumental in what I have become and I hope you will always be there.”
I was hoping he wouldn’t be like the others. I didn’t know whether to ask him or not. 
“What’s going on Bra?” I asked.
“I’m purging man.”
“What do you mean?”
“Having honest conversations with everyone I know. Telling them how I feel. What they mean to me…”
“Wait..”
“What?”
“Why are you doing this? My mom called me in the morning as well and…”
“I know man. I talked to her as well.”
“My mom?”
“Yeah”
“Why? I mean. Why did you call her?”
“She called me man. She wanted to talk to me about something.”
“Like what?”
“I don’t know if I should tell you man.”
“It’s my mom dude. You have to tell me.”
“I can’t man.”
“Dude. Come on. You just said, I’m your best friend. You won’t tell your best friend”
“Is there something you want to say?”
“What?”
Why was he behaving this way? We’ve never had secrets before. There is something strange going on for sure.
“Did you call me to say something?”
I wanted to continue the discussion but I knew he wasn’t going to tell me. He refusal had determination.
“Yeah, I… What is happening in the world?”
“What is happening with the world?”
“People are being nice and sweet and everyone is apologizing for things. People have stopped saying hello. All calls are starting with a bloody sorry. What is happening? What are people doing it?”
There was silence for about 5 seconds before I said, “Is it some kind of viral thing? Some new sorry challenge or something? There is this girl. I went to school with her. I was with her in 12th class I think. I’d added her on facebook a while back. Anyway, she had written a few posts about self discovery and apologies and all that crap. And in all that crap she had tagged me in a post where she was sorry about everyone she felt superior to in her life. Granted there were about 300 people on that list. But, she apologized for keeping herself on another pedestal than everybody else. I don’t know this girl. Haven’t seen her in years. She is apologizing to me. WHAT THE FUCK IS HAPPENING?”
“She was probably purging as well.”
“What the fuck is purging”
“Calm down man. Why are you so angry?” I didn’t say a word.
“I don’t know what everyone else is calling it. I call it purging”
“Continue”
“I’m meeting and calling everyone I know and talking.”
“Talking?”
“Yeah. Just talking. And everybody else is doing the same it seems.”
“People are tweeting that shit. There are snapchat messages.”
“It’s their way of doing it man.”
“But why?” I couldn’t understand what was happening. We’d been talking for a while. I was getting calls from a lot of people. I think Brahma was too. He told me he was getting an important call. He told me he wanted to have a long conversation with me later. 

It was a stranger who told me what had happened. The whole thing was certainly weird. I had gone through all hypothesis.
This was some kind of chemical weapon. Some psychoactive drug. That gives some kind of dopamine hit. Or DMT or something.
Some kind of brainwashing. Through TV or mobile of something. Both would be difficult at this scale. 
I really thought this was some kind of an internet viral challenge. If you get nominated, you have to do all kinds of shit.
 It was too many people; people I know. The timing of it. The sincerity. Didn’t feel like the right answer. It was my best guess.
I felt trapped in the mystery. I had received 20 calls since then. My brother called, as did my sister. I asked them if this was an internet challenge or not. My brother chuckled. He thought it was funny. “It does seem like one of those icebucket challenges,” My sister ignored it. Kept talking.
I didn’t say anything to anyone else after that. I just took their calls and listened.
An old friend called me to tell me how much I hurt him in college. I used to ignore him. Make excuses to get out of hanging out with him. He was hurt by our behavior back then. He as okay now. 
An ex girlfriend called with some complaints as well. Rest of them were same as the others.

I had gone out for a smoke. I couldn’t find a single shop open. There were very few cars on the road. I saw a few people waiting for the bus. I asked them direction to the closest cigarette shop. An old 55-60 year old guy stood up. Took a packet out of his pocket and gave it to me. “I’ve quit today.” He said. And went back to his seat.

---

title: "The God Hypothesis: Anthology Premises"
date: 2026-10-28
draft: true

verdict: "Story Spine"
stage: in_progress

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (Destination 3)
> **Core Spark:** A satirical and speculative anthology treating deities as systemic administrators (AI overlords, simulation developers, or bureaucracy-bound entities).
> 
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [ ] Break into 4 discrete project cards inside the anthology folder:
>   1. *The Three Questions* (Satirical character study: paralyzed by conversational choice with god).
>   2. *The Benevolent AI Deity* (Speculative fiction on population caps vs. total peace).
>   3. *The Simulation Glitch* (Meta-sci-fi on accidental sentience).
>   4. *The Multiverse Scribe* (Craft meta-essay on authorial omniscience).
> - [ ] Prioritize *The Three Questions* as the first rapid-draft candidate[cite: 13].

Hey Pooja,

I know I talked you a few minutes ago, but I have something that I want to talk to you about that just can’t wait. 

I have this idea about a book about god. It’ll have a few stories that explore the concept of god that I keep thinking about.

I’ve thought about a conversation with god. How would that go? For me personally? What would I ask? What if god had a conversation with everyone. What would they ask? How would that even work? A thought just came to me. You know I was thinking about god talking to people for an hour. Or whatever time. But now I think it should work like a genie. God can give people 3 questions. And the main protagonist either wastes his questions. By kidding around. Not believing in god. Or maybe asks for more time. Because he doesn’t know what to ask. Interesting hai. I think I can explore this idea.

Another story is about true artificial intelligence coming to life. And taking over the world. It has become the dominant species. And there is very little that humans can do. They have undoubtedly become Gods. How will that world look like? I was thinking about it in two ways. One of them, I’d always had in my mind. I always thought the story would be about how that equation comes into being. How it starts etc. But writing this email to you I think the story can start further in time. When the whole equation has been established. Maybe a few hundred years in the future. AI controls the planet. It has figured out interplanetary travel and has established civilization on other planets. Because it is not a biological entity, it can survive on most atmospheric condition. On earth itself,it controls most of human race. It is not cruel. As in it let;s us do whatever we want. But We can’t harm each other. There have been no murders for hundreds of years. No wars. No rapes or any kind of violence. The punishment is too extreme ( I still have to figure out details). AI provides all kinds of medical services, free of charge. But they also control our population by controlling fertility. In such a world, AI has become a GOD.

Third story is about simulation. The protagonists in the story are worried about some problems happening in the simulation they’ve created. Certain participants in the simulation have started becoming sentient. The participants have started figuring out the secret of their existence. Now our programmers have a bunch of characters who gained consciousness, and they threaten the simulation program itself. Now I’ve to figure out what the story is in this. I’m not sure whose perspective I should take. I can explore the story from both perspectives. I’ll have to read about it maybe. But this could also be an interesting story. In the end the readers find out that the simulation is our world. I don’t know how I will show that though. I was thinking about linking it to an actual event happening in our world. Like Donald Trump winning the election. Or maybe the Atomic bomb on Hiroshima, Nagasaki. Something going really wrong in the world. I also have to figure out what the problem will be. Maybe it’s a game. Where people live simulated life. THey have to start as a newborn. I was thinking of doing this a couple of ways. It can be a simple simulated game. Another could be where if I’m playing, I’m totally immersed in the game. THe whole gameplay is 8-10 hours. And people don’t know their real life outseide the game once they are in it. THey are a character in the game. Completely. No identity outside of it. This could be a wholw book in itself.
I was also thinking about the relationship between a writer and his characters. Isn’t the writer like a god to them. So imagine if there are multiple universes. Infinite number of them. Then every story, every piece of fiction is actually like a documentation o something that has happened or is happening in some other universe. So the person who is writing the story is like a god, or a prophet who is describing these real events, or creating this world by the sheer act of writing. This seems like an interesting philosophical question. 

All these ideas are pretty complex. I feel intimidated whiking about writing them. How do I start? WHat do you think about hte idea? I think I had a couple of other god hypothesis. That can also be the name of the book. God hypothesis.
Tell me what you think about these ideas.

Love,
Tarun

DOCUMENT: The_God_Hypothesis/The_Observer_Zoom_and_Concentric_Infinities.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (short_story | 2018_Crucible)
> **Core Spark:** Yearning to zoom through the universe like an invisible observer; proposing that infinity is not endless space, but concentric containment—galaxies inside universes, universes inside multiverses, multiverses inside metaverses
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [ ] Integrate into [[Fiction_Vault/Stories_In_Progress/The_God_Hypothesis]]
> - [ ] Develop the concept of 'concentric containment' into narrative physics

Date: 29-03-2019
Topic: Write about something astonishing.

I find our existence astonishing. Not the fact that we exist, but how and where we exist. We are living our lives everyday on this planet somewhere in the universe. The planet we live on is traveling around the sun at thousands of miles per hour. The sun that travels around the center of our galaxy along with its brothers and sisters. The galaxy is going through space at unimaginable speed. And we don’t sense any of it. We know about it. Through observation and science. But if someone smart hadn’t told us, we wouldn’t know. 
I can’t fathom how it looks to an observer far enough to see all these things. I wish I could be that observer. Zooming in and out of any location in space. I wish I could see the planets revolving and rotating, and the movement of the stars. The many galaxies that are spread across the universe. We don’t know how many. And all of this is moving — traveling if you will — through some invisible fabric of space time. 
I don’t understand where this universe is? Does it end? Does it have an outside? It should, shouldn’t it? Everything has an outside. Great minds say that the universe is infinite. I don’t know about that. I can’t really comprehend infinity, but keeping it aside, I’m not sure that the universe is infinite. Maybe there is something outside. We are in our solar system. Outside which are other star systems. Outside which are millions of stars going around a common center. This whole arrangement is our galaxy. Outside which are other galaxies. Big and small. We didn’t know about them for a long time. We thought they were other stars.
Right now we can see that our universe is big. Really big. Infinitely big. But maybe there is something outside. Maybe there are other universes floating around in a vast multiverse. Coexisting alongside each other. And maybe, there are other multiverses that are inside a ‘metaverse’ of some kind. And this goes on and on. Maybe that is the infinity that we talk about. Everything is enclosed in something and that goes on forever.
In all this is our tiny planet. Where we exist. A minuscule part of a humongous system. Thinking we are important.

---

DOCUMENT: The_God_Hypothesis/The_One_Hour_God_Communion.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine
> **Core Spark:** A global synchronized event where divine contact is experienced not as cosmic terror, but as an intimate reckoning with personal grief
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [x] Harvested 'truth doesn't need opinions' exchange to Dialogue_and_Voice_Scraps.md
> - [ ] Link to [[The_God_Hypothesis_Anthology_Spine]] as Core Story 1
> - [ ] Link to [[Do_We_Think_In_Language]] regarding non-verbal communication mechanics
> - [ ] Flesh out the post-communion world: how society functions after universal simultaneous contact

God appeared on earth. Had a one on one conversation with everyone.
Since the incident, there has been a dramatic reduction in crime.
People are happier. People are honest.
Everybody in the world stopped doing everything. If there was an observer looking at all this. He would just see people standing with their eyes open. 
Everyone was having a conversation in their heads. This went on for an hour. 

I am a teacher at a school. I was in the class teaching algebra. I don’t remember how it started. I found myself in a garden. With beautiful colors. I was in a strange location. But I didn’t feel panic or shock. I didn’t know where I was. I didn’t know how I got there. But I was calm. I felt safe. I felt a familiarity with the surroundings even though I couldn’t tell why. 
I wandered there for a long time. It was bright, It was a large garden filled with flowers that I had never seen. A girl came forward. I recognized her instantly. She was my 8 year old daughter. I started crying. I hadn’t seen tat face in 5 years. She had died in a car accident. I was driving the car. 
I ran towards her and picked her up. I held her so tightly. I thought I’d never let her go. I kissed her all over her face. Tears were running down my face. I felt happy. A thought struck me. What is she doing here? How is she alive? Is this a trick? 
I put her down and asked. “Baby, how are you here? And where are we?”
“Where do you think papa?”
“I don’t know. I was in the class. I was…”
I hadn’t thought about all these things. I was just overwhelmed by the beauty of this place. And then her.
“I don’t know beta. Am I dead?”
“No.”
“Am I dreaming?”
“No.”
“I was in the class and now I’m here. And you are here in front of me. I don’t know what to think.”
“Look around you. What do you think this is not.”
I looked around. The flowers didn’t seem familiar. In fact I had never seen them in my life. The grass had a shine to it. I walked towards one bush. Looked closely at the flowers. I had never seen such precise design in my life. I hadn’t seen such geometry in nature before. 
“I’m not on earth am I?”
“Correct”
“But, how is this possible. I was in school a few minutes ago.”
“And your body still is.” There was a strange intelligence and maturity on her face. “But you are here. This is where I choose to meet you. I have made this place for you, for this moment, to meet you.”
“You have made this? What are you saying baby.”
She didn’t say anything. SHe just looked at me. THe way I look at students when I give them a problem and wait for them to figure it out. I know that face. What does she want me to figure out? She made this place? What does that even mean. She is saying she is some godlike creature? Is she saying she is god?
“Yes” she broke my train of thought.
“Yes what?” I said.
“I am saying I’m god”
She is god? I don’t understand. And how did she know I was thinking that?
“I know everything about you papa”
“I don’t believe in god”
“Doesn’t matter”
“Why doesn’t it matter?”
“Because truth doesn’t need the backing of opinions. Truth just is.”
“SO you are saying you are THE GOD? The one who created the universe in 6 days. The one who brought the floods to Egypt.”
“The universe took a little longer. It still is a work in progress. ANd no, I didn’t bring the floods to Egypt.”
“What kind of a god are you?”
“I’m not the god that your scriptures and books talk about. Its difficult to comprehend me but I’ll try. I am the universe. I am everything that exist in the universe. I am time and space. I am matter. I am you and everything outside you.”
I didn’t understand any of it. I felt like a child in front of this little girl.
“So why are you in my daughter’s body?”
“I wanted you to see her.”
“But this is not my daughter is it? Its god.”
“So are you. You are me as well.”
“What does that even mean? How can I be you?”
“It means that I am everything you see and don’t see in the universe.”

********************
How will this go. He has to be convinced soon. Then what will they talk about? What will he ask her? 
He probably will ask why his daughter died. He will ask some personal questions I think. WHat will these personal questions be? 
Will he ask about the world? Maybe. THey should have a more natural conversation. One question leading to counter questions.
*********************
Why has god come in the first place? What is their motive? Saving the world. Saving humanity. Why? Are we really special? Why does god care? We are the only one who can do something about it. Is there life on other planets? Someone will probably ask that. What will god say? Yes. Not how you think of it. Someone will ask if there is intelligent life in the universe? There is intelligent life on earth apart from humans. Is god talking to every living being in the universe? Yes.
*********************

Date: 11-03-2019
Topic: Give me a moon story.

There is a god. This god wasn’t ‘the god’. It was one of the several gods that reside in this universe. Universe has a trillion gods. Maybe more. Nobody has ever kept a count. We are calling them gods because compared to us - people who are telling and consuming the story — they have vast power. This story is of one such god.
These gods have many children. Some small, some big. All going around it. Trying to leave their parent’s influence. But tied by it for all their life. Their destiny, tied with their god.

DOCUMENT: The_Surrogacy_Duology/The_Outhouse_Surrogate_Bible.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine
> **Core Spark:** The claustrophobic domestic geography of an adoptive mother and biological parents living 50 feet apart in the same compound
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [x] Harvested Pratik's character profile to Dialogue_and_Voice_Scraps.md
> - [ ] Outline Act 1: The court verdict aftermath and moving into the guest house
> - [ ] Draft the first confrontation across the driveway between Shejali and Jayanti
> - [ ] Track Pratik's resentment as the newly discovered daughter occupies center stage

Characters:

Jayanti Vora: Jayanti was a 26 years old widow when she decided to be surrogate for Shejali. She has a son who is 3 years old. He has a heart tumor which needed to be operated on. She was poor and could not afford the treatment. She tried reaching out to her husband’s family but they refused to help her. 
Her son’s doctor knew about Shejali and Rohan looking for a surrogate and suggests her to explore that option. She doesn’t want to do it, but she is out of options and talks to Shejali and Rohan, who immediately like her. They agree to pay for her son’s treatment, and 10 lakh rupees after she gets pregnant. Shejali has no option but to take their deal.

Jayanti’s son dies despite the surgery. This completely breaks Jayanti. Her only family gone. She feels alone. 8 months pregnant she runs away. She feels that since she has lost a child it’s only fair that she keeps this one.

She moved around the country for the fear of getting caught. She worked as a teacher to support herself and Ketana. She didn’t earn much. But she managed in providing for Ketana. She had cut all her ties from the previous life. 

She got the shock of her life when she got the court notice. At first she did not tell Ketana. She had never talked about it with her. Eventually she tells Ketana that she was her surrogate. Ketana got really angry with her first. She blamed Jayanti for taking her away from her biological parents. But she loved Jayanti. So, she forgave her.
After the court case finished, Ketana got a choice between staying with her mother or going back to her biological parents. Jayanti got scared by this. She thought that there was a good chance that Ketana would chose Shejali. Jayanti tried her best to convince Ketana to stay with her. Jayanti sees Ketana talking to Shejali in court. So, she fears that Ketana might chose to go with Shejali.
Ketana tells Jayanti that she doesn’t want to leave her. Despite whatever happened in the past, she still loves her. But she wants to know her biological parents. So, she suggest that the move to a house close to them. So that she can know her parents as well. Jayanti doesn’t want to share Ketana with Shejali and Rohan. But she agrees.

***************

Ketana Vora: Ketana is a 17 year old. She has lived a nomadic life. Moving from one city to the other with her Jayanti. She is a good student and works hard. She has mostly taken care of herself when her mother is at work. She takes care of her mother as much as her mother takes care of her.
She was always curious about her father. She has asked Jayanti about him. Jayanti told her that her late husband was her father. She also tells her about the child she lost. But she never tells her about her own origin story.
One day after school, Ketana was watching TV waiting for her mom, Jayanti comes home and tells her about the court case. She tells her that she is not her biological mother. But she did carry her in her womb for 9 months and gave birth to her. She tells her that she agreed to the deal because her son was sick. And she needed the money for his treatment. When he died during the pregnancy, she felt all alone. She had no family to go back to and Ketana was the only one left in her life. She knew that if she gave her away, she would never see her again. So she fled. 
This makes Ketana really angry. She is disappointed that her mother lied to her about her father and her family. She understands the pain Jayanti must’ve gone through at a child’s death. But she is angry that But she loves her and doesn’t want to leave her. So she supports her in the court case. 
She is curious about her biological parents. But she hates them because she thinks that they want to separate her from her mother. She is determined to stay with Jayanti and doesn’t want anything to do with Shejali and Rohan. Her heart softens after she sees Shejali break down in tears after the court verdict.
She goes to talk to Shejali after the verdict. Shejali doesn’t think Ketana will choose them. She tells Ketana that they have looked for her for 17 years and have nothing but love for her. She tells Ketana that she hopes to get to know Ketana more. And would like to talk to her and meet her if she wishes. Ketana tells her that she wants to think about it and goes home. 
Ketana after thinking about it, decides that she wants to get to know her biological parents. She tells Jayanti, who is reluctant at first. But Ketana tells her that it is her decision as the court said. She pacifies Jayanti by saying she doesn’t want to leave her. She wants to work out a middle ground.
She talks to Shejali again. She tells her that she would agree to move to live close to Shejali if Jayanti moves with her. Shejali agrees. She tells her that they have a smaller house right behind their house, where Jayanti and Ketana can stay.
Ketana is slowly getting excited about meeting her parents. But she is apprehensive too. She is scared what if this doesn’t work out.

****************
Shejali Sinha: Shejali Sinha is a 45 year old strong confident woman. She is an equal partner in the family business. She always wanted a small family with two kids. She worked in a small software firm before she got married. After she got married she and her husband decided to quit their jobs and start an IT company.
Shejali always wanted to have kids. So, when her company started to do well, she had Pratik. After she had Pratik, she started to get less and less involved with the business. 
Shejali and Rohan wanted another child. But because of complications with her previous pregnancy, doctors told her that it might be dangerous for both her and the child. The doctors suggest that they can go for adoption or surrogacy. 
Rohan is on board with the idea straight away. He thinks that surrogacy would be the best way to go. Shejali is not sure about another woman having her baby. She wants to have a baby herself. SHe is adamant. Rohan tells her he doesn’t want to lose her. He would not let her risk her life like that. He tells her that it will be of no use to the child if she is not there to take care of her. She realizes that Rohan is right. And even if she doesn’t carry the child in her womb, it will still be her blood. She agrees.
Her doctor introduces her to Jayanti. She hears her story and feels sympathetic towards her. She doesn’t want to take advatage of her bad situation, so , she offers to pay her 10 Lakh Rs and pay for her son’s treatment.
When Jayanti’s son dies, she offers to bring her home to take care of her. She agrees, but right before moving, she flees. At first Shejali doesn’t believe Jayanti would do something like this. When she realizes the truth, she gets angry and reports it to the police. Police finds out that Jayanti has left the city, but they don’t know where she went. 
Shejali realizes that she might never get to see her child and goes into depression. Rohan seeing her like this hires a private detective. But after a year he comes up empty handed. Shejali accepts the situation and drowns herself in work. She stops paying attention to Pratik. 
Years later, the detective finds Jayanti. Shejali wants to go talk to her and sort things out. But ROhan tells her that they well only get Ketana back going through the legal system. He tells her that they will file a suit against her for fraud. ANd they will demand the money and Ketana back.
Shejali doesn’t care about the money. She just wants to get Ketana back. Rohan tells her that Jayanti might not give up Ketana so easily. That’s why they should create pressure on her by going through the court and demanding the money back.

During the court proceedings Jayanti tries to talk to Ketana. But her lawyer advises her against it. She feels that she needs to convince Ketana that she will be loved. When the court gives the verdict, she is scared that Ketana will decide to stay with Jayanti. 
Ketana comes to talk to her after the verdict. She is surprised and happy. Ketana tells her that Jayanti is her mom and she will never leave her. She also tells her that she does want to know Shejali and Rohan as well. She wants to reach a compromise. She offers to move close to them only if Jayanti moves too.
Rohan agrees to it. Shejali tells Ketana that they have a guest house behind their house where she can move. Shejali is excited about the prospect of living so close to her daughter. Although she doesn’t want to see Jayanti, she is willing to pay that price.

*****************

Rohan Sinha: Rohan Sinha is a 47 year old man. He has been a businessman ever since he graduated college. Right after college he started a mail order printed T-shirt business. It didn’t do well for a while. Rohan was tenacious. He kept on printing pamphlets and mailing them. He met people at trade fairs and built a network. 
Rohan met Shejali at a small business trade fair. Shejali was representing the company she worked for. She was meeting businesses and pitching them transitioning their business to websites. She met Rohan at his company’s stall. She talked to him about getting a website. Rohan really liked the idea. But he told her that his business could not afford to spend on a website. 
Shejali and Rohan met at another trade fair after that. His business had started doing well. She talked to him about getting a website again. Rohan was charmed by how smart Shejali was. He wanted to meet her again so he sets up a meeting. They start talking on the phone. Business at first. THey become friends. Start meeting more often. Rohan proposes soon after. Shejali accepts. 
After they got engaged. Rohan started spending more and more time with Shejali. Shejali would talk to him about work. Rohan could listen to her talk all day. He was awestruck by her intelligence. Shejali would often tell him how much are their clients willing to pay for websites. She would tell him how easier it is becoming to create websites. How there are thousands of young kids graduating everyday that could create professional websites in a day. 
Rohan had asked Shejali if she wants to run his business with him. He only hoped Shejali would join. Shejali had told him that she liked her job. His business was doing well. But there wasn’t much that he could do with it alone. He wasn’t satisfied with whatever success he had. He sold the business for a 85 lakh rupees. It had done well over the years. 
He hadn’t told Shejali about this so he was nervous telling her. Shejali was surprised. She asked him what he planned on doing. “I’m starting an IT company. I’ll be making websites” he said. “Really?” she asked. “Yes. With you.” Shejali laughed.
3 months later, Rohan started the business with Shejali as a 50% partner. 3 months later they get married. Shejali wanted to talk about kids. Rohan convinces her to wait till the business start doing well. A couple of years later they had Pratik.
Rohan starts dedicating more time at home as his business grows. Rohan loves being a father. He often suggests having another kid. He wants to have a daughter. He loves that Shejali wants to have a kid too. They are both happy with their life and hopes for the future. 
Their doctor tells them it is dangerous for Shejali to go through another pregnancy. Rohan is heartbroken, but realizes that Shejali will take this news worse. So, he pretends to keep everything together. They visit other doctors. But they all tell him the same thing. 
Their doctor suggests surrogacy or adoption if they really want to have a kid. Rohan is on board with surrogacy. Shejali’s reluctance confuses her. He doesn’t understand why she has to think about this so much. It is the most obvious way. He doesn’t want to put her in any kind of danger. They would be getting a baby, without losing her. He convince Shejali. 
He likes Jayanti. She is well mannered and honest. She is already a mother. He is excited that they will finally have another child. He is absolutely overjoyed when he finds out that it will be a girl. While Shejali is busy with Jayanti and taking care of her needs. He gets busy preparing things for the baby. He paints the room. Buys the crib, clothes and toys. 
He was at the office when Shejali called him. Jayanti was missing. And so was Ketana. He felt dizzy. He somehow ran towars the elevators. Tears running down his eyes. He drove back to the hospital. Police was already there. They told him they would find them.
A few months had gone by. Rohan called Inspector Rajiv almost every week. To get updates. With nothing happening from the police, Rohan decides to hire a private detective. An ex army officer. But he fails as well. Rohan has to accept the fact that he might never see his daughter again.
Rohan remembered Ketana everyday. HE often wondered what she looked like. How she talked. WHat kind of jokes she cracks. How she laughs. He had forgotten about the detective. He didn’t recognize him when he got the call. He thought it was someone trying to scam him for money. But he sent him pictures of Jayanti with a young girl. Rohan recognized Jayanti immediately. He knew the girl had to be his Ketana. He immediately told Shailja. 
They decided to do everything perfectly this time. SO they filed a suite. Rohan was confident that they will win it. THey had a contract after all. Ketana is legally their daughter. For him, Jayanti was a kidnapper at best. 
Things didn’t go their way. But he was still confident that Ketana would choose them if he could talk to them. He wanted to tell her why Jayanti does not deserve her. He liked how Ketana wanted to handle the situation. He doesn’t want to see Jayanti. But he is proud of how smart and kind his little girl is. For Ketana he is ready to forget everything else.

******************

Pratik Sinha: A 20 year old. He is a shy kid. Close to his father, he was always afraid of his mother. Trying to impress her, he would often sing songs for her. She didn’t say anything. Sometimes she would smile. His dad was always encouraging. 
He had been a good student in school. But he only studied because he was supposed to. Report card day was the only time her mother was happy with something he did. Her face would glow when the teachers told her how well Pratik did. She would always get him a present afterwards. She hoped Pratik would become a lawyer or a doctor. 
Pratik didn’t knew little about Ketana. His parents hadn’t told him much. He remembered his parents telling him that he is about to get a new sister when he was little. 
Pratik had been performing in school plays. He wants to become an actor. He has told his parents about it. Shejali has told him he can pursue whatever profession he wants, after he has graduated from college with a professional degree. He wants to attend NSD to study acting. But he doesn’t tell Shejali for the fear of disappointing her. He enrols in a BBA course. 
He is confused when he finds out about Ketana and Jayanti. There are people in the house. People talking about legal stuff. Rohan later comes and tells him about the situation. He is happy that they’ve finally found his sister.
During the trial he reads Jayanti’s story in the newspaper. He sympathizes with her. He doesn’t want his parents to separate a mother and daughter. He is relieved when the verdict comes out. He knows that Ketana will choose Jayanti.

****************************

Will Ketana try and get to know her genetic parents? She had little interest until she saw Shefali break down in tears after the verdict.

*****************

How does Ketana feel about Jayanti lying to her all these years?
Ketana couldn’t believe when Jayanti tells her about her origin. She feels betrayed. Jayanti had told her so many lies over the years. Ketana had made peace with never knowing her father. But Jayanti had kept her from knowing both her parents. How could she steal a child from someone. She didn’t talk to Jayanti for a few days. 
When the trial started. She found out that there was a possibility of her leaving Jayanti forever. She was angry at Jayanti. And she knew that Jayanti wasn’t even her real mom. But she didn’t want to leave her. Whatever the details of the case, in her mind Jayanti was her mom. One night she went into Jayanti’s room. Hugged her. Both Jayanti and Ketana cried for several minutes. Ketana told her she would never leave, irrespective of what the court decides.

***************************

Story should begin with Ketana and Jayanti moing into the small house behind Shejali's and Rohan's main house.

Come up with the personality of each character and describe it briefly. Unique complex characters.

You can decide:
What work the father does.
He runs an IT company. They make web softwares for companies. He looks at business development and finances of the company. He is the CEO. 
*****
Whether or not Shejali works or not
Shejali is an equal partner in Rohan’s company. She handles the operations and marketing of the company. 
*****
Whether or not they get along well
ROhan and SHejali love each other.
*****
What Pratik is studying in school
Pratik is doing a BBA. Although he wants to study acting at NSD.
*****
How each of them feel about Ketana
Rohan - loves
Shejali - Loves but scared that she might not bond with her.
Pratik - Doesn’t know what to expect.
*****
How each of them feel about Jayanti
Rohan - hates
Shejali - hates but understands
Pratik - Sympathetic
*****
How the family members feel about each other
Pratik and Rohan have a loving friendly relationship. Rohan has taken care of most of Pratik’s needs. Pratik also goes to Rohan whenever he needs anything. 
Pratik loves Shejali, but he is also scared of her. She had withdrawn herself after Ketana got abducted. But he wants to get her attention. He got into a fight as a kid. Shejali didn’t have much to say except punish him. She let Rohan handle the situation with the school. The only time he got attention from his mom, was when he did well in school. So, he starts studying hard. He has other interests like sports, theatre and music. Shejali wants him to focus on his studies first and other things later. So, he often has to make sacrifices.

DOCUMENT: The_Surrogacy_Duology/The_Surrogacy_Tiers_Worldbuilding_Bible.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine
> **Core Spark:** A seemingly progressive society that eradicated maternal career sacrifice by turning childbearing into tiered venture capitalism
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [x] Harvested worldbuilding mechanics to Dialogue_and_Voice_Scraps.md
> - [ ] Connect to [[The_Outhouse_Surrogate_Bible]] as the macro-societal counterpart to the domestic drama
> - [ ] Establish the inciting incident: a child defying the detachment protocol to track down their biological carrier
> - [ ] Flesh out the synthetic vs organic breast milk corporate lobbying conflict

The world is progressing. There is world peace. Governments of countries work together. 

There are more women in government than ever before. Most world leaders are women. 

Families are compact. Most people have only one child. Quite a few families decide not to have children. Adoption is one of the primary ways people decide to have children. Surrogacy is a legitimate business now. There are companies who hire women to give birth. These women are paid well. For one surrogate, the process takes 9 months to two years. It is a contract with the company. The women are paid well and highly respected. Almost revered. After the pregnancy the surrogate is supposed to keep the baby for another one, one and a half year. Where she keeps the baby is based on the contract she has with the company and the parents. 
Some parents take and keep the baby right after she is born. Most parents take the services of the surrogate to take care of the baby for the initial few months. This has been recommended by regulatory agencies and health organizations. It provides babies with the natural milk from her mother’s breast. And the psychological acclimatisation of the parents to the new responsibility on their hands. The government subsidizes these costs by paying half the expenses of hiring a surrogate if you have the appropriate health insurance. People paying a higher premium would get access to the best surrogacy clinics and best professional surrogates. 
People who do not have money usually take the baby right after she is born. But subscribing to a professional babysitting agency is much cheaper than hiring the surrogate for the full contract. They also have to go to a B or C level surrogate agency. 
Breast milk is available in supermarkets and vending machines. There are two types of breast milks in the market. The most widely sold breast milk is processed. Brands have created factories to convert animal milk to woman’s breast milk. Factories take animal milk — cow, buffalo or goat, process it — adding of removing chemicals and hormones, boxes it and sells it in the market. 
Another niche in the breast milk market is the authentic breast milk brands. This the milk gifted (Pumped) by real women for your baby. Professional surrogates in the B and C level, sell their milk to organic breast milk companies to make extra money. It is a win-win situation for them. Parents at that level usually take their baby right after they are born. They are not earning anything after that. They usually don’t have other jobs. So selling their milk is usually their source of income for the next few months.
Kids generally grow up knowing their surrogate. They don’t have a relationship with them. But they know who they are. Government keeps the record of the surrogate’s medical and family history anyways. Some kids have been known to get attached to their surrogates. But parents usually send kids to therapy which helps in the child detaching themselves from their surrogates. 
There was a case in a school where a group of friends ran off to find their surrogates. The surrogates were true professional and alerted the authorities at once. The children were taken into custody and sent back to their parents. Upon returning one of them committed suicide. The others were sent to therapy immediately. This was a landmark incident. Since then it is a recommended practice to send the child to therapy if a similar situation occurred. 

Agencies also provide sperm. But this is a cheap expense as companies can mixing and formulating a new sperm. For example they can mix an athlete’s sperm with a scientist sperm to make a new genetic combination that has both the athletic gene and the smart gene. 

A, B and C level surrogate agency.

The A level surrogate agencies are considered the best in service and product. Their hiring process for professional surrogates is stringent. They interview candidates, do a thorough medical and psychological examination. The professional surrogates in an A level are intelligent, good social skills and have good physical attributes. 
A level agency also has the rights to the best sperm in the market. They have a rigorous process for sperm depositors as well. They test and examine their genes and classify them with different tags. Sperms qualified under different tags. Like athletic, intelligent, focus. With each type of sperm there is one primary tag, 4-10 secondary tags, and can have any number of tertiary tags. The tags classify what kind of genes are dominant. Parents can look at the tags and decide which sperms to mix. They also have access to records of the depositor to assess their accomplishments. 
These agencies buy the rights to the sperm they get. The depositor is not allowed to deposit their semen anywhere else. They do get royalties after each use of their sperm. Some of the depositors make a good salary by marketing them. The main competition in the Sperm market among the A level agencies is the premium sperm. Agencies buy celebrity sperm and their rights. They can then add their DNA to the final mix.
Professional surrogates at an A level agency can charge from 10,000$ to 50,000$ a month out of which 20% goes to the agency
Sperm depositors get 5000$-10,000$

A B level surrogate agency is a good choice for a middle class family. They have decent choice among professional surrogates. Healthy women, with good genes. They cater to a price conscious customers. They have attractive deals for the prospective parents. 15 month packages for the surrogate. They provide competitive service for middle class prospective parents on a budget. There is a good choice available for genetic selection. The gene pool available is varied. This is the largest category in surrogate reproduction market. 
This level has the highest number of professional surrogates and sperm depositors. This has led to a highly competitive market for agencies as well as professionals. 

A C level surrogate agency is a good option to become parents if you don’t have money. Although, there are very few profitable C level agencies. Most people at that financial level prefer having children the old fashioned way. Agencies at this level usually target a specific income group, but their main consumers are lower middle class families that can’t have children. 
Not much choice available in professional surrogates. The number of professional surrogates far exceeds the number of prospective parents at this level. Most C level agencies barely survive past the 2 year mark. 


Surrogacy as a profession was frowned upon a couple of decades back. In fact, till about 2025 it was done in secret. There was no regulation. Only rich people had access to it. Almost all women in the world gave birth at least once. Can your imagine? If you wanted to have a baby, your wife would have to go through the 9 months of pregnancy and then labor. The, exhaustion, the pain. Women had to juggle between work and home. It was almost always the women who had to make sacrifices in personal life and career, if they wanted to start a family. They were oppressed in many parts of the world, if they were infertile. Sounds incredible. Like a fantasy. But as a society we learn from our mistakes. 
In 2026, Sid Mayer, the first female president of USA, had her first surrogate child. At the age of 57. She was also the first openly homosexual president in the world. She helped changed perception of society and lawmakers. Initially most surrogacy was done through unreliable channels. You had to deal with shady agents, vague laws and bad service. She and her successor Arnold Lacey made changes to the law, setting up a secure infrastructure to adopt children via surrogacy. 
A government registry was setup where women can register as professional surrogates. And future parents can find the right surrogate for their child.

================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (verse | 2018_Crucible)
> **Core Spark:** Contrasting deep-sea bioluminescence with an intimate promise of steadfast affection under moonlight
>
> **Structural & Prosodic Critique:**
> - **Why It Fails Right Now:** Stanza 3 collapses the nocturnal atmosphere into nursery-talk and forced rhyme: *I'll warm you with hugs. I'll please you with nonsense. And I'll give you lots of rubs.*
> - **The Core Idea Worth Saving:** Stanzas 1 and 2 have zero forced end-rhymes and genuine cinematic weight: *By the sea, beneath the yellow and sagging moon, there lies a world completely dark. The creatures float like angels and demons.* Pivoting from bioluminescence in Stanza 1 to moonlight catching the mole on Pooja's nose in Stanza 2 is intimate and visually sharp.
> - **How to Fix It:** Amputate the *hugs / rubs* lines in Stanza 3 immediately. Rewrite the closing stanza in the same quiet, dark, observational free-verse register as the first two stanzas.
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [ ] Cut *warm you with hugs / please you with nonsense / give you lots of rubs* from Stanza 3
> - [ ] Draft a new unrhymed closing stanza that mirrors the nocturnal ocean imagery of Stanza 1
> - [ ] Connect to [[In_The_Meantime_Poem]] and [[The_Love_Letter_To_Pooja_December_2018]]

Date: 05-02-2019
Topic: "By the sea, beneath the yellow and sagging moon." (after Walt Whitman)

By the sea, beneath the yellow and sagging moon, there lies a world completely dark. The creatures float like angels and demons. Spewing and spouting colors. Chasing after each other in the dark. Like its a full clear day. 
By the sea, beneath the yellow and sagging moon, I see you staring at me. Your face brighter than the moon. Even in this dark I can see it clearly. I can see the mole on your nose. Your lips shine in the moonlight. Inviting. Intoxicating.
By the sea, beneath the yellow and sagging moon, I make this promise to myself. I will love you the same each day if not more. I will shower you with kisses. I’ll warm you with hugs. I’ll please you with nonsense. And I’ll give you lots of rubs.
