DOCUMENT: Verse_and_Lyrics/In_The_Meantime_Poem.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (song_lyrics | 2018_Crucible)
> **Core Spark:** Accepting that figuring out life may take forever, while choosing to love and be grounded in the interim
>
> **Structural & Prosodic Critique:**
> - **Why It Fails Right Now:** Read silently as page poetry, placeholder end-rhymes (*universe / verse*, *stars / mars*, *understand / stand*) feel juvenile and telegraphed.
> - **The Core Idea Worth Saving:** Structurally, this is a **3-verse acoustic folk/indie song** with a built-in chorus turnaround (*In the meantime, let me just love a bit... Maybe I'll lose my way, but you'll bring me home / In the meantime*). The emotional thesis—parking cosmic/career ambition to stay present with your partner while unresolved—is earned and honest.
> - **How to Fix It:** Treat this explicitly as a **lyric sheet for acoustic guitar**. Replace the cosmic filler rhymes with grounded domestic details, and lean into the musicality of the *In the meantime* turnaround.
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [ ] Test chord progressions on acoustic guitar around the *In the meantime* turnaround
> - [ ] Rewrite the opening couplets of Verses 2 and 3 to eliminate the *stars/mars* and *understand/stand* rhymes
> - [ ] Connect to [[It_Was_Noon_and_Nothing_Is_Concluded]] and [[The_Love_Letter_To_Pooja_December_2018]]

Date: 30-11-2018
Topic: In the meantime…

I want to touch the sky. I want to fly so high that I can walk on the clouds. 
I want to run. I want to leave the world behind. If it was up to me I’d go so far I can’t see around.
But I’m trying to figure shit out. 
Don’t know if I’d ever figure it out.
Don’t see a way, I’ll give it my all anyway… 
In the meantime, Let me just love a bit
Let me have fun with it.
Maybe I’ll wander off, 
But you’ll bring me back,
In the meantime…

I want to see the universe. I want this verse to reach the stars.
I want to live on the moon. I want to live like a king. I will make a getaway house on mars.
Let me see what I can do.
Maybe it’s to ambitious too.
Maybe I’ll never get there, I’ll give it my all anyway.
In the meantime, Let me just love a bit.
I’ll be yours if you permit,
Maybe I’ll lose my way,
But you’ll bring me home,
In the meantime…

Maybe my ambitions are too lofty to understand. Maybe I’m a fool to even take that stand.
But I know I have you to back me up all the way.
And even if I lose my shit you’ll never let me stray.
In the meantime, let me just love a bit,
I’ll belong to you, for time infinite,
In the meantime.

DOCUMENT: Verse_and_Lyrics/It_Was_Noon_and_Nothing_Is_Concluded.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (verse | 2018_Crucible)
> **Core Spark:** The contrast between knowing that action is the only cure and remaining physically pinned to the chair as noon turns to night
>
> **Structural & Prosodic Critique:**
> - **Why It Fails Right Now:** Rhyming *concluded / deluded*, *band / hand*, and *outta luck / make a buck* turns acute psychological paralysis into a nursery rhyme. The bouncy AABB meter fights the heavy subject matter.
> - **The Core Idea Worth Saving:** The opening refrain (*It was noon and nothing is concluded*) and the central conflict—*My heart paralyzed but my brain is working overtime* while daylight bleeds into evening darkness—is a sharp capture of executive dysfunction.
> - **How to Fix It:** Strip all end-rhymes completely. Rewrite as rhythmic, unrhymed free verse or a tight prose-poem (matching the cadence of [[Kitni_Baatein_Shikayaten_Monologue]]), keeping *It was noon and nothing is concluded* as the structural anchor line.
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [ ] Amputate forced end-rhymes (deluded, band, vice, buck, suited) while preserving the raw thoughts
> - [ ] Use *It was noon and nothing is concluded* as an unrhymed structural refrain across 3 tight stanzas
> - [ ] Connect to [[Courage_To_Be_Disliked_Full_Reflections]] and [[Standing_In_The_Doorway_Freewrite]]

Date: 26-11-2018
Topic: It was noon and nothing is concluded

It was noon and nothing is concluded
There is too much to do, I’m tired, restless but not deluded.

I possibly can’t do this on my own, I’m waiting for a hand
It’s not coming I know, I’m a one man band

It will soon be bright. Too bright to know wrong from right.
I still can’t make a choice, I can’t move ahead, I still can’t fight.

My heart paralyzed but my brain is working overtime.
I’m stuck, Gotta move, I tell myself, but my courage is dyin’

Ive read all the lines, heard all the philosophers, they say sitting on your ass is a vice
I know action is the key, I know it, you don’t have to tell me twice.

It was noon and nothing is concluded.
There is nothing done yet, I’m sad and getting deluded.

Soon the darkness will be here. 
I won’t know ‘coz I’ve already closed my eyes.
I don’t want to sit silently into the night,
I want to get up and fight.
I can’t bear the stares I get,
It pierces my heart like a cigarette.
I shouldn’t give a fuck, I’m outta luck, I’ve gotta make a buck,
But that’s not me. I’ve don’t care about money. I shouldn’t start.

It is noon now and nothing is concluded.
I need to take something up, make my mind up and just get suited.

DOCUMENT: Verse_and_Lyrics/Kitni_Baatein_Shikayaten_Monologue.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine
> **Core Spark:** The sorrow of trying to make someone smile as a personal job description, only for minor oversights to turn into disproportionate emotional trials
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [x] Harvested heart/blood metaphor to Dialogue_and_Voice_Scraps.md
> - [ ] Link to [[The_Cheetal_Dhaba_Extortion]] and [[The_Safest_Trail_Why_I_Write]]
> - [ ] Preserve the Hindi free-verse rhythm without sanitizing or over-structuring

Kitni baatein kehni hai mujhko tumse. 
Saari shikayaten hi hain. Shayad tumhe kadvi lagen.

Mujhe napasnd hai jaise tum daant deti ho mujhko. 
Mujhe ye shikayat nahi ki tum mujhe daantti ho, ya chillati ho mujhpe.
Agar meri khata hai to mujhe manzoor hai tumhari gaaliyan.
Jitna sharminda karna chaho, kar lo. Mai khamosh rahunga.
Bura to bahut lagega. Mai kuch nahi kahunga.
Lekin ab tum aisi baaton pe sharminda karti ho,
Jinhe nazarandaz kiya ja sakta hai.
Bhool ya nadani samajhke.
Par jab ye nadaaniyan tum karti ho…
To bhi mujh par hi bhadak padti ho. 
Jaise isme bhi meri hi galti ho. 
 
Tum kehti ho ye cheezein zaruri hain tumhare liye.
Zaruri hai tumhare liye, isiliye mai kar raha hun.
Agar koi galti hui bhi hai mujhse,
To wo galti to kisi se bhi ho sakti hain.
Tum se bhi. 
Tumse huin hain kayi baar.
Magar mai nazarandaz karta hun.
Farak nahi padta mujhe ki tum kya galtiyan karti ho.
Mai hun use sudharne ke liye.
Kyunki yahi ‘job description’ hai mera.
Roz mai ye koshish karta hun ki tumhe khush kar sakun, hasa sakun.
Tum muskurao meri baaton pe. 
Hairan ho sun kar kahaniya jo mai sunaun tumko.
Mai chahta hun ki jab tum ghar aao to choomo mujhe, gale lagao.
Mai jo karta hun inhi iraado ke saath karta hun.
Mera aur koi maqsad nahi. 
Isliye chubhta hai mujhko jab tum kuch aisa kehti ho.
Jisse jazbaato ko thes lagti hai.
Ho sakta hai ye bhi meri hi galti hai. 
Mere jazbaat kamzor hain itne.
Zara si thokar pe thes lag jaati hai.
Lekin mujhe farak nahi padta jab koi aur mujhe kuch kehta hai.
Tumhari baat na jaane kyun dard deti hain.

Jaise dil nikal ke rakha jis ke saamne,
Usi ne sara khoon nichod liya.
Aisa lagta hai jaise meri neeyat mayne hi nahi rakhti.

Mujhe lagta tha ki tum samajhti ho mujhko.
Jaanti ho ki mai kaun hun.
Tumhe pasand hai ut-patang harkatein meri.
Tumhe pasand hai meri ye ajeebo-gareeb baatein.
Mujhe lagta tha ki tumhe pasand hun mai,
Shayad khud ko hi jhooth bol raha tha.
Mujhe ye bhi lagta hai kabhi ki meri wahi harkatein, 
waise baatein, jo tum pasand kiya karti thi,
Ab tumhare liye purani ho gayin hain.
Aisa hai to koi baat nahi.
Mai samajhta hun. Aisa hota hai.
Lekin aisa hai to tum yahi bata do mujhko.
Ye mujhe kam bura lagega.
Kam se kam mujhe pata to chal jayega ki wajah kya hai tumhari berukhi ki. 




Agar tum kehti ho ki tumhe mujhse pyar hai. 
To kya itna bhi pyar nahi ki meri jazbaaton ko samajh sako.
Agar mai tumhe batata hun ki mujhe kaisa lagta hai,
To ye kyun ek aur wajah ban jaati hai meri hi kisi nasamjhi ki.
Kya mai akela hi rahun apni in khayalon ke saath.
Shayad yahi thik hoga.  


Galti ye hai ki bhul jaata hun kuch cheezein,
Par ye mai kar bhi to tumhare liye hi raha tha.
Kya ye baat mayene nahi rakhti.

Kya ye baat mayne rakhti hai tumhare liye? 
Mera kyun, kya aur kaise… isse koi farak nahi padta?
Meri neeyat kya thi? 
Kyun itni si galtiyan itni chubh jaati hain tumhe…
ki tumhe baaki kuch yaad nahi rehta.
Kya ye itni badi galti hai,
Ki mere in jazbaat ka koi matlab nahi?

---

DOCUMENT: Verse_and_Lyrics/The_Apple_Pie_and_the_Plumber_Tone.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (verse / spoken word | 2018_Crucible)
> **Core Spark:** Subverting performative egalitarianism by exposing the sudden tonal shift between discussing social justice over dessert and asserting authority over blue-collar service workers.
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [ ] Strip forced couplets and high-school debate generalities (nuclear war, mud-eating, global peace)
> - [ ] Test Concept 1 vs Concept 2 to see which rhythm best carries the satire
> - [ ] Connect to [[Voice_and_Dialogue_Scraps]] under Institutional & Corporate Satire

---

## Pivot Concepts for Revision

### Concept 1: The Self-Implicating Bourgeois Satire (Essay Spine)
* **Premise:** Ditch the outward-pointing finger ("people are hypocrites") and aim the barrel inward. The comedy and bite work because the narrator indicts himself.
* **Anchor Scene:** Sitting comfortably in an air-conditioned room debating ethical consumption, late-stage capitalism, or human dignity over artisanal apple pie, until an urban domestic crisis strikes (the water tank overflows, the plumber arrives, the delivery guy loses the map).
* **The Turn:** Watch how quickly the enlightened egalitarian tone vanishes into crisp managerial authority: *"I consider myself a deeply egalitarian human being until the apartment water tank overflows and I have to address the man holding the wrench."*

### Concept 2: The Cynical Spoken-Word Confessional (Unrhymed Monologue)
* **Premise:** Strip all end-rhymes and pleasant poetic posturing. Match the urgent, biting cadence of *[[Kitni_Baatein_Shikayaten_Monologue]]*.
* **The Subversion:** "If I had my way" is not a beauty pageant wish for universal peace; it is a confession of selfish convenience.
* **Tone & Rhythm Anchor:**
  > *"If I had my way, nobody would starve. Obviously.
  > But really, what I want is for the grocery delivery kid
  > to drop the bag and vanish without making eye contact,
  > so I don't have to carry the rain on his jacket into my afternoon."*

---

## Archival Freewrite (March 15, 2019)

No one would go hungry. Everyone will have a place to live. 
While we are gluttoning away, discussing ‘what is the best way to eat an apple pie.
Men, women and children die, going days without food in the sun and the rain.
There are people who eat mud, and garbage and whatever they find.

Foraging? I thought we progressed. We were ALL chosen ones.
Yet, I see in the magazines, photos, everywhere online.
People living on corpses of delicious meals in tins and cans. 
Was that just a few of us, Rest of us left behind?

If I had my way, food will be a right and not a privilege.

Everyone will be equal. Sex, gender, region or tribe.
We say we want equality, and tolerance and peace. And maybe we do. 
But when we talk to a driver, or a plumber or a cleaner,
Where exactly does that equality go?

We believe women are equal, yet they are supposed to do this, be that.
Rivaled, loathed and othered all the time. Is that equality? 
We are not software or machines.
One to be washed, cleaned and powdered and the others to be ignored.

It’s time we realize our people are hypocrites.
And understand the meaning of our words.

If I had my way, we would all be seen as people and not machines.

There would be no violence. No killing or murder of any kind.
We regret the things ‘we did that one time’, time that you want to rewind.
We kill friends, we kills foes, we kill strangers on the border.
We kill babies, we kill grannies and people who kill. We chop them like animals or fodder.

We were civilized, we should have stopped this madness. 
Yet killing machines are better than ever before. 
And did you know our nuclear capability could destroy the whole world.
Several times over. Aren’t there enough things out there to kill us already.

I don’t know if we can stop this all, I don’t know what else to say. I don’t know what I would do, If I really had my way.

---

================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Verse_and_Lyrics/The_Shooting_Star_Extinction_Monologue.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (verse | 2018_Crucible)
> **Core Spark:** Wishing on a star that died millions of years ago: does something exist if no one notices it? Most of us barely exist; wishing to go down in flames so brightly that whoever watches makes a wish
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [ ] Amputate the first 4 repetitive lines ('And I wish I had shit figured out...')
> - [ ] Anchor the poem on lines 5–14: the mechanics of dead starlight vs our hunger to be seen
> - [ ] Mirror the cadence and tone of [[Kitni_Baatein_Shikayaten_Monologue]]

Date: 03-03-2019
Topic: You see a shooting star…

And I wish I had shit figured out. I wish I knew what I was doing. I wish I can find a way.
I see a shooting star and I wish for money, I wish for health and my family to be okay.
There is so much to wish for. I think I know what really matters. But I feel some needs need to be met.
Like money and career and a little bit of fame on social media. It’s not too much to ask for I feel. 
But it’s just a shooting star. It probably doesn’t even exist anymore. A shadow of what has been.
Does it not exist if it has died years ago? or does it exist because we see it now?
Does something really exist if nobody notices it, knows about it, cares for it? 
That means, most of us barely exist and some of are existing too much. 
And all of us want to exist a little more. 
We want people to notice us. A few aren’t enough. Our friends aren’t enough and neither is our family.
We want more. People in our offices, people we meet on the street. 
People we barely know on social media and people we never meet.
Maybe we are all shooting stars. 
Burning out. Wishing upon ourselves to go down in flames.
But once. Just once. Burn so bright; that anyone who sees us, wishes for the same.

---

================================================================================
DOCUMENT: Verse_and_Lyrics/You_Have_Stayed_Too_Long_Monologue.md
================================================================================

> [!abstract] Editorial Diagnosis & Triage
> **Verdict:** Story Spine (verse | 2018_Crucible)
> **Core Spark:** Generations wiped from living memory once you die, culminating in the refusal to let children inherit unlived dreams: if they are smart, they'll live their own and show ancestors how it's done
>
> **Actionable Next Steps:**
> - [ ] Amputate generic motivational lines ('Stand up. Take action. Move forward.')
> - [ ] Anchor on the ancestral countdown: grandfather fading, father fading, self fading
> - [ ] Preserve the closing subversion: children living their own lives instead of being avatars for parents

Date: 12-03-2019
Topic: “You have stayed too long”

You’re not welcome here anymore. 
What made you think you could stay here forever.
World is out there. 
You haven’t seen amazing things. 
You haven’t done anything worth remembering.
Will you exist after you’re gone.
For a few years maybe. 
In the memory of a loved one.
Then what?
You’ll be forgotten. Like your ancestors.
Your grandfather will be wiped from memory once you’re gone. 
Your father soon after.
Who will remember you.
You have stayed too long like this.
Stand up. Take action. Move forward.
Fall. Then get up. Move forward. Fall again.
Maybe your failures will guide others.
You will be remembered by your failures.
The one who has shown the way.
Put yourself out there.
That is the only way.
No one will see your value from where you are.
Show your worth.
Live your dreams now.
You will not see tomorrow. Not today.
Today you have this moment to deal with.
What will you do?
Stay here, a little longer?
Or take a step. Just one.
They are your dreams to live. 
Live them and be remembered.
Or, hope your children would have courage.
To live your dreams for you.
But if they are smart.
They’ll live their own.
And show their ancestors how it’s done.

---
